# AUDIO-003 Implementation Plan

> **For agentic workers:** REQUIRED SUB-SKILL: Use superpowers:subagent-driven-development (recommended) or superpowers:executing-plans to implement this plan task-by-task. Steps use checkbox (`- [ ]`) syntax for tracking.

**Goal:** Audio files can be imported into the browser "My audio" library exactly like images (content-addressed, deduplicated, locked, verified, recoverable) with container metadata and waveform peaks computed once at import; `local:audio:` refs in a Story resolve, verify and get object URLs like images; the CLI finds the same files in `local-assets/audio/`; a minimal My audio list ships in the asset catalog.

**Architecture:** `inspectAudio` reads container metadata only (mediabunny, Node and browser, no decode); the browser import additionally decodes once (`decodeAudioPeaks`) for peaks and the truncation rule (`isDecodeComplete`). Storage reuses the v0.3 split (`assets` row per ref, per-digest `payloadMeta` + `blobs`) with a `kind: "audio"` payload metadata variant and a new `waveforms` store (IndexedDB version 2, no data migration). Everything image-shaped is widened only where audio needs it; image behaviour is unchanged (AINV-6).

**Tech Stack:** TypeScript (Node `--experimental-strip-types`), mediabunny 1.56.1 (already a dependency after AUDIO-002), React 19, IndexedDB + Web Locks, `node:test`, Playwright (Chromium + system Chrome for AAC), repository ffmpeg via `tests/helpers/ffmpeg.mjs::resolveFfmpegBinaries`.

**Spec:** `docs/audio/AUDIO-003-audio-library-and-import.md`, `docs/audio/README.md` (AINV-n, constants, D-n, S-n) and the v0.3 specs `docs/assets/ASSET-002-browser-local-asset-storage.md`, `ASSET-003-import-and-library-ui.md`, `ASSET-005-cli-local-assets.md` (executors read the spec and README first; the v0.3 specs describe mechanisms this plan reuses and only the code on `main` is authoritative).

**Prerequisite (already merged before this branch starts):** AUDIO-001 (on `main`) and AUDIO-002. From AUDIO-002 this plan uses: `@remotion/media@4.0.529` and `mediabunny@1.56.1` already installed (`package.json` dependencies), `src/audio/sources.ts` (`AudioSource`, `AudioSourceMap`, `BundledAudioPolicy`, `resolveAudioSrc`, `buildAudioSourceMap(story, localSources, bundled)`), `src/audio/plan.ts` (`AudioRole = "music" | "sfx" | "voice"`, `compileAudioPlan`). This plan never installs a package.

## Global Constraints

- TypeScript imports use explicit `.ts`/`.tsx` extensions; Node tests are `tests/*.test.mjs` with `node:test` + `node:assert/strict`, importing `.ts` directly (`node --experimental-strip-types --test <file>`). Type-only imports use `import type`.
- Write the failing test first. After every task: `npm test`, `npm run lint` (eslint + `tsc`), `npm run web:build`, `npx playwright test --project=chromium` (the Playwright web server serves the BUILT bundle: run `npm run web:build` before browser tests). Tasks that touch `tests/harness/**` also run `npm run test:harness`; tasks that touch `scripts/localAssets.ts`/`scripts/assets.ts`/`scripts/renderStory.ts` also run `npm run test:cli-e2e`. The final task runs everything in the spec's Verify block plus `npx playwright test --config=playwright.browser-render.config.mjs`.
- AINV-6 / v0.3 expectations: a Story without audio behaves byte-identically to `main`; an image's metadata record, `VerifiedPayload`, usage objects, `npm run assets` output and error texts stay exactly as they are. The only existing assertions this plan edits are the ones that name something it deliberately changes (listed in the task that changes them): the `ASSET_DB_VERSION` constant and store list, the harness upgrade tests, the `checkLocalAssetByteSize` call arguments and the `reads`/`STORE_NAMES` helpers are extended without changing image expectations.
- The interim MP4 render block from AUDIO-001 (`AUDIO_RENDER_BLOCK_MESSAGE`, the `renderStoryMediaOnWeb` guard with `bypassAudioBlockForTest`, the `renderStory` refusal, the disabled browser button) stays untouched. AUDIO-005 removes it. Because of it, "staged by the CLI" is tested through `stageLocalAssetsForStory` (the function `renderStory` will call), and `renderStory` is only tested to refuse before any read.
- Constants already on `main` (do not redefine): `MAX_LOCAL_AUDIO_BYTES = 50 * 1024 * 1024`, `MAX_AUDIO_DURATION_SECONDS = 300`, `MIN_AUDIO_SAMPLE_RATE = 8_000`, `MAX_AUDIO_SAMPLE_RATE = 96_000`, `MAX_AUDIO_CHANNELS = 2`, `AUDIO_TRUNCATION_TOLERANCE_SECONDS = 0.25` (`src/localAssets/limits.ts`); `WAVEFORM_PEAKS_PER_SECOND = 50`, `MAX_WAVEFORM_PEAKS = 15_000` (`src/audio/constants.ts`). New in this plan: `ASSET_DB_VERSION = 2` (`src/web/assetLibrary/constants.ts`).
- Messages, verbatim: `Video files are not supported. Use an audio file.`; `Ogg Vorbis audio is not supported. Use MP3, WAV, M4A or Ogg Opus.`; `Audio must be mono or stereo.`; `This audio file could not be decoded.`; `This audio file looks truncated.`; `already in My audio`.
- Accepted (container, codec): (`mp3`,`mp3`), (`wave`,`pcm-*`), (`mp4`,`aac`), (`ogg`,`opus`). Vorbis is rejected (the repository ffmpeg has no Vorbis encoder, see Task 1): the ruling is recorded in the spec in the last task.
- Payload MIME types from the inspected container: `audio/mpeg`, `audio/wav`, `audio/mp4`, `audio/ogg`. Labels default to the filename without extension; deduplication is by digest within the `audio` category. Identity is the key only (INV-11): no `digest`/`ref`/`category` field is ever read from a stored record to look anything up.
- Dependencies: nothing is added in this plan. Never run anything against production or `*.netlify.app`; never kill processes by image name; commits end with `Co-Authored-By: Claude Opus 5.5 <noreply@anthropic.com>`. The repository ffmpeg is a minimal build (see Task 1): do not assume filters or encoders beyond what Task 1 lists.
- Windows dev machine (PowerShell 5.1 for the user; Git Bash for agents); CI on ubuntu-latest, Node 22.
- On the Windows checkout the existing source files have CRLF line endings; edit them with the Edit tool (which keeps them) and do not reformat or convert whole files. New files may use LF. Several snippets below are written as "replace this text with that text": the old text is the file's current text on `main` (quoted from it).

## Review Focus

1. **A valid 25-50 MiB audio file rejected by an image limit (or a 26 MiB image accepted by the audio limit)** on any path: browser import, `payloadMeta` decoder, integrity reader, CLI scanner. A reasonable person expects a 30 MiB WAV to import and a 26 MiB image to still be refused. Pinned in Tasks 3 (check function), 5 (decoder and integrity reader), 9 (import) and 11 (CLI).
2. **A truncated or undecodable file saved silently**: a truncated MP3 whose Xing header still declares 10 s, an M4A in a browser without AAC decode, a clip that decodes to zero samples. Expected: `This audio file looks truncated.` / `This audio file could not be decoded.` and nothing stored. Pinned in Tasks 4, 9 and 10 (browser spec, plus the system-Chrome M4A spec).
3. **Existing My assets data after the IndexedDB bump and a shared digest delete**: v0.3 rows and payloads intact after the 1 to 2 upgrade, a blocked upgrade that still ends `ready`, deleting an audio row never touching image stores (and an image delete never touching waveforms), no "other category" lookup for audio. Pinned in Task 6.
4. **Audio refs flowing through image-only pipelines**: the image budget counting audio refs or bytes, the Player status line saying an audio file shows "a placeholder", `evaluateLocalAssetBudget` reading `width`/`height` of audio metadata, an image row pointing at audio metadata (or the reverse) treated as ready. Expected: image budget unchanged, audio reported as audio, category/kind mismatch is `corrupt`. Pinned in Tasks 5 (the budget fails closed) and 7 (collector, resolution, status line).
5. **Bad durations reaching the page**: `NaN`, `Infinity`, `0` or a negative duration in a stored or injected record would make `compileTimeline` throw inside the Player. Expected: those values never enter the Story's duration map, and the Player length uses one map. Pinned in Tasks 2 (catalog map), 6 (`readAudioDurations`) and 8 (single map).

## Rulings assumed by this plan (confirmed by the controller, 2026-10-13; each is repeated where it matters)

- Execution order: AUDIO-002 is merged first; this plan builds on its `src/audio/sources.ts`, `src/audio/plan.ts` (`AudioRole`) and dependencies. Before Task 1, check that `Preview`/App still match what Task 8 expects and rule on any drift.

- Fixture Task order: fixtures (Task 1) come before `inspectAudio` (Task 2) because its tests need them; no Ogg Vorbis fixture exists (no encoder), so Vorbis is rejected.
- Image `payloadMeta` records keep NO `kind` field (the v0.3 shape); a stored `kind: "image"` is tolerated and normalised away; only audio carries `kind: "audio"`. `payloadKindOf(meta)` is the discriminator.
- Duration authority is `input.getDurationFromMetadata() ?? input.computeDuration()` (mediabunny), because a truncated MP3 with a Xing header must still report its declared length for the truncation rule to work (measured: `computeDuration()` returns 5.98 s for the truncated fixture, the metadata duration 10.031 s).
- Peaks are laid over the container duration by timestamp (`peaksPerSecond = n / durationSeconds` then places every peak in time); the spec's "over the decoded length" differs by less than the truncation tolerance.
- A different audio file chosen in "Import matching file" is refused with the mismatch text and nothing is written (the replacement dialog needs scene-field pickers that AUDIO-004 owns); the image flow keeps its dialog.
- `npm run assets` prints the audio section only when `<root>/audio/` exists, so every v0.3 output line stays identical.
- Library read functions take the store first: `readAudioMetadata(store, ref)`, `readWaveform(store, digest)`, `readAudioDurations(store, refs)`.

### Task 1: Audio fixtures

What the repository ffmpeg really has (measured on `@remotion/compositor-win32-x64-msvc`, ffmpeg n7.1; the linux packages use the same `--disable-filters/--disable-encoders` configuration):
- Audio encoders: `aac`, `libfdk_aac`, `libmp3lame`, `opus` (experimental), `libopus`, `pcm_s16le`, `pcm_s24le`. **No Vorbis encoder** (`-c:a vorbis` fails with "Unknown encoder"); Vorbis can only be decoded.
- Muxers: `wav`, `mp3`, `mp4` (no `m4a`/`ipod` name: use `-f mp4`), `ogg`, `opus`, `adts`, `mov`, `matroska`, `webm`, `mpegts`, `avi`. Video: `libx264` only through the filtergraph (a `-f lavfi -i` video input needs the `wrapped_avframe` decoder, which this build lacks).
- Filters: `sine`, `anullsrc`, `aformat`, `aresample`, `amix`, `apad`, `adelay`, `atrim`, `pan`, `volume`, `loudnorm`, `silencedetect`, `nullsrc`, `scale`, `format`, `concat`... **No `anoisesrc`, `highpass`, `lowpass`, `afade`, `aevalsrc`**. The seeded noise therefore reuses the technique of `scripts/generateBundledAudio.mjs`: a sum of seeded sine partials (here 64, linear-uniform in [100, 2000] Hz, which is low-passed white noise).
- Measured: two runs of the generator below write byte-identical files on one machine. Across platforms only the PCM fixtures are expected to match within 1 LSB (same as `tests/bundled-audio.test.mjs`); lossy encoders may differ, so the committed bytes are authoritative (Stories reference fixtures by digest) and no test compares a lossy file with a regenerated one from another machine.
- Probed with mediabunny 1.56.1 (Node): MP3 durations come from the Xing header (`tone-220-10s.mp3` 10.031 s, `noise-2s.mp3` 2.0376 s, `tone-440-2.2s.mp3` 2.2465 s, `tone-880-0.4s.mp3` 0.4441 s), WAV and M4A are exact, Opus is `tone-220-10s.opus.ogg` 10.0135 s; `truncated.mp3`: `getDurationFromMetadata()` 10.031 s but `computeDuration()` 5.98 s.

**Files:**
- Modify: `scripts/generateBundledAudio.mjs` (export four helpers; no behaviour change)
- Create: `scripts/generateAudioFixtures.mjs`, `tests/helpers/audioBytes.mjs`, `tests/audio-fixtures.test.mjs`, `tests/fixtures/local-audio/README.md`, and the 21 committed files listed by `fixtureFiles` below (`tests/fixtures/local-audio/*`)

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `resolveFfmpegBinaries()` from `tests/helpers/ffmpeg.mjs` (`{ffmpeg, ffprobe, dir}`), `noise`/`sine`/`prng` from `scripts/generateBundledAudio.mjs`.
- Produces (used by every later task):
  - `scripts/generateAudioFixtures.mjs`: `fixtureFiles: string[]`, `toneFixtures: Array<{hz: number; seconds: number; label: string}>`, `generateAudioFixtures(outDir: string): void`, `patchWavChannels(bytes: Uint8Array, channels: number): Buffer`.
  - `tests/helpers/audioBytes.mjs`: `readWavHeader(bytes): {channels: number; sampleRate: number; bitsPerSample: number; dataBytes: number; frames: number}`, `buildWav({sampleRate?: number; channels?: number; seconds?: number; dataBytes?: number}): Buffer` (silent 16-bit PCM WAV; `dataBytes` overrides `seconds`), `fixtureBytes(name: string): Uint8Array` (reads `tests/fixtures/local-audio/<name>`).
  - Fixture files (all in `tests/fixtures/local-audio/`): `tone-220-10s.{mp3,wav,m4a,opus.ogg}`, `tone-440-2.2s.{...}`, `tone-880-0.4s.{...}` (stereo; MP3 and M4A 44.1 kHz, WAV and Opus 48 kHz; unit-amplitude sine), `tone-220-12s.wav`, `noise-2s.mp3`, `mono-440.wav`, `three-channel.wav`, `zero-channel.wav`, `long-301s.mp3` (mono, 8 kHz, 8 kb/s), `with-video.mp4` (1 s, 64x64 H.264 plus AAC), `truncated.mp3` (first 60% of `tone-220-10s.mp3`), `not-audio.txt`. No Ogg Vorbis file.

- [ ] **Step 2: Write the helpers and the failing test**

`tests/helpers/audioBytes.mjs`:

```js
import assert from "node:assert/strict";
import {readFileSync} from "node:fs";

/** Reads a committed fixture from tests/fixtures/local-audio as a Uint8Array. */
export const fixtureBytes = (name) =>
  new Uint8Array(
    readFileSync(new URL(`../fixtures/local-audio/${name}`, import.meta.url)),
  );

/** Walks the RIFF chunks of a WAV and returns its format fields and data size. */
export const readWavHeader = (bytes) => {
  const buffer = Buffer.from(bytes.buffer, bytes.byteOffset, bytes.byteLength);

  assert.equal(buffer.toString("ascii", 0, 4), "RIFF");
  assert.equal(buffer.toString("ascii", 8, 12), "WAVE");

  let offset = 12;
  let format = null;

  while (offset + 8 <= buffer.length) {
    const id = buffer.toString("ascii", offset, offset + 4);
    const size = buffer.readUInt32LE(offset + 4);

    if (id === "fmt ") {
      format = {
        channels: buffer.readUInt16LE(offset + 10),
        sampleRate: buffer.readUInt32LE(offset + 12),
        bitsPerSample: buffer.readUInt16LE(offset + 22),
      };
    } else if (id === "data") {
      assert.notEqual(format, null, "fmt chunk must precede data");

      const blockAlign = (format.channels * format.bitsPerSample) / 8;

      return {
        ...format,
        dataBytes: size,
        frames: blockAlign === 0 ? 0 : size / blockAlign,
      };
    }

    offset += 8 + size + (size % 2);
  }

  throw new Error("No data chunk");
};

/**
 * A silent 16-bit PCM WAV with a valid 44-byte header. `dataBytes` (a multiple
 * of the block size) overrides `seconds`; it lets a test build a 30 MiB or a
 * 51 MiB file without any encoder.
 */
export const buildWav = ({
  sampleRate = 48_000,
  channels = 2,
  seconds = 1,
  dataBytes,
} = {}) => {
  const blockAlign = channels * 2;
  const data = dataBytes ?? Math.round(seconds * sampleRate) * blockAlign;
  const buffer = Buffer.alloc(44 + data);

  buffer.write("RIFF", 0, "ascii");
  buffer.writeUInt32LE(36 + data, 4);
  buffer.write("WAVE", 8, "ascii");
  buffer.write("fmt ", 12, "ascii");
  buffer.writeUInt32LE(16, 16);
  buffer.writeUInt16LE(1, 20);
  buffer.writeUInt16LE(channels, 22);
  buffer.writeUInt32LE(sampleRate, 24);
  buffer.writeUInt32LE(sampleRate * blockAlign, 28);
  buffer.writeUInt16LE(blockAlign, 32);
  buffer.writeUInt16LE(16, 34);
  buffer.write("data", 36, "ascii");
  buffer.writeUInt32LE(data, 40);

  return buffer;
};
```

`tests/audio-fixtures.test.mjs`:

```js
import assert from "node:assert/strict";
import {createHash} from "node:crypto";
import {mkdtempSync, readFileSync, readdirSync, rmSync, statSync} from "node:fs";
import {tmpdir} from "node:os";
import {join} from "node:path";
import {after, before, describe, it} from "node:test";
import {
  fixtureFiles,
  generateAudioFixtures,
  patchWavChannels,
} from "../scripts/generateAudioFixtures.mjs";
import {fixtureBytes, readWavHeader} from "./helpers/audioBytes.mjs";

const DIR = "tests/fixtures/local-audio";
const sha = (bytes) => createHash("sha256").update(bytes).digest("hex");
const wavSamples = (bytes) => {
  const buffer = Buffer.from(bytes.buffer, bytes.byteOffset, bytes.byteLength);
  const start = buffer.indexOf("data") + 8;

  return {buffer, start};
};

describe("committed audio fixtures", () => {
  const outputs = [];
  let first;
  let second;

  const generate = () => {
    const out = mkdtempSync(join(tmpdir(), "tora-audio-fixtures-"));

    outputs.push(out);
    generateAudioFixtures(out);

    return out;
  };

  before(() => {
    first = generate();
    second = generate();
  });
  after(() => {
    for (const dir of outputs) rmSync(dir, {recursive: true, force: true});
  });

  it("lists exactly the committed files", () => {
    assert.deepEqual(readdirSync(DIR).filter((name) => name !== "README.md").sort(), [...fixtureFiles].sort());
  });

  it("has no Ogg Vorbis fixture (the repository ffmpeg cannot encode Vorbis)", () => {
    assert.equal(fixtureFiles.some((name) => /vorbis/iu.test(name)), false);
  });

  it("keeps the committed fixtures small enough to review (under 8 MiB in total)", () => {
    const total = fixtureFiles.reduce((sum, name) => sum + statSync(join(DIR, name)).size, 0);

    assert.ok(total < 8 * 1024 * 1024, `${total} bytes`);
  });

  it("the generator is deterministic: two runs write byte-identical files", () => {
    for (const name of fixtureFiles) {
      assert.equal(sha(readFileSync(join(first, name))), sha(readFileSync(join(second, name))), name);
    }
  });

  it("a fresh run matches every committed PCM fixture (same header, every sample within 1 LSB)", () => {
    for (const name of fixtureFiles.filter((file) => file.endsWith(".wav"))) {
      const a = wavSamples(readFileSync(join(first, name)));
      const b = wavSamples(readFileSync(join(DIR, name)));

      assert.equal(a.buffer.length, b.buffer.length, `${name}: length`);
      assert.deepEqual(a.buffer.subarray(0, a.start), b.buffer.subarray(0, b.start), `${name}: header`);

      for (let at = a.start; at + 1 < a.buffer.length; at += 2) {
        assert.ok(Math.abs(a.buffer.readInt16LE(at) - b.buffer.readInt16LE(at)) <= 1, `${name}: sample ${(at - a.start) / 2}`);
      }
    }
  });

  it("the tone WAVs have the documented format, length and a full-scale peak", () => {
    const expected = [
      ["tone-220-10s.wav", 2, 480_000],
      ["tone-440-2.2s.wav", 2, 105_600],
      ["tone-880-0.4s.wav", 2, 19_200],
      ["tone-220-12s.wav", 2, 576_000],
      ["mono-440.wav", 1, 105_600],
      ["three-channel.wav", 3, 19_200],
    ];

    for (const [name, channels, frames] of expected) {
      const header = readWavHeader(fixtureBytes(name));

      assert.equal(header.channels, channels, name);
      assert.equal(header.sampleRate, 48_000, name);
      assert.equal(header.bitsPerSample, 16, name);
      assert.equal(header.frames, frames, name);

      const {buffer, start} = wavSamples(fixtureBytes(name));
      let peak = 0;

      for (let at = start; at + 1 < buffer.length; at += 2) peak = Math.max(peak, Math.abs(buffer.readInt16LE(at)));

      assert.ok(peak >= 32_000, `${name} peak ${peak}`);
    }
  });

  it("zero-channel.wav is mono-440.wav with only the fmt channel field patched to 0", () => {
    const mono = fixtureBytes("mono-440.wav");
    const zero = fixtureBytes("zero-channel.wav");

    assert.equal(readWavHeader(zero).channels, 0);
    assert.deepEqual(Buffer.from(zero), patchWavChannels(mono, 0));
    assert.deepEqual(Buffer.from(zero.subarray(24)), Buffer.from(mono.subarray(24)));
  });

  it("truncated.mp3 is the first 60% of the 10 s MP3", () => {
    const full = fixtureBytes("tone-220-10s.mp3");
    const cut = fixtureBytes("truncated.mp3");

    assert.equal(cut.length, Math.floor(full.length * 0.6));
    assert.deepEqual(Buffer.from(cut), Buffer.from(full.subarray(0, cut.length)));
  });

  it("not-audio.txt is plain text", () => {
    assert.equal(new TextDecoder().decode(fixtureBytes("not-audio.txt")), "This is not an audio file.\n");
  });

  it("with-video.mp4 and the lossy fixtures carry their container signatures", () => {
    const ascii = (name, from, to) => Buffer.from(fixtureBytes(name).subarray(from, to)).toString("latin1");

    assert.equal(ascii("with-video.mp4", 4, 8), "ftyp");
    assert.equal(ascii("tone-220-10s.m4a", 4, 8), "ftyp");
    assert.equal(ascii("tone-220-10s.opus.ogg", 0, 4), "OggS");
    assert.ok(["ID3", "\xff\xfb", "\xff\xf3", "\xff\xf2"].some((sig) => ascii("tone-220-10s.mp3", 0, sig.length) === sig));
  });
});
```

`scripts/generateAudioFixtures.mjs`:

```js
// Generates the committed audio fixtures (AUDIO-003 task 2) with the repository's
// ffmpeg (the one in the installed @remotion/compositor-* package).
//
//   node scripts/generateAudioFixtures.mjs [outDir]     (default: tests/fixtures/local-audio)
//
// That ffmpeg is a minimal build: no anoisesrc, highpass, lowpass or afade, no
// Vorbis encoder (so there is no Ogg Vorbis fixture), and no m4a muxer name (the
// MP4 muxer is selected with `-f mp4`). Tones come from the `sine` source lifted
// to a unit-amplitude (full scale) sine; the noise is a seeded sum of sine
// partials below 2 kHz (the same technique as generateBundledAudio.mjs).
//
// The PCM files (.wav) match across platforms within 1 LSB. The lossy files
// (mp3, m4a, opus.ogg, mp4) are deterministic for one ffmpeg build only: the
// committed bytes are authoritative (Stories reference them by digest) and
// tests never compare them with a regenerated copy.

import {Buffer} from "node:buffer";
import {execFileSync} from "node:child_process";
import {mkdirSync, readFileSync, writeFileSync} from "node:fs";
import {join, resolve} from "node:path";
import process from "node:process";
import {pathToFileURL} from "node:url";
import {resolveFfmpegBinaries} from "../tests/helpers/ffmpeg.mjs";
import {noise} from "./generateBundledAudio.mjs";

/** `sine` source lifted to a unit-amplitude sine (ffmpeg's `sine` peaks at 1/8). */
const tone = (hz, seconds, rate) =>
  `sine=f=${hz}:d=${seconds}:r=${rate},aformat=sample_fmts=fltp,volume=8`;

/** Seeded low-passed white noise: 64 partials, linear-uniform in [100, 2000] Hz, RMS 0.25. */
const noiseGraph = (seconds) =>
  `${noise({label: "n", seed: 21, count: 64, lo: 100, hi: 2000, tilt: 0, seconds, rms: 0.25, linear: true})};[n]aformat=sample_fmts=fltp[mono]`;

const MONO_TO_STEREO = "pan=stereo|c0=c0|c1=c0";
const MONO_TO_THREE = "pan=3.0|c0=c0|c1=c0|c2=c0";

/** The three tones, in every container: 220 Hz music, 440 Hz voice, 880 Hz effect. */
export const toneFixtures = [
  {hz: 220, seconds: 10, label: "220-10s"},
  {hz: 440, seconds: 2.2, label: "440-2.2s"},
  {hz: 880, seconds: 0.4, label: "880-0.4s"},
];

export const fixtureFiles = [
  ...toneFixtures.flatMap(({label}) => [
    `tone-${label}.mp3`,
    `tone-${label}.wav`,
    `tone-${label}.m4a`,
    `tone-${label}.opus.ogg`,
  ]),
  "tone-220-12s.wav",
  "noise-2s.mp3",
  "mono-440.wav",
  "three-channel.wav",
  "zero-channel.wav",
  "long-301s.mp3",
  "with-video.mp4",
  "truncated.mp3",
  "not-audio.txt",
];

/** Patches the `fmt ` channel count (RIFF offset 22) of a canonical 44-byte-header WAV. */
export const patchWavChannels = (bytes, channels) => {
  const out = Buffer.from(bytes);

  if (out.toString("ascii", 12, 16) !== "fmt ") {
    throw new Error("Expected a canonical WAV with `fmt ` as its first chunk.");
  }

  out.writeUInt16LE(channels, 22);
  return out;
};

export const generateAudioFixtures = (outDir) => {
  mkdirSync(outDir, {recursive: true});

  const {ffmpeg, dir} = resolveFfmpegBinaries();
  const env = {
    ...process.env,
    LD_LIBRARY_PATH: [dir, process.env.LD_LIBRARY_PATH].filter(Boolean).join(":"),
    DYLD_LIBRARY_PATH: [dir, process.env.DYLD_LIBRARY_PATH].filter(Boolean).join(":"),
  };
  const exec = (args) =>
    execFileSync(
      ffmpeg,
      [
        "-hide_banner", "-loglevel", "error", "-y",
        "-cpuflags", "0",
        "-bitexact", "-fflags", "+bitexact", "-flags:a", "+bitexact",
        "-filter_threads", "1", "-filter_complex_threads", "1",
        ...args,
      ],
      {env, windowsHide: true, stdio: ["ignore", "inherit", "inherit"]},
    );
  const path = (name) => join(outDir, name);
  // A graph ending in the `[out]` pad -> one file; `codec` are the output options.
  const render = (name, graph, codec, seconds) =>
    exec([
      "-filter_complex", graph, "-map", "[out]", "-map_metadata", "-1",
      ...codec, "-t", String(seconds), path(name),
    ]);
  const stereoTone = (hz, seconds, rate) =>
    `${tone(hz, seconds, 48_000)},aresample=${rate},${MONO_TO_STEREO}[out]`;

  for (const {hz, seconds, label} of toneFixtures) {
    render(`tone-${label}.wav`, stereoTone(hz, seconds, 48_000), ["-c:a", "pcm_s16le", "-f", "wav"], seconds);
    render(`tone-${label}.mp3`, stereoTone(hz, seconds, 44_100), ["-c:a", "libmp3lame", "-b:a", "128k", "-f", "mp3"], seconds);
    render(`tone-${label}.m4a`, stereoTone(hz, seconds, 44_100), ["-c:a", "aac", "-b:a", "128k", "-f", "mp4"], seconds);
    render(`tone-${label}.opus.ogg`, stereoTone(hz, seconds, 48_000), ["-c:a", "libopus", "-b:a", "96k", "-f", "ogg"], seconds);
  }

  render("tone-220-12s.wav", stereoTone(220, 12, 48_000), ["-c:a", "pcm_s16le", "-f", "wav"], 12);
  render("noise-2s.mp3", `${noiseGraph(2)};[mono]aresample=44100,${MONO_TO_STEREO}[out]`, ["-c:a", "libmp3lame", "-b:a", "128k", "-f", "mp3"], 2);
  render("mono-440.wav", `${tone(440, 2.2, 48_000)}[out]`, ["-c:a", "pcm_s16le", "-f", "wav"], 2.2);
  render("three-channel.wav", `${tone(440, 0.4, 48_000)},${MONO_TO_THREE}[out]`, ["-c:a", "pcm_s16le", "-f", "wav"], 0.4);
  writeFileSync(path("zero-channel.wav"), patchWavChannels(readFileSync(path("mono-440.wav")), 0));
  render("long-301s.mp3", `${tone(220, 301, 8_000)}[out]`, ["-c:a", "libmp3lame", "-b:a", "8k", "-f", "mp3"], 301);

  // A video track plus an audio track: the video check must win. The lavfi video
  // input needs a decoder this build lacks, so both sources live in the filtergraph.
  exec([
    "-filter_complex",
    `nullsrc=s=64x64:r=10:d=1,format=yuv420p[v];${tone(440, 1, 48_000)}[a]`,
    "-map", "[v]", "-map", "[a]", "-map_metadata", "-1",
    "-flags:v", "+bitexact", "-c:v", "libx264", "-preset", "ultrafast", "-threads", "1",
    "-x264-params", "threads=1:sliced-threads=0", "-c:a", "aac", "-b:a", "64k", "-t", "1",
    "-f", "mp4", path("with-video.mp4"),
  ]);

  const full = readFileSync(path("tone-220-10s.mp3"));

  writeFileSync(path("truncated.mp3"), full.subarray(0, Math.floor(full.length * 0.6)));
  writeFileSync(path("not-audio.txt"), "This is not an audio file.\n");
};

if (process.argv[1] && import.meta.url === pathToFileURL(process.argv[1]).href) {
  generateAudioFixtures(resolve(process.argv[2] ?? "tests/fixtures/local-audio"));
}
```

- [ ] **Step 5: Generate the committed fixtures and write their README**

Run: `node scripts/generateAudioFixtures.mjs` then `ls tests/fixtures/local-audio` (21 files plus the README, about 6.3 MB in total).

`tests/fixtures/local-audio/README.md`:

```markdown
# Local audio fixtures

Generated by `node scripts/generateAudioFixtures.mjs` with the repository ffmpeg and committed, because Stories reference them by digest.

| File | What it is | Used by |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `tone-220-10s.*`, `tone-440-2.2s.*`, `tone-880-0.4s.*` | Unit-amplitude sine (music 220 Hz, voice 440 Hz, effect 880 Hz) in MP3 (44.1 kHz), WAV (48 kHz), M4A (AAC, 44.1 kHz) and Ogg Opus (48 kHz), stereo | inspection, import, AUDIO-005/006 |
| `tone-220-12s.wav` | 220 Hz, 12 s, WAV | AUDIO-006 main reference (music) |
| `noise-2s.mp3` | 2.0 s seeded low-passed (< 2 kHz) white noise, MP3 | AUDIO-006 loop reference |
| `mono-440.wav` | 440 Hz mono WAV | mono acceptance |
| `three-channel.wav` | 3-channel WAV | rejection `Audio must be mono or stereo.` |
| `zero-channel.wav` | `mono-440.wav` with its `fmt ` channel field patched to 0 | same rejection |
| `long-301s.mp3` | 301 s, 8 kHz mono, 8 kb/s MP3 | rejection (longer than 300 s) |
| `with-video.mp4` | 1 s H.264 video plus AAC audio | rejection (`Video files are not supported.`) |
| `truncated.mp3` | first 60% of `tone-220-10s.mp3` (the Xing header still declares 10 s) | truncation rule |
| `not-audio.txt` | plain text | rejection (not a recognized container) |

There is no Ogg Vorbis fixture: the repository ffmpeg has no Vorbis encoder (the Vorbis ruling is recorded in `docs/audio/AUDIO-003-audio-library-and-import.md`).
```

### Task 2: `inspectAudio`

**Files:**
- Create: `src/localAssets/audioInspection.ts`, `tests/audio-inspection.test.mjs`
- Modify: `src/audio/catalog.ts` (`buildAudioDurationMap` drops non-finite and non-positive durations), `tests/audio-catalog.test.mjs`, `tests/bundled-audio.test.mjs` (cross-check)

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: limits from `src/localAssets/limits.ts`; fixtures and `fixtureBytes`/`buildWav` from Task 1.
- Produces:
  - `type AudioContainer = "mp3" | "wave" | "mp4" | "ogg"`, `type AudioCodec = "mp3" | "pcm" | "aac" | "opus" | "vorbis"`, `type AudioMetadata = {container: AudioContainer; codec: AudioCodec; durationSeconds: number; sampleRate: number; channels: 1 | 2}`, `type AudioInspection = {ok: true; metadata: AudioMetadata} | {ok: false; message: string}`.
  - `inspectAudio(bytes: Blob | Uint8Array, deps?: {readInfo?: ReadRawAudioInfo}): Promise<AudioInspection>`.
  - `RawAudioInfo = {container: AudioContainer | null; videoTrackCount: number; audioTracks: ReadonlyArray<{codec: string | null; sampleRate: number; channels: number}>; durationSeconds: number}`, `ReadRawAudioInfo = (source: Blob | Uint8Array) => Promise<RawAudioInfo>`, `readAudioInfoWithMediabunny: ReadRawAudioInfo`, `evaluateRawAudioInfo(info: RawAudioInfo): AudioInspection`.
  - `isAcceptedAudioCombination(container: AudioContainer, codec: AudioCodec): boolean`, `audioMimeTypeFor(container: AudioContainer): "audio/mpeg" | "audio/wav" | "audio/mp4" | "audio/ogg"`, `type AudioMimeType`, `audioFileExtensionFor(container: AudioContainer): "mp3" | "wav" | "m4a" | "ogg"`, `audioContainers`, `audioCodecs` (readonly tuples).
  - Message constants: `AUDIO_EMPTY_MESSAGE`, `AUDIO_TOO_LARGE_MESSAGE`, `AUDIO_UNSUPPORTED_MESSAGE`, `AUDIO_VIDEO_MESSAGE`, `AUDIO_TRACKS_MESSAGE`, `AUDIO_CODEC_MESSAGE`, `AUDIO_VORBIS_MESSAGE`, `AUDIO_DURATION_UNKNOWN_MESSAGE`, `AUDIO_TOO_LONG_MESSAGE`, `AUDIO_SAMPLE_RATE_MESSAGE`, `AUDIO_CHANNELS_MESSAGE`.

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing tests**

`tests/audio-inspection.test.mjs`:

```js
import assert from "node:assert/strict";
import {describe, it} from "node:test";
import {
  AUDIO_CHANNELS_MESSAGE,
  AUDIO_CODEC_MESSAGE,
  AUDIO_DURATION_UNKNOWN_MESSAGE,
  AUDIO_EMPTY_MESSAGE,
  AUDIO_SAMPLE_RATE_MESSAGE,
  AUDIO_TOO_LARGE_MESSAGE,
  AUDIO_TOO_LONG_MESSAGE,
  AUDIO_TRACKS_MESSAGE,
  AUDIO_UNSUPPORTED_MESSAGE,
  AUDIO_VIDEO_MESSAGE,
  AUDIO_VORBIS_MESSAGE,
  audioFileExtensionFor,
  audioMimeTypeFor,
  evaluateRawAudioInfo,
  inspectAudio,
  isAcceptedAudioCombination,
} from "../src/localAssets/audioInspection.ts";
import {MAX_LOCAL_AUDIO_BYTES} from "../src/localAssets/limits.ts";
import {fixtureBytes} from "./helpers/audioBytes.mjs";

const accepted = [
  ["tone-220-10s.mp3", "mp3", "mp3", 10.031, 44_100, 2],
  ["tone-220-10s.wav", "wave", "pcm", 10, 48_000, 2],
  ["tone-220-10s.m4a", "mp4", "aac", 10, 44_100, 2],
  ["tone-220-10s.opus.ogg", "ogg", "opus", 10.0135, 48_000, 2],
  ["tone-440-2.2s.wav", "wave", "pcm", 2.2, 48_000, 2],
  ["tone-440-2.2s.m4a", "mp4", "aac", 2.2, 44_100, 2],
  ["tone-880-0.4s.wav", "wave", "pcm", 0.4, 48_000, 2],
  ["tone-880-0.4s.opus.ogg", "ogg", "opus", 0.4135, 48_000, 2],
  ["tone-220-12s.wav", "wave", "pcm", 12, 48_000, 2],
  ["mono-440.wav", "wave", "pcm", 2.2, 48_000, 1],
  ["noise-2s.mp3", "mp3", "mp3", 2.0376, 44_100, 2],
];

describe("inspectAudio on the fixtures", () => {
  for (const [name, container, codec, seconds, sampleRate, channels] of accepted) {
    it(`accepts ${name}`, async () => {
      const result = await inspectAudio(fixtureBytes(name));

      assert.equal(result.ok, true, JSON.stringify(result));
      assert.deepEqual(Object.keys(result.metadata).sort(), ["channels", "codec", "container", "durationSeconds", "sampleRate"]);
      assert.equal(result.metadata.container, container);
      assert.equal(result.metadata.codec, codec);
      assert.equal(result.metadata.sampleRate, sampleRate);
      assert.equal(result.metadata.channels, channels);
      assert.ok(Math.abs(result.metadata.durationSeconds - seconds) < 0.0005, `${name}: ${result.metadata.durationSeconds}`);
    });
  }

  it("reports the declared (metadata) duration of the truncated MP3, not the shorter scanned one", async () => {
    const result = await inspectAudio(fixtureBytes("truncated.mp3"));

    assert.equal(result.ok, true);
    assert.ok(Math.abs(result.metadata.durationSeconds - 10.031) < 0.0005, `${result.metadata.durationSeconds}`);
  });

  it("gives the same result for a Blob and a Uint8Array", async () => {
    for (const [name] of accepted) {
      const bytes = fixtureBytes(name);

      assert.deepEqual(await inspectAudio(new Blob([bytes])), await inspectAudio(bytes), name);
    }
  });

  const rejected = [
    ["a text file", "not-audio.txt", AUDIO_UNSUPPORTED_MESSAGE],
    ["an MP4 with a video track", "with-video.mp4", AUDIO_VIDEO_MESSAGE],
    ["a 301 s MP3", "long-301s.mp3", AUDIO_TOO_LONG_MESSAGE],
    ["a 3-channel WAV", "three-channel.wav", "Audio must be mono or stereo."],
    ["a 0-channel WAV", "zero-channel.wav", "Audio must be mono or stereo."],
  ];

  for (const [title, name, message] of rejected) {
    it(`rejects ${title} with its exact message`, async () => {
      assert.deepEqual(await inspectAudio(fixtureBytes(name)), {ok: false, message});
    });
  }

  it("rejects an empty Blob and an empty Uint8Array", async () => {
    assert.deepEqual(await inspectAudio(new Uint8Array(0)), {ok: false, message: AUDIO_EMPTY_MESSAGE});
    assert.deepEqual(await inspectAudio(new Blob([])), {ok: false, message: AUDIO_EMPTY_MESSAGE});
  });

  it("rejects a few bytes that start like a container but are cut short", async () => {
    const result = await inspectAudio(fixtureBytes("tone-220-10s.wav").subarray(0, 20));

    assert.equal(result.ok, false);
    assert.ok(result.message.length > 0);
  });
});

const info = (overrides = {}) => ({
  container: "wave",
  videoTrackCount: 0,
  audioTracks: [{codec: "pcm-s16", sampleRate: 48_000, channels: 2}],
  durationSeconds: 5,
  ...overrides,
});
const track = (overrides) => ({codec: "pcm-s16", sampleRate: 48_000, channels: 2, ...overrides});
const via = (raw) => inspectAudio(new Uint8Array(8), {readInfo: async () => raw});
const messageOf = async (raw) => {
  const result = await via(raw);

  assert.equal(result.ok, false, JSON.stringify(result));

  return result.message;
};

describe("inspectAudio rules (injected reader)", () => {
  it("rejects by byte size before the reader is called", async () => {
    let calls = 0;
    const readInfo = async () => {
      calls += 1;
      return info();
    };

    assert.deepEqual(await inspectAudio(new Uint8Array(0), {readInfo}), {ok: false, message: AUDIO_EMPTY_MESSAGE});
    assert.deepEqual(await inspectAudio(new Uint8Array(MAX_LOCAL_AUDIO_BYTES + 1), {readInfo}), {ok: false, message: AUDIO_TOO_LARGE_MESSAGE});
    assert.equal(calls, 0);
    assert.equal((await inspectAudio(new Uint8Array(MAX_LOCAL_AUDIO_BYTES), {readInfo})).ok, true);
    assert.equal(calls, 1);
  });

  it("applies the rules in the documented order", async () => {
    assert.equal(await messageOf(info({container: null, videoTrackCount: 1})), AUDIO_UNSUPPORTED_MESSAGE);
    assert.equal(await messageOf(info({videoTrackCount: 1, audioTracks: []})), AUDIO_VIDEO_MESSAGE);
    assert.equal(await messageOf(info({audioTracks: []})), AUDIO_TRACKS_MESSAGE);
    assert.equal(await messageOf(info({audioTracks: [track(), track()]})), AUDIO_TRACKS_MESSAGE);
    assert.equal(await messageOf(info({audioTracks: [track({codec: "flac"})], durationSeconds: Number.NaN})), AUDIO_CODEC_MESSAGE);
    assert.equal(await messageOf(info({durationSeconds: Number.NaN, audioTracks: [track({sampleRate: 1, channels: 5})]})), AUDIO_DURATION_UNKNOWN_MESSAGE);
    assert.equal(await messageOf(info({audioTracks: [track({sampleRate: 1, channels: 5})]})), AUDIO_SAMPLE_RATE_MESSAGE);
    assert.equal(await messageOf(info({audioTracks: [track({channels: 5})]})), AUDIO_CHANNELS_MESSAGE);
  });

  it("accepts only the (container, codec) pairs of the spec", async () => {
    const pairs = [
      ["mp3", "mp3", true], ["wave", "pcm-s16", true], ["wave", "pcm-f32", true], ["wave", "pcm-s24", true],
      ["mp4", "aac", true], ["ogg", "opus", true],
      ["mp3", "aac", false], ["wave", "mp3", false], ["wave", "ulaw", false], ["wave", "alaw", false],
      ["mp4", "mp3", false], ["mp4", "flac", false], ["ogg", "flac", false], ["ogg", "aac", false], ["mp3", null, false],
    ];

    for (const [container, codec, ok] of pairs) {
      const result = await via(info({container, audioTracks: [track({codec})]}));

      assert.equal(result.ok, ok, `${container}/${codec}`);

      if (!ok) assert.equal(result.message, AUDIO_CODEC_MESSAGE, `${container}/${codec}`);
    }
  });

  it("rejects Ogg Vorbis with the Vorbis message (no Vorbis fixture can be produced)", async () => {
    assert.deepEqual(await via(info({container: "ogg", audioTracks: [track({codec: "vorbis"})]})), {ok: false, message: "Ogg Vorbis audio is not supported. Use MP3, WAV, M4A or Ogg Opus."});
    assert.equal(AUDIO_VORBIS_MESSAGE, "Ogg Vorbis audio is not supported. Use MP3, WAV, M4A or Ogg Opus.");
  });

  it("checks the duration: not finite, <= 0 or > 300 s", async () => {
    for (const durationSeconds of [Number.NaN, Number.POSITIVE_INFINITY, Number.NEGATIVE_INFINITY, 0, -1]) {
      assert.equal(await messageOf(info({durationSeconds})), AUDIO_DURATION_UNKNOWN_MESSAGE, String(durationSeconds));
    }

    assert.equal(await messageOf(info({durationSeconds: 300.0001})), AUDIO_TOO_LONG_MESSAGE);
    assert.equal((await via(info({durationSeconds: 300}))).ok, true);
    assert.equal((await via(info({durationSeconds: 0.005}))).ok, true);
  });

  it("checks the sample rate: a finite integer in [8000, 96000]", async () => {
    for (const sampleRate of [7_999, 96_001, 44_100.5, Number.NaN, Number.POSITIVE_INFINITY, 0, -48_000]) {
      assert.equal(await messageOf(info({audioTracks: [track({sampleRate})]})), AUDIO_SAMPLE_RATE_MESSAGE, String(sampleRate));
    }

    for (const sampleRate of [8_000, 44_100, 96_000]) {
      assert.equal((await via(info({audioTracks: [track({sampleRate})]}))).ok, true, String(sampleRate));
    }
  });

  it("checks the channel count: exactly 1 or 2 (zero, negative, non-integral, more than 2 all fail)", async () => {
    for (const channels of [0, -1, 1.5, 2.0000001, 3, 6, Number.NaN, Number.POSITIVE_INFINITY]) {
      assert.equal(await messageOf(info({audioTracks: [track({channels})]})), "Audio must be mono or stereo.", String(channels));
    }

    for (const channels of [1, 2]) {
      assert.equal((await via(info({audioTracks: [track({channels})]}))).metadata.channels, channels);
    }
  });

  it("treats a failing reader as an unsupported file", async () => {
    const result = await inspectAudio(new Uint8Array(8), {readInfo: async () => { throw new Error("boom"); }});

    assert.deepEqual(result, {ok: false, message: AUDIO_UNSUPPORTED_MESSAGE});
  });

  it("evaluateRawAudioInfo returns exactly the AudioMetadata keys", () => {
    const result = evaluateRawAudioInfo(info({container: "mp4", audioTracks: [track({codec: "aac", sampleRate: 44_100, channels: 1})], durationSeconds: 1.5}));

    assert.deepEqual(result, {ok: true, metadata: {container: "mp4", codec: "aac", durationSeconds: 1.5, sampleRate: 44_100, channels: 1}});
  });
});

describe("audio payload helpers", () => {
  it("maps containers to MIME types and staged extensions, never from the file's own type", () => {
    assert.deepEqual(["mp3", "wave", "mp4", "ogg"].map(audioMimeTypeFor), ["audio/mpeg", "audio/wav", "audio/mp4", "audio/ogg"]);
    assert.deepEqual(["mp3", "wave", "mp4", "ogg"].map(audioFileExtensionFor), ["mp3", "wav", "m4a", "ogg"]);
  });

  it("knows the accepted combinations", () => {
    assert.equal(isAcceptedAudioCombination("mp3", "mp3"), true);
    assert.equal(isAcceptedAudioCombination("ogg", "vorbis"), false);
    assert.equal(isAcceptedAudioCombination("mp4", "mp3"), false);
  });
});
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement `src/localAssets/audioInspection.ts`**

```ts
import {
  ALL_FORMATS,
  BlobSource,
  BufferSource,
  Input,
  MP3,
  MP4,
  OGG,
  WAVE,
} from "mediabunny";
import {
  MAX_AUDIO_CHANNELS,
  MAX_AUDIO_DURATION_SECONDS,
  MAX_AUDIO_SAMPLE_RATE,
  MAX_LOCAL_AUDIO_BYTES,
  MIN_AUDIO_SAMPLE_RATE,
} from "./limits.ts";

export type AudioContainer = "mp3" | "wave" | "mp4" | "ogg";
export type AudioCodec = "mp3" | "pcm" | "aac" | "opus" | "vorbis";

export type AudioMetadata = {
  container: AudioContainer;
  codec: AudioCodec;
  durationSeconds: number;
  sampleRate: number;
  channels: 1 | 2;
};

export type AudioInspection =
  | {ok: true; metadata: AudioMetadata}
  | {ok: false; message: string};

/** What a media reader reports, before any acceptance rule is applied. */
export type RawAudioInfo = {
  /** null: a container this feature does not accept (or not a media file at all). */
  container: AudioContainer | null;
  videoTrackCount: number;
  audioTracks: ReadonlyArray<{
    /** The reader's codec name ("mp3", "aac", "opus", "vorbis", "pcm-s16", "flac"...), or null. */
    codec: string | null;
    sampleRate: number;
    channels: number;
  }>;
  /** NaN when the reader did not compute it (an earlier rule already rejects the file). */
  durationSeconds: number;
};

export type ReadRawAudioInfo = (
  source: Blob | Uint8Array,
) => Promise<RawAudioInfo>;

const MIB = 1024 * 1024;

export const AUDIO_EMPTY_MESSAGE = "This audio file is empty.";
export const AUDIO_TOO_LARGE_MESSAGE = `This audio file is larger than ${MAX_LOCAL_AUDIO_BYTES / MIB} MiB.`;
export const AUDIO_UNSUPPORTED_MESSAGE =
  "This is not a supported audio file. Use MP3, WAV, M4A or Ogg Opus.";
export const AUDIO_VIDEO_MESSAGE =
  "Video files are not supported. Use an audio file.";
export const AUDIO_TRACKS_MESSAGE =
  "The file must contain exactly one audio track.";
export const AUDIO_CODEC_MESSAGE =
  "This audio codec is not supported. Use MP3, WAV, M4A or Ogg Opus.";
export const AUDIO_VORBIS_MESSAGE =
  "Ogg Vorbis audio is not supported. Use MP3, WAV, M4A or Ogg Opus.";
export const AUDIO_DURATION_UNKNOWN_MESSAGE =
  "The length of this audio file could not be read.";
export const AUDIO_TOO_LONG_MESSAGE = `Audio can be at most ${MAX_AUDIO_DURATION_SECONDS} seconds (${MAX_AUDIO_DURATION_SECONDS / 60} minutes) long.`;
export const AUDIO_SAMPLE_RATE_MESSAGE = `Audio sample rate must be between ${MIN_AUDIO_SAMPLE_RATE} and ${MAX_AUDIO_SAMPLE_RATE} Hz.`;
export const AUDIO_CHANNELS_MESSAGE = "Audio must be mono or stereo.";

export const audioContainers = ["mp3", "wave", "mp4", "ogg"] as const;
export const audioCodecs = ["mp3", "pcm", "aac", "opus", "vorbis"] as const;

const ACCEPTED_CODECS: Readonly<Record<AudioContainer, AudioCodec>> = {
  mp3: "mp3",
  wave: "pcm",
  mp4: "aac",
  ogg: "opus",
};

/** True for the (container, codec) pairs a Story may use: mp3/mp3, wave/pcm, mp4/aac, ogg/opus. */
export const isAcceptedAudioCombination = (
  container: AudioContainer,
  codec: AudioCodec,
): boolean => ACCEPTED_CODECS[container] === codec;

const AUDIO_MIME_TYPES = {
  mp3: "audio/mpeg",
  wave: "audio/wav",
  mp4: "audio/mp4",
  ogg: "audio/ogg",
} as const satisfies Record<AudioContainer, string>;

export type AudioMimeType = (typeof AUDIO_MIME_TYPES)[AudioContainer];

/** The payload MIME type, chosen from the inspected container and never from the file's own type. */
export const audioMimeTypeFor = (container: AudioContainer): AudioMimeType =>
  AUDIO_MIME_TYPES[container];

const AUDIO_FILE_EXTENSIONS = {
  mp3: "mp3",
  wave: "wav",
  mp4: "m4a",
  ogg: "ogg",
} as const satisfies Record<AudioContainer, string>;

/** The extension of a staged copy, by inspected container. */
export const audioFileExtensionFor = (
  container: AudioContainer,
): (typeof AUDIO_FILE_EXTENSIONS)[AudioContainer] =>
  AUDIO_FILE_EXTENSIONS[container];

const toAudioCodec = (reported: string | null): AudioCodec | null => {
  if (reported === null) {
    return null;
  }

  if (reported.startsWith("pcm-")) {
    return "pcm";
  }

  return reported === "mp3" ||
    reported === "aac" ||
    reported === "opus" ||
    reported === "vorbis"
    ? reported
    : null;
};

const isIntegerInRange = (value: number, min: number, max: number): boolean =>
  Number.isFinite(value) &&
  Number.isInteger(value) &&
  value >= min &&
  value <= max;

/**
 * Applies the acceptance rules, in the order the spec lists them, to what a
 * reader found. Pure: the byte-size rules run before any reader is called.
 */
export const evaluateRawAudioInfo = (info: RawAudioInfo): AudioInspection => {
  if (info.container === null) {
    return {ok: false, message: AUDIO_UNSUPPORTED_MESSAGE};
  }

  if (info.videoTrackCount > 0) {
    return {ok: false, message: AUDIO_VIDEO_MESSAGE};
  }

  if (info.audioTracks.length !== 1) {
    return {ok: false, message: AUDIO_TRACKS_MESSAGE};
  }

  const [track] = info.audioTracks;
  const codec = toAudioCodec(track.codec);

  if (info.container === "ogg" && codec === "vorbis") {
    return {ok: false, message: AUDIO_VORBIS_MESSAGE};
  }

  if (codec === null || !isAcceptedAudioCombination(info.container, codec)) {
    return {ok: false, message: AUDIO_CODEC_MESSAGE};
  }

  if (!Number.isFinite(info.durationSeconds) || info.durationSeconds <= 0) {
    return {ok: false, message: AUDIO_DURATION_UNKNOWN_MESSAGE};
  }

  if (info.durationSeconds > MAX_AUDIO_DURATION_SECONDS) {
    return {ok: false, message: AUDIO_TOO_LONG_MESSAGE};
  }

  if (
    !isIntegerInRange(
      track.sampleRate,
      MIN_AUDIO_SAMPLE_RATE,
      MAX_AUDIO_SAMPLE_RATE,
    )
  ) {
    return {ok: false, message: AUDIO_SAMPLE_RATE_MESSAGE};
  }

  if (!isIntegerInRange(track.channels, 1, MAX_AUDIO_CHANNELS)) {
    return {ok: false, message: AUDIO_CHANNELS_MESSAGE};
  }

  return {
    ok: true,
    metadata: {
      container: info.container,
      codec,
      durationSeconds: info.durationSeconds,
      sampleRate: track.sampleRate,
      channels: track.channels as 1 | 2,
    },
  };
};

const UNRECOGNIZED: RawAudioInfo = {
  container: null,
  videoTrackCount: 0,
  audioTracks: [],
  durationSeconds: Number.NaN,
};

/**
 * Metadata only, no decoding (S-7): runs unchanged in Node and in the browser.
 * The duration is the one the container declares (AINV-3): the metadata
 * duration when the file has one (an MP3 with a Xing header, an MP4 or WAV
 * header), else the end of the last packet. A truncated MP3 with a Xing header
 * therefore keeps its declared length, which is what the truncation rule needs.
 * Any reader failure means "not a supported audio file".
 */
export const readAudioInfoWithMediabunny: ReadRawAudioInfo = async (source) => {
  const input = new Input({
    source:
      source instanceof Uint8Array
        ? new BufferSource(source)
        : new BlobSource(source),
    formats: ALL_FORMATS,
  });

  try {
    if (!(await input.canRead())) {
      return UNRECOGNIZED;
    }

    const format = await input.getFormat();
    const container: AudioContainer | null =
      format === MP3
        ? "mp3"
        : format === WAVE
          ? "wave"
          : format === MP4
            ? "mp4"
            : format === OGG
              ? "ogg"
              : null;

    if (container === null) {
      return UNRECOGNIZED;
    }

    const videoTracks = await input.getVideoTracks();
    const audioTracks = await input.getAudioTracks();
    const described = await Promise.all(
      audioTracks.map(async (track) => ({
        codec: await track.getCodec(),
        sampleRate: await track.getSampleRate(),
        channels: await track.getNumberOfChannels(),
      })),
    );
    let durationSeconds = Number.NaN;

    if (videoTracks.length === 0 && audioTracks.length === 1) {
      durationSeconds =
        (await input.getDurationFromMetadata()) ??
        (await input.computeDuration());
    }

    return {
      container,
      videoTrackCount: videoTracks.length,
      audioTracks: described,
      durationSeconds,
    };
  } catch {
    return UNRECOGNIZED;
  } finally {
    input.dispose();
  }
};

/**
 * Reads the container metadata of an audio file and applies the acceptance
 * rules. `bytes` may be a Blob or File (read lazily) or a Uint8Array. Never
 * returns metadata outside the AudioMetadata type.
 */
export const inspectAudio = async (
  bytes: Blob | Uint8Array,
  deps: {readInfo?: ReadRawAudioInfo} = {},
): Promise<AudioInspection> => {
  const size = bytes instanceof Uint8Array ? bytes.byteLength : bytes.size;

  if (!(size > 0)) {
    return {ok: false, message: AUDIO_EMPTY_MESSAGE};
  }

  if (size > MAX_LOCAL_AUDIO_BYTES) {
    return {ok: false, message: AUDIO_TOO_LARGE_MESSAGE};
  }

  let info: RawAudioInfo;

  try {
    info = await (deps.readInfo ?? readAudioInfoWithMediabunny)(bytes);
  } catch {
    return {ok: false, message: AUDIO_UNSUPPORTED_MESSAGE};
  }

  return evaluateRawAudioInfo(info);
};
```

In `src/audio/catalog.ts`, inside `buildAudioDurationMap`, replace `if (typeof seconds === "number") map[ref] = seconds;` with:

```ts
    // AINV-2 / compileTimeline: a NaN, infinite or non-positive duration would make
    // the timeline throw inside the Player, so it never enters the map.
    if (typeof seconds === "number" && Number.isFinite(seconds) && seconds > 0) {
      map[ref] = seconds;
    }
```

```js
import assert from "node:assert/strict";
import {describe, it} from "node:test";
import {checkLocalAssetByteSize} from "../src/localAssets/imageInspection.ts";
import {
  MAX_LOCAL_ASSET_BYTES,
  MAX_LOCAL_AUDIO_BYTES,
  maxLocalAssetBytes,
} from "../src/localAssets/limits.ts";
import {buildWav} from "./helpers/audioBytes.mjs";
import {inspectAudio} from "../src/localAssets/audioInspection.ts";

const MIB = 1024 * 1024;

describe("per-category byte limits", () => {
  it("maps each category to its own ceiling", () => {
    assert.equal(maxLocalAssetBytes("pose"), 25 * MIB);
    assert.equal(maxLocalAssetBytes("background"), 25 * MIB);
    assert.equal(maxLocalAssetBytes("audio"), 50 * MIB);
    assert.equal(MAX_LOCAL_ASSET_BYTES, 25 * MIB);
    assert.equal(MAX_LOCAL_AUDIO_BYTES, 50 * MIB);
  });

  it("accepts 30 MiB of audio and rejects a 26 MiB image (image limit unchanged)", () => {
    assert.deepEqual(checkLocalAssetByteSize(30 * MIB, "audio"), {ok: true});
    assert.deepEqual(checkLocalAssetByteSize(26 * MIB, "pose"), {ok: false, reason: "too-large"});
    assert.deepEqual(checkLocalAssetByteSize(26 * MIB, "background"), {ok: false, reason: "too-large"});
    assert.deepEqual(checkLocalAssetByteSize(25 * MIB, "pose"), {ok: true});
  });

  it("bounds audio at exactly 50 MiB and treats zero as empty", () => {
    assert.deepEqual(checkLocalAssetByteSize(50 * MIB, "audio"), {ok: true});
    assert.deepEqual(checkLocalAssetByteSize(50 * MIB + 1, "audio"), {ok: false, reason: "too-large"});
    assert.deepEqual(checkLocalAssetByteSize(0, "audio"), {ok: false, reason: "empty"});
    assert.deepEqual(checkLocalAssetByteSize(Number.NaN, "audio"), {ok: false, reason: "empty"});
  });
});

describe("generated large WAVs (built at test time, never committed)", () => {
  it("a 30 MiB 48 kHz stereo WAV is about 164 s and inspects as valid audio", async () => {
    const wav = buildWav({dataBytes: 30 * MIB - 44});

    assert.equal(wav.length, 30 * MIB);

    const result = await inspectAudio(wav);

    assert.equal(result.ok, true, JSON.stringify(result));
    assert.ok(Math.abs(result.metadata.durationSeconds - 163.84) < 0.01, `${result.metadata.durationSeconds}`);
  });

  it("a 51 MiB file with a valid WAV header is rejected by size before the reader runs", async () => {
    const wav = buildWav({dataBytes: 51 * MIB - 44});
    let reads = 0;
    const result = await inspectAudio(wav, {readInfo: async () => { reads += 1; throw new Error("must not read"); }});

    assert.equal(result.ok, false);
    assert.match(result.message, /larger than 50 MiB/u);
    assert.equal(reads, 0);
  });
});
```

```ts
/** The largest accepted file for one category (images 25 MiB, audio 50 MiB). */
export const maxLocalAssetBytes = (category: LocalAssetCategory): number =>
  category === "audio" ? MAX_LOCAL_AUDIO_BYTES : MAX_LOCAL_ASSET_BYTES;
```

`src/localAssets/imageInspection.ts`: change the import to `import {MAX_LOCAL_ASSET_DIMENSION, MAX_LOCAL_ASSET_PIXELS, maxLocalAssetBytes} from "./limits.ts";` (drop `MAX_LOCAL_ASSET_BYTES` if it is no longer used there), add `import type {LocalAssetCategory} from "./refs.ts";`, and replace `checkLocalAssetByteSize`:

```ts
export const checkLocalAssetByteSize = (
  byteSize: number,
  category: LocalAssetCategory,
): {ok: true} | {ok: false; reason: "empty" | "too-large"} => {
  if (!(byteSize > 0)) {
    return {ok: false, reason: "empty"};
  }

  if (byteSize > maxLocalAssetBytes(category)) {
    return {ok: false, reason: "too-large"};
  }

  return {ok: true};
};
```

In `inspectImageBytes` replace `checkLocalAssetByteSize(bytes.length)` with `checkLocalAssetByteSize(bytes.length, "pose")` and add the comment `// Pose and background share the image limit; the header inspector only ever sees images.`. In `importAsset.ts` replace `checkLocalAssetByteSize(file.size)` with `checkLocalAssetByteSize(file.size, category)`. In `scripts/localAssets.ts` replace `checkLocalAssetByteSize(stats.size)` with `checkLocalAssetByteSize(stats.size, category)`.

**Files:**
- Create: `src/audio/decodeCheck.ts`, `src/web/assetLibrary/audioPeaks.ts`, `tests/audio-decode-check.test.mjs`, `tests/audio-peaks.test.mjs`
- Modify: `tests/harness/vite.harness.config.mts` (serve the audio fixtures), `tests/harness/asset-library.ts` (two hooks), `tests/harness/asset-library-indexeddb.spec.mjs` (one new `test.describe`)

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `AudioMetadata`, `inspectAudio` (Task 2); `WAVEFORM_PEAKS_PER_SECOND`, `MAX_WAVEFORM_PEAKS` (`src/audio/constants.ts`); `AUDIO_TRUNCATION_TOLERANCE_SECONDS`; fixtures (Task 1).
- Produces:
  - `isDecodeComplete(decodedSeconds: number, durationSeconds: number): boolean` (`src/audio/decodeCheck.ts`; the D-9 rule, used here by the import and later by AUDIO-005).
  - `type DecodedAudioSummary = {decodedSeconds: number; peaks: Uint8Array; peaksPerSecond: number}`, `class AudioDecodeError extends Error` (`name` `"AudioDecodeError"`, optional `cause`), `peakBucketCount(durationSeconds: number): number`, `createPeakAccumulator(durationSeconds: number): {push(startSeconds: number, sampleRate: number, planes: readonly Float32Array[]): void; finish(): DecodedAudioSummary}`, `decodeAudioPeaks(blob: Blob, metadata: AudioMetadata, deps?: {signal?: AbortSignal}): Promise<DecodedAudioSummary>` (`src/web/assetLibrary/audioPeaks.ts`).
  - Harness hooks `window.toraHarness.decodeAudioFixture(name)` and `aacDecodeSupported()` (used by this task's harness specs).

Measured in Chromium 140 (Playwright 1.55 bundled): `AudioDecoder` supports `mp3`, `opus`, `pcm-s16`, **not AAC** (`mp4a.40.2`); system Chrome (`channel: "chrome"`) decodes AAC. M4A import therefore needs system Chrome (Task 10 puts its browser spec in `playwright.browser-render.config.mjs`).

`tests/audio-decode-check.test.mjs`:

```js
import assert from "node:assert/strict";
import {describe, it} from "node:test";
import {isDecodeComplete} from "../src/audio/decodeCheck.ts";

describe("isDecodeComplete (D-9)", () => {
  it("a 0.12 s clip: nothing decoded fails, 0.05 fails, 0.11 passes", () => {
    assert.equal(isDecodeComplete(0, 0.12), false);
    assert.equal(isDecodeComplete(0.05, 0.12), false);
    assert.equal(isDecodeComplete(0.11, 0.12), true);
  });

  it("a 10 s file: 9.8 passes, 9.7 and 6.0 fail, the boundary 9.75 passes", () => {
    assert.equal(isDecodeComplete(9.8, 10), true);
    assert.equal(isDecodeComplete(9.7, 10), false);
    assert.equal(isDecodeComplete(6, 10), false);
    assert.equal(isDecodeComplete(9.75, 10), true);
    assert.equal(isDecodeComplete(10, 10), true);
    assert.equal(isDecodeComplete(10.5, 10), true);
  });

  it("zero decoded samples always fail, even for a 0.4 s file and a sub-frame one", () => {
    assert.equal(isDecodeComplete(0, 0.4), false);
    assert.equal(isDecodeComplete(0, 0.005), false);
    assert.equal(isDecodeComplete(0.003, 0.005), true);
    assert.equal(isDecodeComplete(0.002, 0.005), false);
  });

  it("never passes a non-finite or negative decoded length", () => {
    assert.equal(isDecodeComplete(Number.NaN, 10), false);
    assert.equal(isDecodeComplete(-1, 10), false);
  });
});
```

`tests/audio-peaks.test.mjs`:

```js
import assert from "node:assert/strict";
import {describe, it} from "node:test";
import {isDecodeComplete} from "../src/audio/decodeCheck.ts";
import {inspectAudio} from "../src/localAssets/audioInspection.ts";
import {
  AudioDecodeError,
  createPeakAccumulator,
  decodeAudioPeaks,
  peakBucketCount,
} from "../src/web/assetLibrary/audioPeaks.ts";
import {fixtureBytes} from "./helpers/audioBytes.mjs";

const plane = (values) => Float32Array.from(values);

describe("peakBucketCount", () => {
  it("is ceil(duration x 50) capped at 15000, and at least 1", () => {
    assert.equal(peakBucketCount(10), 500);
    assert.equal(peakBucketCount(2.2), 111);
    assert.equal(peakBucketCount(0.12), 6);
    assert.equal(peakBucketCount(0.005), 1);
    assert.equal(peakBucketCount(300), 15_000);
    assert.equal(peakBucketCount(400), 15_000);
  });
});

describe("peak accumulator", () => {
  it("keeps the max |sample| over all channels per bucket, quantized to 0..255", () => {
    // 1 s at 100 Hz: 50 buckets of 2 frames.
    const accumulator = createPeakAccumulator(1);
    const left = new Array(100).fill(0);
    const right = new Array(100).fill(0);

    left[0] = 0.4;
    right[3] = -1;
    accumulator.push(0, 100, [plane(left), plane(right)]);

    const summary = accumulator.finish();

    assert.equal(summary.peaks.length, 50);
    assert.equal(summary.peaks[0], 102);
    assert.equal(summary.peaks[1], 255);
    assert.equal(summary.peaks.slice(2).every((value) => value === 0), true);
    assert.equal(summary.peaksPerSecond, 50);
    assert.equal(summary.decodedSeconds, 1);
  });

  it("clamps values above full scale, ignores NaN, and clamps out-of-range frames to the ends", () => {
    const accumulator = createPeakAccumulator(1);

    accumulator.push(0, 100, [plane([1.5, Number.NaN])]);
    accumulator.push(1.5, 100, [plane([0.5])]);
    accumulator.push(-0.5, 100, [plane([1])]);

    const {peaks} = accumulator.finish();

    assert.equal(peaks[0], 255);
    assert.equal(peaks[49], 128);
  });

  it("gives the same peaks for one big chunk and for several chunks with their own start times", () => {
    const values = Array.from({length: 100}, (_, index) => Math.sin(index) * 0.9);
    const whole = createPeakAccumulator(1);
    const chunks = createPeakAccumulator(1);

    whole.push(0, 100, [plane(values)]);

    for (let start = 0; start < 100; start += 25) {
      chunks.push(start / 100, 100, [plane(values.slice(start, start + 25))]);
    }

    assert.deepEqual(chunks.finish().peaks, whole.finish().peaks);
  });

  it("derives peaksPerSecond from the bucket count and the duration, and reports 0 decoded seconds when nothing was pushed", () => {
    const accumulator = createPeakAccumulator(2.2);
    const summary = accumulator.finish();

    assert.equal(summary.peaks.length, 111);
    assert.equal(summary.peaksPerSecond, 111 / 2.2);
    assert.equal(summary.decodedSeconds, 0);
    assert.equal(isDecodeComplete(summary.decodedSeconds, 2.2), false);
  });
});

describe("decodeAudioPeaks on PCM fixtures (mediabunny decodes PCM without WebCodecs)", () => {
  const decode = async (name, deps) => {
    const bytes = fixtureBytes(name);
    const inspected = await inspectAudio(bytes);

    assert.equal(inspected.ok, true);

    return {
      metadata: inspected.metadata,
      summary: await decodeAudioPeaks(new Blob([bytes]), inspected.metadata, deps),
    };
  };

  it("a full-scale 220 Hz tone gives 500 peaks near 255 and a complete 10 s decode", async () => {
    const {metadata, summary} = await decode("tone-220-10s.wav");

    assert.equal(summary.peaks.length, 500);
    assert.equal(summary.peaksPerSecond, 50);
    assert.equal(summary.decodedSeconds, 10);
    assert.equal(Math.min(...summary.peaks) >= 250, true);
    assert.equal(isDecodeComplete(summary.decodedSeconds, metadata.durationSeconds), true);
  });

  it("decodes mono and short files with the right bucket count", async () => {
    assert.equal((await decode("mono-440.wav")).summary.peaks.length, 111);
    assert.equal((await decode("tone-880-0.4s.wav")).summary.peaks.length, 20);
  });

  it("rejects with the abort reason when the signal is already aborted", async () => {
    const controller = new AbortController();

    controller.abort();
    await assert.rejects(decode("tone-220-10s.wav", {signal: controller.signal}), (error) => error.name === "AbortError");
  });

  it("an MP3 cannot be decoded in Node (no WebCodecs): AudioDecodeError, not a crash", {skip: typeof AudioDecoder !== "undefined"}, async () => {
    await assert.rejects(decode("tone-220-10s.mp3"), (error) => error instanceof AudioDecodeError && error.name === "AudioDecodeError");
  });
});
```

`tests/harness/vite.harness.config.mts` - replace the file with:

```ts
import {readFile} from "node:fs/promises";
import path from "node:path";
import {defineConfig, type Plugin} from "vite";

// Vite's dev server treats an extensionless URL as a JS module and tries to
// transform it, which would fail for the extensionless WebP fixture (and would
// mangle the audio fixtures). This harness-only config serves the committed
// local-asset and local-audio fixtures as raw bytes, before Vite's own
// middleware runs. It is not part of the production build.
const RAW_FIXTURE_DIRECTORIES = [
  {prefix: "/tests/fixtures/local-assets/", segments: ["tests", "fixtures", "local-assets"]},
  {prefix: "/tests/fixtures/local-audio/", segments: ["tests", "fixtures", "local-audio"]},
];
const FIXTURE_NAME = /^[A-Za-z0-9][A-Za-z0-9._-]*$/;

const serveRawFixtures = (root: string): Plugin => ({
  name: "serve-raw-local-asset-fixtures",
  configureServer(server) {
    server.middlewares.use(async (request, response, next) => {
      const pathname = (request.url ?? "").split("?")[0];
      const directory = RAW_FIXTURE_DIRECTORIES.find((entry) =>
        pathname.startsWith(entry.prefix),
      );

      if (directory === undefined) {
        next();
        return;
      }

      const name = pathname.slice(directory.prefix.length);

      if (!FIXTURE_NAME.test(name)) {
        next();
        return;
      }

      try {
        const bytes = await readFile(path.join(root, ...directory.segments, name));

        response.setHeader("content-type", "application/octet-stream");
        response.setHeader("cache-control", "no-store");
        response.end(bytes);
      } catch {
        next();
      }
    });
  },
});

const root = process.cwd();

export default defineConfig({
  root,
  base: "/",
  plugins: [serveRawFixtures(root)],
});
```

```ts
  /* ---- real audio decoding (AUDIO-003) ---- */

  /**
   * Fetches a committed audio fixture, inspects it and runs the REAL
   * decodeAudioPeaks (WebCodecs) on it. Never throws: failures are returned.
   */
  async decodeAudioFixture(name: string) {
    const response = await fetch(`/tests/fixtures/local-audio/${name}`);

    if (!response.ok) {
      throw new Error(`Fixture ${name} answered ${response.status}`);
    }

    const bytes = new Uint8Array(await response.arrayBuffer());
    const inspected = await inspectAudio(bytes);

    if (!inspected.ok) {
      return {kind: "rejected" as const, message: inspected.message};
    }

    try {
      const summary = await decodeAudioPeaks(
        new Blob([bytes as BlobPart]),
        inspected.metadata,
      );

      return {
        kind: "decoded" as const,
        metadata: inspected.metadata,
        decodedSeconds: summary.decodedSeconds,
        peaksLength: summary.peaks.length,
        peaksPerSecond: summary.peaksPerSecond,
        minPeak: Math.min(...summary.peaks),
        maxPeak: Math.max(...summary.peaks),
        complete: isDecodeComplete(
          summary.decodedSeconds,
          inspected.metadata.durationSeconds,
        ),
      };
    } catch (error) {
      return {kind: "decode-failed" as const, error: describeError(error)};
    }
  },

  async aacDecodeSupported(): Promise<boolean> {
    if (typeof AudioDecoder === "undefined") {
      return false;
    }

    const support = await AudioDecoder.isConfigSupported({
      codec: "mp4a.40.2",
      sampleRate: 44_100,
      numberOfChannels: 2,
    });

    return support.supported === true;
  },
```

`src/audio/decodeCheck.ts`:

```ts
import {AUDIO_TRUNCATION_TOLERANCE_SECONDS} from "../localAssets/limits.ts";

/**
 * D-9, the one truncation rule: a decode is complete when something was
 * decoded and it is at most `min(tolerance, duration / 2)` shorter than the
 * duration the container declares. The `> 0` term and the half-duration cap
 * keep short clips honest (a 0.12 s clip that decodes to nothing never passes).
 */
export const isDecodeComplete = (
  decodedSeconds: number,
  durationSeconds: number,
): boolean =>
  decodedSeconds > 0 &&
  decodedSeconds >=
    durationSeconds -
      Math.min(AUDIO_TRUNCATION_TOLERANCE_SECONDS, durationSeconds / 2);
```

`src/web/assetLibrary/audioPeaks.ts`:

```ts
import {ALL_FORMATS, AudioSampleSink, BlobSource, Input} from "mediabunny";
import {
  MAX_WAVEFORM_PEAKS,
  WAVEFORM_PEAKS_PER_SECOND,
} from "../../audio/constants.ts";
import type {AudioMetadata} from "../../localAssets/audioInspection.ts";

export type DecodedAudioSummary = {
  decodedSeconds: number;
  peaks: Uint8Array;
  peaksPerSecond: number;
};

/** The decoder could not read the file (unsupported codec in this browser, or a corrupt stream). */
export class AudioDecodeError extends Error {
  override name = "AudioDecodeError";
  cause?: unknown;

  constructor(message: string, options?: {cause?: unknown}) {
    super(message);

    if (options?.cause !== undefined) {
      this.cause = options.cause;
    }
  }
}

/** `min(ceil(durationSeconds x WAVEFORM_PEAKS_PER_SECOND), MAX_WAVEFORM_PEAKS)`, at least 1. */
export const peakBucketCount = (durationSeconds: number): number =>
  Math.max(
    1,
    Math.min(
      Math.ceil(durationSeconds * WAVEFORM_PEAKS_PER_SECOND),
      MAX_WAVEFORM_PEAKS,
    ),
  );

/**
 * Streaming max-abs peaks. Buckets are laid over the container duration, so
 * `peaksPerSecond = buckets / durationSeconds` places every peak in time; a
 * frame is assigned by its timestamp and out-of-range frames clamp to the ends.
 */
export const createPeakAccumulator = (durationSeconds: number) => {
  const buckets = peakBucketCount(durationSeconds);
  const maxima = new Float32Array(buckets);
  let decodedFrames = 0;
  let decodedRate = 0;

  return {
    /** `planes[c][i]` is frame `i` of channel `c`; the first frame starts at `startSeconds`. */
    push(
      startSeconds: number,
      sampleRate: number,
      planes: readonly Float32Array[],
    ): void {
      const frames = planes[0]?.length ?? 0;
      const framesPerBucket = (durationSeconds * sampleRate) / buckets;
      const firstFrame = startSeconds * sampleRate;

      for (let index = 0; index < frames; index += 1) {
        const bucket = Math.min(
          buckets - 1,
          Math.max(0, Math.floor((firstFrame + index) / framesPerBucket)),
        );
        let peak = maxima[bucket];

        for (const plane of planes) {
          const magnitude = Math.abs(plane[index]);

          if (magnitude > peak) {
            peak = magnitude;
          }
        }

        maxima[bucket] = peak;
      }

      decodedFrames += frames;
      decodedRate = sampleRate;
    },

    finish(): DecodedAudioSummary {
      const peaks = new Uint8Array(buckets);

      for (let index = 0; index < buckets; index += 1) {
        peaks[index] = Math.min(255, Math.round(255 * maxima[index]));
      }

      return {
        decodedSeconds: decodedRate > 0 ? decodedFrames / decodedRate : 0,
        peaks,
        peaksPerSecond: buckets / durationSeconds,
      };
    },
  };
};

/**
 * Decodes the whole file once (mediabunny, WebCodecs in the browser) to compute
 * the waveform peaks and the decoded length. Throws AudioDecodeError when the
 * browser cannot decode the file, and rethrows an abort.
 */
export const decodeAudioPeaks = async (
  blob: Blob,
  metadata: AudioMetadata,
  deps: {signal?: AbortSignal} = {},
): Promise<DecodedAudioSummary> => {
  const input = new Input({source: new BlobSource(blob), formats: ALL_FORMATS});

  try {
    const track = await input.getPrimaryAudioTrack();

    if (track === null || !(await track.canDecode())) {
      throw new AudioDecodeError("This browser cannot decode this audio file.");
    }

    const accumulator = createPeakAccumulator(metadata.durationSeconds);
    const sink = new AudioSampleSink(track);

    for await (const sample of sink.samples()) {
      try {
        deps.signal?.throwIfAborted();

        const planes: Float32Array[] = [];

        for (let channel = 0; channel < sample.numberOfChannels; channel += 1) {
          const plane = new Float32Array(sample.numberOfFrames);

          sample.copyTo(plane, {planeIndex: channel, format: "f32-planar"});
          planes.push(plane);
        }

        accumulator.push(sample.timestamp, sample.sampleRate, planes);
      } finally {
        sample.close();
      }
    }

    return accumulator.finish();
  } catch (error) {
    if (deps.signal?.aborted === true || error instanceof AudioDecodeError) {
      throw error;
    }

    throw new AudioDecodeError("The audio file could not be decoded.", {
      cause: error,
    });
  } finally {
    input.dispose();
  }
};
```

### Task 5: Payload metadata kinds (records, integrity, post-lock check)

`PayloadMetadata` becomes an image-or-audio union and every reader of it learns both kinds: the stored-record decoders, the integrity verification and the comparison used after the shared lock. Image behaviour is untouched: an image record has no `kind` field before and after (a stored `kind: "image"` is tolerated and dropped on read).

**Files:**
- Modify: `src/localAssets/readiness.ts` (types, `evaluateLocalAssetBudget`, `describeBudgetFailure`), `src/web/assetLibrary/records.ts`, `src/web/assetLibrary/integrity.ts`, `src/web/localAssetState.ts` (remove its private `samePayload`, import the shared one), `src/web/assetLibrary/importAsset.ts` (type of `payloadMeta` becomes `ImagePayloadMetadata`), `src/web/assetLibrary/store.ts` (the `import` mutation's `payloadMeta` type becomes `ImagePayloadMetadata`; `getPayloadMeta` keeps `PayloadMetadata`)
- Create: `tests/audio-library.test.mjs`
- Modify tests: `tests/asset-integrity.test.mjs` (extend), `tests/local-asset-readiness.test.mjs` (extend)

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `AudioContainer`, `AudioCodec`, `AudioMimeType`, `audioContainers`, `audioCodecs`, `isAcceptedAudioCombination`, `audioMimeTypeFor`, `inspectAudio` (Task 2); `maxLocalAssetBytes` (Task 3); constants `MAX_AUDIO_DURATION_SECONDS`, `MIN_AUDIO_SAMPLE_RATE`, `MAX_AUDIO_SAMPLE_RATE`, `MAX_WAVEFORM_PEAKS`.
- Produces (`src/localAssets/readiness.ts`):
  - `type ImagePayloadMetadata = {kind?: "image"; mimeType: "image/png" | "image/jpeg" | "image/webp"; byteSize: number; width: number; height: number}`
  - `type AudioPayloadMetadata = {kind: "audio"; mimeType: AudioMimeType; byteSize: number; container: AudioContainer; codec: AudioCodec; durationSeconds: number; sampleRate: number; channels: 1 | 2}`
  - `type PayloadMetadata = ImagePayloadMetadata | AudioPayloadMetadata`, `type PayloadKind = "image" | "audio"`, `isAudioPayload(meta: PayloadMetadata): meta is AudioPayloadMetadata`, `payloadKindOf(meta: PayloadMetadata): PayloadKind`, `maxPayloadBytes(kind: PayloadKind): number`.
  - `LocalAssetBudgetResult` failure `exceeded` may now contain `"metadata"`; `evaluateLocalAssetBudget` fails closed on audio metadata.
- Produces (`src/web/assetLibrary/records.ts`): `type WaveformRecord = {peaksPerSecond: number; peaks: Uint8Array}`, `decodeWaveform(digest: string, value: unknown): Decoded<WaveformRecord>`, `payloadMatchesCategory(meta: PayloadMetadata, category: LocalAssetCategory): boolean`; `decodePayloadMeta(digest, value)` returns `Decoded<PayloadMetadata>` (image value unchanged: `{mimeType, byteSize, width, height}`).
- Produces (`src/web/assetLibrary/integrity.ts`): `type VerifiedPayload = ({digest: string} & ImagePayloadMetadata) | ({digest: string} & AudioPayloadMetadata)`, `samePayload(verified: VerifiedPayload, current: PayloadMetadata): boolean` (exported; kind-discriminated, audio fields compared exactly).
