`tests/audio-library.test.mjs` (new; later tasks append to it):

```js
import assert from "node:assert/strict";
import {describe, it} from "node:test";
import {
  MAX_AUDIO_DURATION_SECONDS,
  MAX_LOCAL_ASSET_BYTES,
  MAX_LOCAL_AUDIO_BYTES,
} from "../src/localAssets/limits.ts";
import {payloadKindOf} from "../src/localAssets/readiness.ts";
import {buildLocalAssetRef} from "../src/localAssets/refs.ts";
import {MAX_WAVEFORM_PEAKS} from "../src/audio/constants.ts";
import {
  decodeAssetRow,
  decodePayloadMeta,
  decodeWaveform,
  payloadMatchesCategory,
} from "../src/web/assetLibrary/records.ts";

const digestOf = (n) => n.toString(16).padStart(64, "0");
const DIGEST = digestOf(1);
const audioRef = (n) => buildLocalAssetRef("audio", digestOf(n));
const poseRef = (n) => buildLocalAssetRef("pose", digestOf(n));
const CREATED_AT = "2026-01-02T03:04:05.000Z";
const validRow = {label: "Voice", originalFilename: "voice.mp3", createdAt: CREATED_AT};
const imageMeta = {mimeType: "image/png", byteSize: 100, width: 10, height: 20};
const audioMeta = {
  kind: "audio",
  mimeType: "audio/mpeg",
  byteSize: 36_381,
  container: "mp3",
  codec: "mp3",
  durationSeconds: 2.2465,
  sampleRate: 44_100,
  channels: 2,
};
const MIB = 1024 * 1024;

describe("decodePayloadMeta: image records are unchanged", () => {
  it("returns the v0.3 shape without a kind field", () => {
    assert.deepEqual(decodePayloadMeta(DIGEST, imageMeta), {status: "present", value: imageMeta});
    assert.equal("kind" in decodePayloadMeta(DIGEST, imageMeta).value, false);
    assert.equal(payloadKindOf(decodePayloadMeta(DIGEST, imageMeta).value), "image");
  });

  it("tolerates a stored kind: image and drops it", () => {
    assert.deepEqual(decodePayloadMeta(DIGEST, {...imageMeta, kind: "image"}), {status: "present", value: imageMeta});
  });

  it("rejects an unknown kind", () => {
    for (const kind of ["Audio", "video", "", 3, null]) {
      assert.equal(decodePayloadMeta(DIGEST, {...imageMeta, kind}).status, "corrupt", String(kind));
    }
  });

  it("keeps the 25 MiB image ceiling: a 30 MiB image record is corrupt", () => {
    assert.equal(decodePayloadMeta(DIGEST, {...imageMeta, byteSize: MAX_LOCAL_ASSET_BYTES}).status, "present");
    assert.equal(decodePayloadMeta(DIGEST, {...imageMeta, byteSize: 30 * MIB}).status, "corrupt");
  });
});

describe("decodePayloadMeta: audio records", () => {
  it("decodes a valid record exactly", () => {
    assert.deepEqual(decodePayloadMeta(DIGEST, audioMeta), {status: "present", value: audioMeta});
    assert.equal(payloadKindOf(audioMeta), "audio");
  });

  it("accepts every accepted (container, codec, mime) triple", () => {
    for (const [container, codec, mimeType] of [
      ["mp3", "mp3", "audio/mpeg"], ["wave", "pcm", "audio/wav"], ["mp4", "aac", "audio/mp4"], ["ogg", "opus", "audio/ogg"],
    ]) {
      assert.equal(decodePayloadMeta(DIGEST, {...audioMeta, container, codec, mimeType}).status, "present", container);
    }
  });

  it("accepts a 30 MiB audio record and rejects 50 MiB + 1", () => {
    assert.equal(decodePayloadMeta(DIGEST, {...audioMeta, byteSize: 30 * MIB}).status, "present");
    assert.equal(decodePayloadMeta(DIGEST, {...audioMeta, byteSize: MAX_LOCAL_AUDIO_BYTES}).status, "present");
    assert.equal(decodePayloadMeta(DIGEST, {...audioMeta, byteSize: MAX_LOCAL_AUDIO_BYTES + 1}).status, "corrupt");
  });

  it("rejects out-of-range or mistyped fields", () => {
    const bad = [
      {mimeType: "audio/ogg"}, // not the container's MIME type
      {mimeType: "image/png"},
      {container: "flac"}, {container: 7},
      {codec: "vorbis"}, {codec: "aac"}, // not an accepted pair for mp3
      {byteSize: 0}, {byteSize: -1}, {byteSize: 1.5}, {byteSize: Number.NaN}, {byteSize: "36381"},
      {durationSeconds: 0}, {durationSeconds: -1}, {durationSeconds: Number.NaN}, {durationSeconds: Number.POSITIVE_INFINITY},
      {durationSeconds: MAX_AUDIO_DURATION_SECONDS + 0.001}, {durationSeconds: "2.2"},
      {sampleRate: 7_999}, {sampleRate: 96_001}, {sampleRate: 44_100.5}, {sampleRate: Number.NaN},
      {channels: 0}, {channels: 3}, {channels: 1.5}, {channels: -1}, {channels: Number.NaN},
    ];

    for (const patch of bad) {
      assert.equal(decodePayloadMeta(DIGEST, {...audioMeta, ...patch}).status, "corrupt", JSON.stringify(patch));
    }
  });

  it("is strict: any extra key, including identity fields, is corrupt", () => {
    for (const extra of [{width: 10}, {thumbnail: 1}, {digest: DIGEST}, {digest: digestOf(2)}, {ref: audioRef(1)}, {ref: audioRef(2)}, {category: "audio"}]) {
      assert.equal(decodePayloadMeta(DIGEST, {...audioMeta, ...extra}).status, "corrupt", JSON.stringify(extra));
    }

    const missing = {...audioMeta};

    delete missing.channels;
    assert.equal(decodePayloadMeta(DIGEST, missing).status, "corrupt");
  });

  it("maps undefined to absent and a bad key to corrupt", () => {
    assert.deepEqual(decodePayloadMeta(DIGEST, undefined), {status: "absent"});
    assert.equal(decodePayloadMeta("not-a-digest", audioMeta).status, "corrupt");
  });
});

describe("payloadMatchesCategory: an audio row must point at audio metadata and an image row at image metadata", () => {
  it("matches by kind", () => {
    assert.equal(payloadMatchesCategory(audioMeta, "audio"), true);
    assert.equal(payloadMatchesCategory(audioMeta, "pose"), false);
    assert.equal(payloadMatchesCategory(audioMeta, "background"), false);
    assert.equal(payloadMatchesCategory(imageMeta, "pose"), true);
    assert.equal(payloadMatchesCategory(imageMeta, "background"), true);
    assert.equal(payloadMatchesCategory(imageMeta, "audio"), false);
  });
});

describe("decodeWaveform", () => {
  const peaks = Uint8Array.from([0, 10, 255]);

  it("decodes exactly {peaksPerSecond, peaks}", () => {
    assert.deepEqual(decodeWaveform(DIGEST, {peaksPerSecond: 50, peaks}), {status: "present", value: {peaksPerSecond: 50, peaks}});
    assert.deepEqual(decodeWaveform(DIGEST, undefined), {status: "absent"});
  });

  it("rejects any extra key, including a digest of the right or the wrong value (the key is the only identity)", () => {
    for (const extra of [{digest: DIGEST}, {digest: digestOf(2)}, {ref: audioRef(1)}, {category: "audio"}, {other: 1}]) {
      assert.equal(decodeWaveform(DIGEST, {peaksPerSecond: 50, peaks, ...extra}).status, "corrupt", JSON.stringify(extra));
    }
  });

  it("rejects bad peaksPerSecond and bad peaks", () => {
    for (const peaksPerSecond of [0, -1, Number.NaN, Number.POSITIVE_INFINITY, "50", undefined]) {
      assert.equal(decodeWaveform(DIGEST, {peaksPerSecond, peaks}).status, "corrupt", String(peaksPerSecond));
    }

    for (const bad of [[], [1, 2, 3], new Uint8Array(0), new Uint16Array(3), new Float32Array(3), "abc", null, new Uint8Array(MAX_WAVEFORM_PEAKS + 1)]) {
      assert.equal(decodeWaveform(DIGEST, {peaksPerSecond: 50, peaks: bad}).status, "corrupt");
    }

    assert.equal(decodeWaveform(DIGEST, {peaksPerSecond: 50, peaks: new Uint8Array(MAX_WAVEFORM_PEAKS)}).status, "present");
    assert.equal(decodeWaveform(DIGEST, {peaks}).status, "corrupt");
    assert.equal(decodeWaveform(DIGEST, {peaksPerSecond: 50}).status, "corrupt");
    assert.equal(decodeWaveform(DIGEST, null).status, "corrupt");
    assert.equal(decodeWaveform("nope", {peaksPerSecond: 50, peaks}).status, "corrupt");
  });
});

describe("decodeAssetRow for audio keys (category-correct)", () => {
  it("decodes a canonical audio row and tolerates only identity fields equal to what the key implies", () => {
    assert.deepEqual(decodeAssetRow(audioRef(1), validRow), {status: "present", value: validRow});
    assert.deepEqual(decodeAssetRow(audioRef(1), {...validRow, ref: audioRef(1), category: "audio", digest: DIGEST}), {status: "present", value: validRow});

    for (const extra of [{category: "pose"}, {category: "background"}, {ref: poseRef(1)}, {ref: audioRef(2)}, {digest: digestOf(2)}]) {
      assert.equal(decodeAssetRow(audioRef(1), {...validRow, ...extra}).status, "corrupt", JSON.stringify(extra));
    }
  });
});

test("the budget fails closed on non-image metadata and names it", () => {
  const audio = {
    kind: "audio", mimeType: "audio/wav", byteSize: 1000, container: "wave", codec: "pcm",
    durationSeconds: 1, sampleRate: 48_000, channels: 2,
  };
  const result = evaluateLocalAssetBudget([usageOf(1)], new Map([[digestOf(1), audio]]));

  assert.equal(result.ok, false);
  assert.deepEqual(result.exceeded, ["metadata"]);
  assert.equal(describeBudgetFailure(result), "A local asset's stored details do not match an image.");
});
```

Extend `tests/asset-integrity.test.mjs` (append; add the imports `inspectAudio`, `audioMimeTypeFor` from `../src/localAssets/audioInspection.ts`, `samePayload` from `../src/web/assetLibrary/integrity.ts`, `fixtureBytes` from `./helpers/audioBytes.mjs`, and `MAX_LOCAL_AUDIO_BYTES`):

```js
const audioMetaFor = async (bytes, overrides = {}) => {
  const inspected = await inspectAudio(bytes);

  assert.equal(inspected.ok, true);

  const {container, codec, durationSeconds, sampleRate, channels} = inspected.metadata;

  return {
    kind: "audio",
    mimeType: audioMimeTypeFor(container),
    byteSize: bytes.length,
    container,
    codec,
    durationSeconds,
    sampleRate,
    channels,
    ...overrides,
  };
};

const putAudio = async (store, bytes, overrides = {}) => {
  const digest = await sha256Hex(bytes);
  const meta = await audioMetaFor(bytes, overrides);

  store.setRaw("payloadMeta", digest, meta);
  store.setRaw("blobs", digest, {blob: new Blob([bytes], {type: "application/octet-stream"})});

  return {digest, meta};
};

test("an audio payload verifies: SHA-256, size, and inspectAudio of the bytes match the metadata", async () => {
  for (const name of ["tone-440-2.2s.wav", "tone-440-2.2s.mp3", "tone-440-2.2s.m4a", "tone-440-2.2s.opus.ogg"]) {
    const store = createMemoryAssetStore();
    const {digest, meta} = await putAudio(store, fixtureBytes(name));
    const result = await verifyPayload(store, digest);

    assert.equal(result.ok, true, `${name}: ${result.detail}`);
    assert.deepEqual(result.payload, {digest, ...meta});
    assert.equal(result.blob.type, meta.mimeType);
  }
});

test("an audio payload with altered bytes is corrupt", async () => {
  const store = createMemoryAssetStore();
  const original = fixtureBytes("tone-440-2.2s.wav");
  const {digest} = await putAudio(store, original);
  const altered = Uint8Array.from(original);

  altered[altered.length - 5] ^= 0xff;
  store.setRaw("blobs", digest, {blob: new Blob([altered])});

  const result = await verifyPayload(store, digest);

  assert.equal(result.ok, false);
  assert.equal(result.reason, "corrupt");
  assert.match(result.detail, /SHA-256/u);
});

test("each altered audio metadata field is corrupt, and a duration within 1 ms is accepted", async () => {
  const bytes = fixtureBytes("tone-440-2.2s.mp3");
  const actual = await audioMetaFor(bytes);
  const cases = [
    [{durationSeconds: actual.durationSeconds + 0.01}, /duration/u],
    [{sampleRate: 48_000}, /sample rate|channels/u],
    [{channels: 1}, /sample rate|channels/u],
    [{codec: "aac", container: "mp4", mimeType: "audio/mp4"}, /format/u],
    [{mimeType: "audio/ogg"}, /format|mimeType/u],
  ];

  for (const [patch, expected] of cases) {
    const store = createMemoryAssetStore();
    const {digest} = await putAudio(store, bytes, patch);
    const result = await verifyPayload(store, digest);

    assert.equal(result.ok, false, JSON.stringify(patch));
    assert.match(result.detail, expected, JSON.stringify(patch));
  }

  const store = createMemoryAssetStore();
  const {digest} = await putAudio(store, bytes, {durationSeconds: actual.durationSeconds + 0.0005});

  assert.equal((await verifyPayload(store, digest)).ok, true);
});

test("PNG bytes under audio metadata are corrupt, never decoded", async () => {
  const store = createMemoryAssetStore();
  const bytes = pngBytes();
  const digest = await sha256Hex(bytes);

  store.setRaw("payloadMeta", digest, {
    kind: "audio", mimeType: "audio/wav", byteSize: bytes.length, container: "wave", codec: "pcm",
    durationSeconds: 1, sampleRate: 48_000, channels: 2,
  });
  store.setRaw("blobs", digest, {blob: new Blob([bytes])});

  const result = await verifyPayload(store, digest);

  assert.equal(result.ok, false);
  assert.match(result.detail, /not a supported audio file/u);
});

test("the byte ceiling is per kind: 30 MiB passes the size gate for audio and fails it for an image", async () => {
  const size = 30 * 1024 * 1024;
  const audioDigest = "a".repeat(64);
  const imageDigest = "b".repeat(64);
  const audioBlob = fakeBlob({size, bytes: new Uint8Array([1, 2, 3])});
  const imageBlob = fakeBlob({size, bytes: new Uint8Array([1, 2, 3])});

  // The decoders accept a 30 MiB audio record and reject a 30 MiB image record.
  const store = createMemoryAssetStore();

  store.setRaw("payloadMeta", audioDigest, {
    kind: "audio", mimeType: "audio/wav", byteSize: size, container: "wave", codec: "pcm",
    durationSeconds: 150, sampleRate: 48_000, channels: 2,
  });
  store.setRaw("blobs", audioDigest, {blob: audioBlob.blob});
  store.setRaw("payloadMeta", imageDigest, {mimeType: "image/png", byteSize: size, width: 4, height: 3});
  store.setRaw("blobs", imageDigest, {blob: imageBlob.blob});

  const audio = await verifyPayload(store, audioDigest);
  const image = await verifyPayload(store, imageDigest);

  // Audio got past the size gate (it reads the stub bytes and fails the digest check instead).
  assert.equal(audio.ok, false);
  assert.match(audio.detail, /SHA-256/u);
  assert.equal(audioBlob.state.calls, 1);
  // The image record is rejected by its decoder, and its bytes are never read.
  assert.equal(image.ok, false);
  assert.match(image.detail, /payload metadata is corrupt/u);
  assert.equal(imageBlob.state.calls, 0);
  assert.equal(MAX_LOCAL_AUDIO_BYTES, 50 * 1024 * 1024);
});

test("samePayload is discriminated by kind and compares audio fields exactly", () => {
  const image = {digest: "d", mimeType: "image/png", byteSize: 10, width: 2, height: 3};
  const audio = {
    digest: "d", kind: "audio", mimeType: "audio/wav", byteSize: 10, container: "wave", codec: "pcm",
    durationSeconds: 2.2, sampleRate: 48_000, channels: 2,
  };
  const {digest: _imageDigest, ...imageMetaOnly} = image;
  const {digest: _audioDigest, ...audioMetaOnly} = audio;

  assert.equal(samePayload(image, imageMetaOnly), true);
  assert.equal(samePayload(audio, audioMetaOnly), true);
  assert.equal(samePayload(image, {...imageMetaOnly, kind: "image"}), true);
  // Different kinds fail closed, in both directions.
  assert.equal(samePayload(image, audioMetaOnly), false);
  assert.equal(samePayload(audio, imageMetaOnly), false);

  for (const patch of [
    {mimeType: "audio/mpeg"}, {byteSize: 11}, {container: "mp3"}, {codec: "mp3"},
    {sampleRate: 44_100}, {channels: 1}, {durationSeconds: 2.2000001},
  ]) {
    assert.equal(samePayload(audio, {...audioMetaOnly, ...patch}), false, JSON.stringify(patch));
  }

  for (const patch of [{mimeType: "image/jpeg"}, {byteSize: 11}, {width: 3}, {height: 4}]) {
    assert.equal(samePayload(image, {...imageMetaOnly, ...patch}), false, JSON.stringify(patch));
  }
});

test("a cached audio payload is served without reading the blob, and re-verified after its metadata changed", async () => {
  const store = createMemoryAssetStore();
  const cache = new IntegrityCache();
  const {digest, meta} = await putAudio(store, fixtureBytes("tone-440-2.2s.wav"));

  assert.equal((await verifyPayloadsSequentially(store, [digest], cache)).get(digest).ok, true);
  assert.equal(store.reads.blobs, 1);
  assert.equal((await verifyPayloadsSequentially(store, [digest], cache)).get(digest).ok, true);
  assert.equal(store.reads.blobs, 1);

  store.setRaw("payloadMeta", digest, {...meta, durationSeconds: meta.durationSeconds + 0.5});

  const result = (await verifyPayloadsSequentially(store, [digest], cache)).get(digest);

  assert.equal(result.ok, false);
  assert.equal(result.reason, "corrupt");
  assert.equal(store.reads.blobs, 2);
});
```

In `src/localAssets/readiness.ts` replace the `PayloadMetadata` type with:

```ts
import type {
  AudioCodec,
  AudioContainer,
  AudioMimeType,
} from "./audioInspection.ts";
import {maxLocalAssetBytes} from "./limits.ts";
```

(merge these into the existing imports; the first is type-only so readiness stays free of mediabunny at runtime) and:

```ts
export type ImagePayloadMetadata = {
  /** Absent in every record this build writes and in every v0.3 record; a stored "image" is tolerated and dropped on read. */
  kind?: "image";
  mimeType: "image/png" | "image/jpeg" | "image/webp";
  byteSize: number;
  width: number;
  height: number;
};

export type AudioPayloadMetadata = {
  kind: "audio";
  mimeType: AudioMimeType;
  byteSize: number;
  container: AudioContainer;
  codec: AudioCodec;
  durationSeconds: number;
  sampleRate: number;
  channels: 1 | 2;
};

export type PayloadMetadata = ImagePayloadMetadata | AudioPayloadMetadata;
export type PayloadKind = "image" | "audio";

export const isAudioPayload = (
  meta: PayloadMetadata,
): meta is AudioPayloadMetadata => meta.kind === "audio";

export const payloadKindOf = (meta: PayloadMetadata): PayloadKind =>
  meta.kind === "audio" ? "audio" : "image";

/** The per-payload ceiling (D-8): images 25 MiB, audio 50 MiB. */
export const maxPayloadBytes = (kind: PayloadKind): number =>
  maxLocalAssetBytes(kind === "audio" ? "audio" : "pose");
```

3. In `decodePayloadMeta`, after the identity check and before `const {mimeType, byteSize, width, height} = value;` insert:

```ts

```

and change the image ceiling `byteSize > MAX_LOCAL_ASSET_BYTES` to `byteSize > maxPayloadBytes("image")`. The image return value stays `{mimeType, byteSize, width, height}` (no `kind`); its cast `mimeType as PayloadMetadata["mimeType"]` (now the union of image and audio types) becomes `mimeType as ImagePayloadMetadata["mimeType"]`.
4. Add (above `decodePayloadMeta`):

```ts
const AUDIO_PAYLOAD_KEYS = [
  "byteSize",
  "channels",
  "codec",
  "container",
  "durationSeconds",
  "kind",
  "mimeType",
  "sampleRate",
];

const decodeAudioPayloadMeta = (
  value: Record<string, unknown>,
): Decoded<PayloadMetadata> => {
  const keys = Object.keys(value).sort();

  if (
    keys.length !== AUDIO_PAYLOAD_KEYS.length ||
    keys.some((key, index) => key !== AUDIO_PAYLOAD_KEYS[index])
  ) {
    return corrupt("audio payload metadata has unexpected fields");
  }

  const {mimeType, byteSize, container, codec, durationSeconds, sampleRate, channels} = value;

  if (
    typeof container !== "string" ||
    !(audioContainers as readonly string[]).includes(container)
  ) {
    return corrupt("unknown audio container");
  }

  if (typeof codec !== "string" || !(audioCodecs as readonly string[]).includes(codec)) {
    return corrupt("unknown audio codec");
  }

  if (!isAcceptedAudioCombination(container as AudioContainer, codec as AudioCodec)) {
    return corrupt("audio container and codec are not an accepted pair");
  }

  if (mimeType !== audioMimeTypeFor(container as AudioContainer)) {
    return corrupt("mimeType does not match the audio container");
  }

  if (!isPositiveInteger(byteSize) || byteSize > maxPayloadBytes("audio")) {
    return corrupt("byteSize is out of range");
  }

  if (
    typeof durationSeconds !== "number" ||
    !Number.isFinite(durationSeconds) ||
    durationSeconds <= 0 ||
    durationSeconds > MAX_AUDIO_DURATION_SECONDS
  ) {
    return corrupt("durationSeconds is out of range");
  }

  if (
    typeof sampleRate !== "number" ||
    !Number.isInteger(sampleRate) ||
    sampleRate < MIN_AUDIO_SAMPLE_RATE ||
    sampleRate > MAX_AUDIO_SAMPLE_RATE
  ) {
    return corrupt("sampleRate is out of range");
  }

  if (channels !== 1 && channels !== 2) {
    return corrupt("channels must be 1 or 2");
  }

  const decoded: AudioPayloadMetadata = {
    kind: "audio",
    mimeType: mimeType as AudioPayloadMetadata["mimeType"],
    byteSize,
    container: container as AudioContainer,
    codec: codec as AudioCodec,
    durationSeconds,
    sampleRate,
    channels,
  };

  return present(decoded);
};
```

5. Append at the end of the file:

```ts
/** The waveform store: the key is the only identity, so any extra key (even a matching `digest`) is invalid. */
export const decodeWaveform = (
  digest: string,
  value: unknown,
): Decoded<WaveformRecord> => {
  if (value === undefined) {
    return absent;
  }

  if (!SHA256_HEX_PATTERN.test(digest)) {
    return corrupt("key is not a sha256 digest");
  }

  if (!isRecord(value)) {
    return corrupt("waveform record is not an object");
  }

  const keys = Object.keys(value).sort();

  if (keys.length !== 2 || keys[0] !== "peaks" || keys[1] !== "peaksPerSecond") {
    return corrupt("waveform record has unexpected fields");
  }

  const {peaksPerSecond, peaks} = value;

  if (
    typeof peaksPerSecond !== "number" ||
    !Number.isFinite(peaksPerSecond) ||
    peaksPerSecond <= 0
  ) {
    return corrupt("peaksPerSecond is out of range");
  }

  if (
    !(peaks instanceof Uint8Array) ||
    peaks.length < 1 ||
    peaks.length > MAX_WAVEFORM_PEAKS
  ) {
    return corrupt("peaks is not a Uint8Array of 1 to MAX_WAVEFORM_PEAKS values");
  }

  return present({peaksPerSecond, peaks});
};

/** An audio row must point at audio metadata and an image row at image metadata. */
export const payloadMatchesCategory = (
  meta: PayloadMetadata,
  category: LocalAssetCategory,
): boolean => (payloadKindOf(meta) === "audio") === (category === "audio");
```

export type VerifiedPayload =

3. Above `checkBytes` add:

```ts
/** The metadata duration may differ from `inspectAudio` of the bytes by at most this much. */
const AUDIO_DURATION_TOLERANCE_SECONDS = 0.001;

/** Never decodes: compares the container metadata of the bytes with the stored record. */
const checkAudioBytes = async (
  bytes: Uint8Array,
  meta: AudioPayloadMetadata,
): Promise<string | null> => {
  const inspected = await inspectAudio(bytes);

  if (!inspected.ok) {
    return `stored bytes are not a supported audio file (${inspected.message})`;
  }

  const {metadata} = inspected;

  if (
    metadata.container !== meta.container ||
    metadata.codec !== meta.codec ||
    audioMimeTypeFor(metadata.container) !== meta.mimeType
  ) {
    return "stored bytes do not match the recorded format";
  }

  if (metadata.sampleRate !== meta.sampleRate || metadata.channels !== meta.channels) {
    return "stored bytes do not match the recorded sample rate or channels";
  }

  if (
    Math.abs(metadata.durationSeconds - meta.durationSeconds) >
    AUDIO_DURATION_TOLERANCE_SECONDS
  ) {
    return "stored bytes do not match the recorded duration";
  }

  return null;
};
```

4. In `checkBytes`, right after the SHA-256 check and before `const inspected = inspectImageBytes(bytes);` add `if (isAudioPayload(meta)) { return checkAudioBytes(bytes, meta); }`.
5. In `verifyPayload` replace the size gate `blob.size > MAX_LOCAL_ASSET_BYTES` with `blob.size > maxPayloadBytes(payloadKindOf(meta))` and replace the returned `payload` with `payload: {digest, ...meta},` (for images this equals the former `{digest, mimeType, byteSize, width, height}`).
6. Replace the private `samePayload` with the exported one:

```ts
/**
 * The comparison between a verified payload and the current stored metadata,
 * used for cache hits and again after the shared lock (render preparation).
 * Different kinds are never the same; audio fields are compared exactly (both
 * sides are stored values, so any difference is a change; the 1 ms tolerance
 * only applies when `inspectAudio` of the bytes is compared with the record).
 */
export const samePayload = (
  verified: VerifiedPayload,
  current: PayloadMetadata,
): boolean => {
  if (isAudioPayload(verified)) {
    return (
      isAudioPayload(current) &&
      verified.mimeType === current.mimeType &&
      verified.byteSize === current.byteSize &&
      verified.container === current.container &&
      verified.codec === current.codec &&
      verified.sampleRate === current.sampleRate &&
      verified.channels === current.channels &&
      verified.durationSeconds === current.durationSeconds
    );
  }

  return (
    !isAudioPayload(current) &&
    verified.mimeType === current.mimeType &&
    verified.byteSize === current.byteSize &&
    verified.width === current.width &&
    verified.height === current.height
  );
};
```

TypeScript will report the remaining places that read `.width`/`.height` of a `PayloadMetadata`; the only ones are in the files above.

### Task 6: Storage layer v2 (waveforms store, audio transactions, library reads)

IndexedDB version 2 adds the `waveforms` store; an audio import commits row + `payloadMeta` + blob + waveform in ONE transaction; deleting the last ref removes all of them; the "other category" of a delete is category-correct (audio has none, an image has the other image category). The library gets `readAudioMetadata`, `readWaveform` and `readAudioDurations`. Note on the spec: the current `onupgradeneeded` on `main` already creates a store only when `!database.objectStoreNames.contains(name)` (the spec describes an older shape); this task only adds `waveforms` to the list and proves the 1 to 2 upgrade in a real browser.

  - `src/web/assetLibrary/library.ts`: `LocalAssetEntry = {ref: LocalAssetRef; category: LocalAssetCategory; digest: string; row: Decoded<AssetRow>}`; `listLocalAssetPage(store, category: LocalAssetCategory, cursor?)`, `countLocalAssets(store, category: LocalAssetCategory)`, `renameLocalAsset(library, ref: LocalAssetRef, label, options?)`, `deleteLocalAsset(library, ref: LocalAssetRef, options?)`; new `readAudioMetadata(store: Pick<AssetLibraryStore, "getAssetRow" | "getPayloadMeta">, ref: LocalAudioRef): Promise<AudioPayloadMetadata | null>` (null when the row is absent or corrupt, the metadata is absent, corrupt or not audio), `readWaveform(store: Pick<AssetLibraryStore, "getWaveform">, digest: string): Promise<WaveformRecord | null>`, `readAudioDurations(store: Pick<AssetLibraryStore, "getAssetRow" | "getPayloadMeta">, refs: Iterable<LocalAudioRef>): Promise<AudioDurationMap>` (one entry per present ref with a finite duration greater than 0; absent refs are omitted; never lists the library).
  - `LocalAssetSourceMap = Readonly<Partial<Record<LocalAssetRef, LocalAssetSource>>>` (values unchanged, so the visual resolvers still accept only `url | static | pending`).
  - Memory store (`tests/helpers/memoryAssetStore.mjs`): `stores.waveforms`, `ALL_STORE_NAMES`, `getWaveform`, `import-audio`; `STORE_NAMES` and `reads` keep their four v0.3 entries (waveform reads are not counted).
  - Harness hooks: `seedAudio({fixture, label?})`, `inspectAudioRef(ref)`, `applyBrokenAudioImport(fixture)`, `seedVersion1Database()`, `getWaveform(digest)`.

```js
const backgroundRef = (n) => buildLocalAssetRef("background", digestOf(n));
const peaks = Uint8Array.from([1, 2, 3, 250]);
const audioMutation = (ref = audioRef(1), overrides = {}) => ({
  kind: "import-audio",
  ref,
  defaultLabel: "Voice",
  originalFilename: "voice.mp3",
  createdAt: CREATED_AT,
  payloadMeta: {...audioMeta},
  blob: new Blob([new Uint8Array([1, 2, 3])], {type: "audio/mpeg"}),
  waveform: {peaksPerSecond: 50, peaks},
  ...overrides,
});
const makeLibrary = (store = createMemoryAssetStore()) => {
  const posted = [];

  return {store, locks: createMemoryLockManager(), posted, channel: {post: (message) => posted.push(message)}};
};

describe("storage constants", () => {
  it("the database version is 2", () => {
    assert.equal(ASSET_DB_VERSION, 2);
  });
});

describe("deletionScope is category-correct", () => {
  it("audio has no other category and owns the waveform; an image's other category is the other image category", () => {
    assert.deepEqual(deletionScope("audio", DIGEST), {protectingRefs: [], derivedStores: ["waveforms"]});
    assert.deepEqual(deletionScope("pose", DIGEST), {protectingRefs: [backgroundRef(1)], derivedStores: ["thumbnails"]});
    assert.deepEqual(deletionScope("background", DIGEST), {protectingRefs: [poseRef(1)], derivedStores: ["thumbnails"]});
  });
});

describe("audio transactions (in-memory store)", () => {
  it("an audio import writes row, payloadMeta, blob and waveform in one apply and no thumbnail", async () => {
    const store = createMemoryAssetStore();
    const result = await store.apply(audioMutation());

    assert.deepEqual(result, {kind: "imported", ref: audioRef(1), created: true});
    assert.equal(store.applyCalls.length, 1);
    assert.deepEqual(store.getRaw("assets", audioRef(1)), {label: "Voice", originalFilename: "voice.mp3", createdAt: CREATED_AT});
    assert.deepEqual(store.getRaw("payloadMeta", DIGEST), audioMeta);
    assert.equal(store.hasRaw("blobs", DIGEST), true);
    assert.deepEqual(Object.keys(store.getRaw("waveforms", DIGEST)).sort(), ["peaks", "peaksPerSecond"]);
    assert.equal(store.stores.thumbnails.size, 0);
    assert.deepEqual((await store.getWaveform(DIGEST)).value.peaks, peaks);
    assert.equal((await store.getPayloadMeta(DIGEST)).value.kind, "audio");
  });

  it("re-importing keeps label and createdAt, refreshes the filename and repairs a missing waveform", async () => {
    const store = createMemoryAssetStore();

    await store.apply(audioMutation());
    await store.apply({kind: "rename", ref: audioRef(1), label: "Renamed"});
    store.deleteRaw("waveforms", DIGEST);

    const again = await store.apply(audioMutation(audioRef(1), {defaultLabel: "Fresh", originalFilename: "new.mp3", createdAt: "2030-01-01T00:00:00.000Z"}));

    assert.equal(again.created, false);
    assert.deepEqual(store.getRaw("assets", audioRef(1)), {label: "Renamed", originalFilename: "new.mp3", createdAt: CREATED_AT});
    assert.equal((await store.getWaveform(DIGEST)).status, "present");
  });

  it("an import kind that does not match its ref's category throws before writing anything", async () => {
    const store = createMemoryAssetStore();
    const before = store.snapshot();

    await assert.rejects(store.apply(audioMutation(poseRef(1))), /import-audio does not accept a pose ref/u);
    await assert.rejects(
      store.apply({kind: "import", ref: audioRef(1), defaultLabel: "x", originalFilename: "x", createdAt: CREATED_AT, payloadMeta: {mimeType: "image/png", byteSize: 1, width: 1, height: 1}, blob: new Blob([new Uint8Array([1])]), thumbnail: {blob: new Blob([new Uint8Array([1])]), mimeType: "image/png", width: 1, height: 1}}),
      /import does not accept a audio ref/u,
    );
    assert.deepEqual(store.snapshot(), before);
  });

  it("deleting the last audio ref removes payloadMeta, blob and waveform; other digests stay", async () => {
    const store = createMemoryAssetStore();

    await store.apply(audioMutation(audioRef(1)));
    await store.apply(audioMutation(audioRef(2), {payloadMeta: {...audioMeta, byteSize: 7}}));

    assert.deepEqual(await store.apply({kind: "delete", ref: audioRef(1)}), {kind: "deleted", ref: audioRef(1), payloadRemoved: true});
    assert.equal(store.hasRaw("payloadMeta", digestOf(1)), false);
    assert.equal(store.hasRaw("blobs", digestOf(1)), false);
    assert.equal(store.hasRaw("waveforms", digestOf(1)), false);
    assert.equal(store.hasRaw("waveforms", digestOf(2)), true);
    assert.deepEqual(await store.apply({kind: "delete", ref: audioRef(1)}), {kind: "not-found", ref: audioRef(1)});
  });

  it("an image delete never touches waveforms and an audio delete never touches thumbnails", async () => {
    const store = createMemoryAssetStore();
    const thumbnail = {blob: new Blob([new Uint8Array([1])]), mimeType: "image/png", width: 1, height: 1};

    store.setRaw("assets", poseRef(5), validRow);
    store.setRaw("payloadMeta", digestOf(5), imageMeta);
    store.setRaw("waveforms", digestOf(5), {peaksPerSecond: 50, peaks});
    await store.apply({kind: "delete", ref: poseRef(5)});
    assert.equal(store.hasRaw("waveforms", digestOf(5)), true);

    store.setRaw("assets", audioRef(6), validRow);
    store.setRaw("payloadMeta", digestOf(6), audioMeta);
    store.setRaw("thumbnails", digestOf(6), thumbnail);
    await store.apply({kind: "delete", ref: audioRef(6)});
    assert.equal(store.hasRaw("thumbnails", digestOf(6)), true);
  });

  it("the shared-digest rule still holds between the two image categories", async () => {
    const store = createMemoryAssetStore();

    store.setRaw("assets", poseRef(7), validRow);
    store.setRaw("assets", backgroundRef(7), validRow);
    store.setRaw("payloadMeta", digestOf(7), imageMeta);

    assert.equal((await store.apply({kind: "delete", ref: poseRef(7)})).payloadRemoved, false);
    assert.equal((await store.apply({kind: "delete", ref: backgroundRef(7)})).payloadRemoved, true);
  });

  it("a quota failure in an audio import leaves every store unchanged", async () => {
    const store = createMemoryAssetStore();

    await store.apply(audioMutation(audioRef(1)));

    const before = store.snapshot();

    store.failNextApply();
    await assert.rejects(store.apply(audioMutation(audioRef(2))));
    assert.deepEqual(store.snapshot(), before);
  });

  it("waveform identity is the key only: a value under key A carrying digest B is corrupt and never redirects to B", async () => {
    const store = createMemoryAssetStore();
    const other = Uint8Array.from([9, 9, 9]);

    store.setRaw("waveforms", digestOf(2), {peaksPerSecond: 50, peaks: other});
    store.setRaw("waveforms", digestOf(1), {peaksPerSecond: 50, peaks, digest: digestOf(2)});

    assert.equal((await store.getWaveform(digestOf(1))).status, "corrupt");
    assert.equal(await readWaveform(store, digestOf(1)), null);
    assert.deepEqual((await readWaveform(store, digestOf(2))).peaks, other);

    store.setRaw("waveforms", digestOf(1), {peaksPerSecond: 50, peaks, digest: digestOf(1)});
    assert.equal((await store.getWaveform(digestOf(1))).status, "corrupt");
  });

  it("lists and counts only the requested category and pages audio like images", async () => {
    const store = createMemoryAssetStore();

    for (let n = 1; n <= LOCAL_ASSET_PAGE_SIZE + 1; n += 1) {
      store.setRaw("assets", audioRef(n), {...validRow, label: `Audio ${n}`});
    }

    store.setRaw("assets", poseRef(1), validRow);
    store.setRaw("assets", backgroundRef(1), validRow);

    assert.equal(await store.countAssets("audio"), LOCAL_ASSET_PAGE_SIZE + 1);
    assert.equal(await store.countAssets("pose"), 1);

    const first = await listLocalAssetPage(store, "audio");

    assert.equal(first.entries.length, LOCAL_ASSET_PAGE_SIZE);
    assert.equal(first.hasNext, true);
    assert.equal(first.entries.every((entry) => entry.category === "audio"), true);
    assert.equal(first.entries.at(-1).ref, audioRef(LOCAL_ASSET_PAGE_SIZE));

    const second = await listLocalAssetPage(store, "audio", {after: first.entries.at(-1).ref});

    assert.deepEqual(second.entries.map((entry) => entry.ref), [audioRef(LOCAL_ASSET_PAGE_SIZE + 1)]);
    assert.equal(second.hasNext, false);
  });
});

describe("library audio reads", () => {
  const seedAudio = (store, n, patch = {}) => {
    store.setRaw("assets", audioRef(n), {...validRow, label: `Audio ${n}`});
    store.setRaw("payloadMeta", digestOf(n), {...audioMeta, ...patch});
  };

  it("readAudioMetadata returns the stored metadata of a present row only", async () => {
    const store = createMemoryAssetStore();

    seedAudio(store, 1);
    store.setRaw("payloadMeta", digestOf(2), audioMeta); // payload without a row
    store.setRaw("assets", audioRef(3), "corrupt row");
    store.setRaw("payloadMeta", digestOf(3), audioMeta);
    store.setRaw("assets", audioRef(4), validRow);
    store.setRaw("payloadMeta", digestOf(4), imageMeta); // an audio row pointing at image metadata
    store.setRaw("assets", audioRef(5), validRow); // no metadata

    assert.deepEqual(await readAudioMetadata(store, audioRef(1)), audioMeta);

    for (const n of [2, 3, 4, 5, 6]) {
      assert.equal(await readAudioMetadata(store, audioRef(n)), null, String(n));
    }
  });

  it("readAudioDurations finds a ref that is not on the first library page and omits an absent one, without listing", async () => {
    const store = createMemoryAssetStore();

    for (let n = 1; n <= LOCAL_ASSET_PAGE_SIZE + 1; n += 1) {
      seedAudio(store, n, {durationSeconds: n / 10});
    }

    const last = audioRef(LOCAL_ASSET_PAGE_SIZE + 1);
    const firstPage = await listLocalAssetPage(store, "audio");

    assert.equal(firstPage.entries.some((entry) => entry.ref === last), false);

    store.resetReads();

    const durations = await readAudioDurations(store, [last, audioRef(900), last]);

    assert.deepEqual(durations, {[last]: (LOCAL_ASSET_PAGE_SIZE + 1) / 10});
    assert.equal(store.reads.assets, 2);
    assert.equal(store.reads.payloadMeta, 1);
  });

  it("readAudioDurations drops NaN, Infinity, zero and negative durations even from a foreign store", async () => {
    const meta = (durationSeconds) => ({status: "present", value: {...audioMeta, durationSeconds}});
    const foreignStore = {
      getAssetRow: async () => ({status: "present", value: validRow}),
      getPayloadMeta: async (digest) => meta([Number.NaN, Number.POSITIVE_INFINITY, 0, -1, 0.005][Number.parseInt(digest.slice(-1), 16) - 1]),
    };
    const refs = [1, 2, 3, 4, 5].map(audioRef);

    assert.deepEqual(await readAudioDurations(foreignStore, refs), {[audioRef(5)]: 0.005});
  });

  it("readWaveform returns the record or null", async () => {
    const store = createMemoryAssetStore();

    store.setRaw("waveforms", DIGEST, {peaksPerSecond: 50, peaks});
    store.setRaw("waveforms", digestOf(2), {peaksPerSecond: 0, peaks});

    assert.deepEqual(await readWaveform(store, DIGEST), {peaksPerSecond: 50, peaks});
    assert.equal(await readWaveform(store, digestOf(2)), null);
    assert.equal(await readWaveform(store, digestOf(3)), null);
  });

  it("rename and delete work on audio refs through the library and announce ref and digest", async () => {
    const library = makeLibrary();

    await library.store.apply(audioMutation());

    assert.deepEqual(await renameLocalAsset(library, audioRef(1), "  Narration  "), {kind: "renamed", ref: audioRef(1)});
    assert.equal(library.store.getRaw("assets", audioRef(1)).label, "Narration");
    assert.deepEqual(await deleteLocalAsset(library, audioRef(1)), {kind: "deleted", ref: audioRef(1), payloadRemoved: true});
    assert.deepEqual(library.posted.at(-1), {type: "asset-library-changed", refs: [audioRef(1)], digests: [DIGEST]});
    assert.equal(await countLocalAssets(library.store, "audio"), 0);
  });
});
```

`src/web/assetLibrary/payloadScope.ts`:

```ts
import {
  buildLocalAssetRef,
  type LocalAssetCategory,
  type LocalAssetRef,
} from "../../localAssets/refs.ts";

export type DerivedStoreName = "thumbnails" | "waveforms";

/**
 * What deleting one row may take with it. `protectingRefs` are the rows of the
 * same digest in OTHER categories whose presence keeps the shared payload (even
 * a corrupt row protects it): the two image categories protect each other,
 * audio has no other category. `derivedStores` are the per-digest stores that
 * hold derivatives of this kind of payload (an image has no waveform, audio has
 * no thumbnail).
 */
export const deletionScope = (
  category: LocalAssetCategory,
  digest: string,
): {
  protectingRefs: LocalAssetRef[];
  derivedStores: readonly DerivedStoreName[];
} =>
  category === "audio"
    ? {protectingRefs: [], derivedStores: ["waveforms"]}
    : {
        protectingRefs: [
          buildLocalAssetRef(
            category === "pose" ? "background" : "pose",
            digest,
          ),
        ],
        derivedStores: ["thumbnails"],
      };
```

`src/web/assetLibrary/store.ts` - replace the file contents above `LocalAssetLibraryErrorCode` with:

```ts
import type {
  AudioPayloadMetadata,
  ImagePayloadMetadata,
  PayloadMetadata,
} from "../../localAssets/readiness.ts";
import type {
  LocalAssetCategory,
  LocalAssetRef,
  LocalAudioRef,
  LocalImageRef,
} from "../../localAssets/refs.ts";
import type {
  AssetRow,
  Decoded,
  ThumbnailRecord,
  WaveformRecord,
} from "./records.ts";

export type AssetPageCursor = {after?: LocalAssetRef; before?: LocalAssetRef};

export type AssetLibraryMutation =
  | {
      kind: "import";
      ref: LocalImageRef;
      defaultLabel: string;
      originalFilename: string;
      createdAt: string;
      payloadMeta: ImagePayloadMetadata;
      blob: Blob;
      thumbnail: ThumbnailRecord;
    }
  | {
      kind: "import-audio";
      ref: LocalAudioRef;
      defaultLabel: string;
      originalFilename: string;
      createdAt: string;
      payloadMeta: AudioPayloadMetadata;
      blob: Blob;
      waveform: WaveformRecord;
    }
  | {kind: "rename"; ref: LocalAssetRef; label: string}
  | {kind: "delete"; ref: LocalAssetRef};

export type AssetLibraryMutationResult =
  | {kind: "imported"; ref: LocalAssetRef; created: boolean} // created=false: existing row reused/repaired
  | {kind: "renamed"; ref: LocalAssetRef}
  | {kind: "deleted"; ref: LocalAssetRef; payloadRemoved: boolean}
  | {kind: "not-found"; ref: LocalAssetRef}; // rename/delete of a row that no longer exists
```

3. `buildRange(category: LocalAssetCategory, ...)`; `isCanonicalKeyOf(key, category: LocalAssetCategory): key is LocalAssetRef`; the `listAssets` result element type becomes `{ref: LocalAssetRef; row: Decoded<AssetRow>}`.
4. Replace the whole `applyMutation` with:

```ts
  const applyMutation = async (
    mutation: AssetLibraryMutation,
  ): Promise<AssetLibraryMutationResult> => {
    const {digest, category} = refParts(mutation.ref);

    if (
      mutation.kind !== "import" &&
      mutation.kind !== "import-audio" &&
      mutation.kind !== "rename" &&
      mutation.kind !== "delete"
    ) {
      throw new Error(
        `Unknown mutation kind: ${String((mutation as {kind?: unknown}).kind)}`,
      );
    }

    // A programming error, not a storage failure: nothing is opened or written.
    if (
      (mutation.kind === "import" && category === "audio") ||
      (mutation.kind === "import-audio" && category !== "audio")
    ) {
      throw new Error(`${mutation.kind} does not accept a ${category} ref`);
    }

    return transact(STORE_NAMES, "readwrite", async (transaction) => {
      const assets = transaction.objectStore("assets");

      if (mutation.kind === "import" || mutation.kind === "import-audio") {
        const existing = decodeAssetRow(
          mutation.ref,
          await requestToPromise<unknown>(assets.get(mutation.ref)),
        );
        const keep = existing.status === "present" ? existing.value : null;

        await requestToPromise(
          assets.put(
            {
              label: keep?.label ?? mutation.defaultLabel,
              originalFilename: mutation.originalFilename,
              createdAt: keep?.createdAt ?? mutation.createdAt,
            },
            mutation.ref,
          ),
        );
        await requestToPromise(
          transaction.objectStore("payloadMeta").put({...mutation.payloadMeta}, digest),
        );
        await requestToPromise(
          transaction.objectStore("blobs").put({blob: mutation.blob}, digest),
        );

        if (mutation.kind === "import") {
          const thumbnail: ThumbnailRecord = {...mutation.thumbnail};

          await requestToPromise(
            transaction.objectStore("thumbnails").put(thumbnail, digest),
          );
        } else {
          await requestToPromise(
            transaction.objectStore("waveforms").put(
              {
                peaksPerSecond: mutation.waveform.peaksPerSecond,
                peaks: mutation.waveform.peaks,
              },
              digest,
            ),
          );
        }

        return {
          kind: "imported",
          ref: mutation.ref,
          created: existing.status === "absent",
        };
      }

      if (mutation.kind === "rename") {
        const existing = decodeAssetRow(
          mutation.ref,
          await requestToPromise<unknown>(assets.get(mutation.ref)),
        );

        if (existing.status === "absent") {
          return {kind: "not-found", ref: mutation.ref};
        }

        const row: AssetRow =
          existing.status === "present"
            ? {...existing.value, label: mutation.label}
            : {
                label: mutation.label,
                originalFilename: "",
                createdAt: new Date().toISOString(),
              };

        await requestToPromise(assets.put(row, mutation.ref));
        return {kind: "renamed", ref: mutation.ref};
      }

      if (
        (await requestToPromise<IDBValidKey | undefined>(
          assets.getKey(mutation.ref),
        )) === undefined
      ) {
        return {kind: "not-found", ref: mutation.ref};
      }

      await requestToPromise(assets.delete(mutation.ref));

      const {protectingRefs, derivedStores} = deletionScope(category, digest);
      let payloadRemoved = true;

      // A corrupt row in the other category still protects the shared payload.
      for (const otherRef of protectingRefs) {
        if (
          (await requestToPromise<IDBValidKey | undefined>(
            assets.getKey(otherRef),
          )) !== undefined
        ) {
          payloadRemoved = false;
        }
      }

      if (payloadRemoved) {
        const names: StoreName[] = ["payloadMeta", "blobs", ...derivedStores];

        for (const name of names) {
          await requestToPromise(transaction.objectStore(name).delete(digest));
        }
      }

      return {kind: "deleted", ref: mutation.ref, payloadRemoved};
    });
  };
```

      if ((mutation.kind === "import-audio") !== (category === "audio")) {

      if (mutation.kind === "import") {
        staged.thumbnails.set(digest, {...mutation.thumbnail});
      } else {
        staged.waveforms.set(digest, {
          peaksPerSecond: mutation.waveform.peaksPerSecond,
          peaks: mutation.waveform.peaks,
        });
      }

      result = {kind: "imported", ref: mutation.ref, created: existing.status === "absent"};
```

- [ ] **Step 6: Implement the library reads and widen the library layer types**

`src/web/assetLibrary/library.ts`: change imports/types so `LocalAssetEntry` is `{ref: LocalAssetRef; category: LocalAssetCategory; digest: string; row: Decoded<AssetRow>}`; `listLocalAssetPage(store, category: LocalAssetCategory, cursor)` and `countLocalAssets(store, category: LocalAssetCategory)` and `renameLocalAsset`/`deleteLocalAsset` take `LocalAssetRef`. Add (with `import type {AudioDurationMap} from "../../audio/catalog.ts"`, `import {isAudioPayload, type AudioPayloadMetadata} from "../../localAssets/readiness.ts"`, `type LocalAudioRef` from refs, `type WaveformRecord` from records):

```ts
/**
 * The stored metadata of a local audio ref, or null when the ref is not usable:
 * its row is absent or corrupt, or its metadata is absent, corrupt or not audio.
 * The ref is the key; nothing stored redirects it (INV-11).
 */
export const readAudioMetadata = async (
  store: Pick<AssetLibraryStore, "getAssetRow" | "getPayloadMeta">,
  ref: LocalAudioRef,
): Promise<AudioPayloadMetadata | null> => {
  const parsed = parseLocalAssetRef(ref);

  if (parsed === null) {
    return null;
  }

  const row = await store.getAssetRow(ref);

  if (row.status !== "present") {
    return null;
  }

  const meta = await store.getPayloadMeta(parsed.digest);

  return meta.status === "present" && isAudioPayload(meta.value)
    ? meta.value
    : null;
};

/** The waveform stored under `digest`, or null when absent or corrupt. */
export const readWaveform = async (
  store: Pick<AssetLibraryStore, "getWaveform">,
  digest: string,
): Promise<WaveformRecord | null> => {
  const record = await store.getWaveform(digest);

  return record.status === "present" ? record.value : null;
};

/**
 * The durations of exactly these refs (the Story's refs, never the visible
 * library page, which holds LOCAL_ASSET_PAGE_SIZE rows at a time). Refs that are
 * absent stay out of the map (they remain `missing`); a duration that is not
 * finite and greater than 0 never enters it.
 */
export const readAudioDurations = async (
  store: Pick<AssetLibraryStore, "getAssetRow" | "getPayloadMeta">,
  refs: Iterable<LocalAudioRef>,
): Promise<AudioDurationMap> => {
  const durations: Partial<Record<LocalAudioRef, number>> = {};

  for (const ref of new Set(refs)) {
    const metadata = await readAudioMetadata(store, ref);

    if (
      metadata !== null &&
      Number.isFinite(metadata.durationSeconds) &&
      metadata.durationSeconds > 0
    ) {
      durations[ref] = metadata.durationSeconds;
    }
  }

  return durations;
};
```

```ts
  /* ---- audio rows (AUDIO-003) ---- */

  /** Imports a committed audio fixture with a tiny synthetic waveform (decoding has its own specs). */
  async seedAudio(options: {fixture: string; label?: string}) {
    const mutation = await buildAudioImportMutation(options);
    const lib = requireLibrary();
    const result = await withAssetLibraryLock(lib.locks, "exclusive", () =>
      lib.store.apply(mutation),
    );

    channel.post({
      type: "asset-library-changed",
      refs: [mutation.ref],
      digests: [mutation.ref.slice(-64)],
    });

    return {result, ref: mutation.ref, digest: mutation.ref.slice(-64)};
  },

  /** An audio import whose blob IndexedDB cannot clone: the transaction aborts after earlier writes. */
  async applyBrokenAudioImport(fixture: string) {
    const mutation = await buildAudioImportMutation({fixture});

    try {
      await requireLibrary().store.apply({
        ...mutation,
        blob: (() => undefined) as unknown as Blob,
      });
      return {ok: true as const};
    } catch (error) {
      return {ok: false as const, error: describeError(error)};
    }
  },

  async inspectAudioRef(ref: LocalAudioRef) {
    const store = requireLibrary().store;
    const parsed = parseLocalAssetRef(ref);

    if (parsed === null) {
      throw new Error("not a ref");
    }

    const [row, meta, blob, waveform] = await Promise.all([
      store.getAssetRow(ref),
      store.getPayloadMeta(parsed.digest),
      store.getBlob(parsed.digest),
      store.getWaveform(parsed.digest),
    ]);

    return {
      row,
      meta,
      blob:
        blob.status === "present"
          ? {
              status: "present" as const,
              size: blob.value.size,
              sha256: await sha256Hex(new Uint8Array(await blob.value.arrayBuffer())),
            }
          : blob,
      waveform:
        waveform.status === "present"
          ? {
              status: "present" as const,
              peaksPerSecond: waveform.value.peaksPerSecond,
              peaks: Array.from(waveform.value.peaks),
            }
          : waveform,
    };
  },

  async getWaveform(digest: string) {
    const record = await requireLibrary().store.getWaveform(digest);

    return record.status === "present"
      ? {status: "present" as const, peaks: Array.from(record.value.peaks)}
      : record;
  },

  /**
   * Creates a version 1 database (the four v0.3 stores) holding one pose and
   * one background in the v0.3 record shapes, then closes it. Run before the
   * library is opened, so the production open performs the 1 -> 2 upgrade.
   */
  async seedVersion1Database() {
    const database = await new Promise<IDBDatabase>((resolve, reject) => {
      const request = indexedDB.open(ASSET_DB_NAME, 1);

      request.onupgradeneeded = () => {
        for (const name of ["assets", "payloadMeta", "blobs", "thumbnails"]) {
          request.result.createObjectStore(name);
        }
      };
      request.onsuccess = () => resolve(request.result);
      request.onerror = () => reject(request.error);
    });
    const seeded: Record<string, {ref: string; digest: string; byteSize: number}> = {};

    try {
      for (const [category, color] of [
        ["pose", "#cc3366"],
        ["background", "#3366cc"],
      ] as const) {
        const {bytes, blob} = await makePng(16, 16, color);
        const digest = await sha256Hex(bytes);
        const ref = buildLocalAssetRef(category, digest);
        const thumbnail = await buildThumbnail("valid");
        const transaction = database.transaction(
          ["assets", "payloadMeta", "blobs", "thumbnails"],
          "readwrite",
        );

        transaction.objectStore("assets").put(
          {
            label: `Legacy ${category}`,
            originalFilename: `legacy-${category}.png`,
            createdAt: "2026-01-01T00:00:00.000Z",
          },
          ref,
        );
        transaction
          .objectStore("payloadMeta")
          .put({mimeType: "image/png", byteSize: bytes.length, width: 16, height: 16}, digest);
        transaction.objectStore("blobs").put({blob}, digest);
        transaction.objectStore("thumbnails").put(thumbnail, digest);
        await transactionDone(transaction);
        seeded[category] = {ref, digest, byteSize: bytes.length};
      }
    } finally {
      database.close();
    }

    return seeded;
  },
```

- Above `toraHarness` add the builder:

```ts
const buildAudioImportMutation = async (options: {
  fixture: string;
  label?: string;
}): Promise<Extract<AssetLibraryMutation, {kind: "import-audio"}>> => {
  const response = await fetch(`/tests/fixtures/local-audio/${options.fixture}`);

  if (!response.ok) {
    throw new Error(`Fixture ${options.fixture} answered ${response.status}`);
  }

  const bytes = new Uint8Array(await response.arrayBuffer());
  const inspected = await inspectAudio(bytes);

  if (!inspected.ok) {
    throw new Error(`Harness audio rejected: ${inspected.message}`);
  }

  const digest = await sha256Hex(bytes);
  const {container, codec, durationSeconds, sampleRate, channels} = inspected.metadata;

  return {
    kind: "import-audio",
    ref: buildLocalAssetRef("audio", digest),
    defaultLabel: options.label ?? options.fixture,
    originalFilename: options.fixture,
    createdAt: new Date().toISOString(),
    payloadMeta: {
      kind: "audio",
      mimeType: audioMimeTypeFor(container),
      byteSize: bytes.length,
      container,
      codec,
      durationSeconds,
      sampleRate,
      channels,
    },
    blob: new Blob([bytes as BlobPart], {type: audioMimeTypeFor(container)}),
    waveform: {peaksPerSecond: 50, peaks: Uint8Array.from([1, 2, 3, 255])},
  };
};
```

    expect((await harness(page, "rawSchema")).version).toBe(1);

      expect(stored.blob).toMatchObject({status: "present", type: "image/png", sha256: seeded[category].digest});

    expect(first).toEqual({kind: "unavailable", message: BLOCKED_MESSAGE});
    expect(await harness(pageB, "lockState")).toEqual({held: ["exclusive"], pending: []});

    await harness(pageA, "releaseStubbornConnection");
    await harness(pageB, "waitForStatus", "ready");

    expect((await harness(pageB, "rawSchema")).version).toBe(2);
    expect((await harness(pageB, "inspectRef", seeded.pose.ref)).row.status).toBe("present");
    expect((await harness(pageB, "inspectRef", seeded.background.ref)).blob.status).toBe("present");
    expect(pageA.errors).toEqual([]);
    expect(pageB.errors).toEqual([]);

    expect(stored.meta.value).toMatchObject({kind: "audio", mimeType: "audio/wav", container: "wave", codec: "pcm", sampleRate: 48000, channels: 2});
    expect(stored.meta.value.durationSeconds).toBeCloseTo(2.2, 3);

    expect(stored.waveform).toEqual({status: "present", peaksPerSecond: 50, peaks: [1, 2, 3, 255]});

    expect(snapshot.waveforms).toEqual([audio.digest]);
    expect(snapshot.thumbnails).toEqual([image.digest]);
    expect(await harness(page, "count", "audio")).toBe(1);
    expect(await harness(page, "count", "pose")).toBe(1);

    const after = await harness(page, "rawKeySnapshot");

    expect(after.waveforms).toEqual([]);
    expect(after.blobs).toEqual([image.digest]);
    expect(after.thumbnails).toEqual([image.digest]);
    expect(after.payloadMeta).toEqual([image.digest]);
    expect((await harness(page, "inspectRef", image.ref)).row.status).toBe("present");

  test("a throwing audio transaction leaves no partial row, payload or waveform", async ({context}) => {
    const page = await openHarnessPage(context);

    await harness(page, "openLibrary");

    const before = await harness(page, "rawKeySnapshot");
    const broken = await harness(page, "applyBrokenAudioImport", "tone-440-2.2s.wav");

    expect(broken.ok).toBe(false);
    expect(broken.error).toMatchObject({code: "storage-error"});
    expect(await harness(page, "rawKeySnapshot")).toEqual(before);
  });

  test("a waveform carrying a foreign digest is corrupt in real IndexedDB and never redirects", async ({context}) => {
    const page = await openHarnessPage(context);

    await harness(page, "openLibrary");

    const a = digestFor(0xa1);
    const b = digestFor(0xb2);

    await page.evaluate(
      ([first, second]) =>
        window.toraHarness.rawPut("waveforms", [
          [first, {peaksPerSecond: 50, peaks: new Uint8Array([1, 2]), digest: second}],
          [second, {peaksPerSecond: 50, peaks: new Uint8Array([9, 9])}],
        ]),
      [a, b],
    );

    expect((await harness(page, "getWaveform", a)).status).toBe("corrupt");
    expect(await harness(page, "getWaveform", b)).toEqual({status: "present", peaks: [9, 9]});
  });

### Task 7: Story resolution of `local:audio:` refs (collector, image-budget filter, status line)

`collectStoryLocalAssetUsages` now includes `local:audio:` refs with their roles; the image budget receives only image usages; an audio row pointing at image metadata (or the reverse) is `corrupt`; `useStoryLocalAssets` therefore resolves, verifies and creates object URLs for local audio exactly as for images, and `localAssets.sources` carries them.

    {ref: audioRefOf(9), category: "audio", digest: digestOf(9), sceneIndexes: [1], roles: ["music", "sfx"]},
    {ref: audioRefOf(3), category: "audio", digest: digestOf(3), sceneIndexes: [0], roles: ["sfx"]},
    {ref: audioRefOf(4), category: "audio", digest: digestOf(4), sceneIndexes: [0, 2], roles: ["voice"]},
  ]);
});

test("bundled sounds are not local usages and an audio-only Story has no local image refs", () => {
  const story = withScenes(storyOf([["formal", "office"]]), [{sfx: {src: "boom"}, voice: {src: audioRefOf(1)}}]);

  assert.deepEqual(collectStoryLocalAssetUsages(story).map((usage) => usage.ref), [audioRefOf(1)]);
  assert.equal(storyHasLocalAssetRefs(story), false);
  assert.deepEqual(collectStoryLocalAssetUsages(withScenes(storyOf([["formal", "office"]]), [{sfx: {src: "boom"}}])), []);

test("splitUsagesByCategory feeds the image budget with image usages only", () => {
  const usages = collectStoryLocalAssetUsages(
    withScenes(storyOf([[poseRef(1), "office"]]), [{voice: {src: audioRefOf(2)}}]),
  );
  const split = splitUsagesByCategory(usages);

  assert.deepEqual(split.image.map((usage) => usage.ref), [poseRef(1)]);
  assert.deepEqual(split.audio.map((usage) => usage.ref), [audioRefOf(2)]);

  const result = evaluateLocalAssetBudget(split.image, new Map([[digestOf(1), metadata(100, 4, 5)]]));

  assert.deepEqual(result, {ok: true, refCount: 1, totalBytes: 100, totalPixels: 20});
});

Append to `tests/local-asset-state.test.mjs` (add to its imports: `import {inspectAudio, audioMimeTypeFor} from "../src/localAssets/audioInspection.ts";` and `import {fixtureBytes} from "./helpers/audioBytes.mjs";`):

```js
const audioRefOf = (digest) => buildLocalAssetRef("audio", digest);

/** Seeds one real audio fixture (row, metadata from inspectAudio, bytes). */
const seedAudio = async (store, name) => {
  const bytes = fixtureBytes(name);
  const digest = await sha256Hex(bytes);
  const inspected = await inspectAudio(bytes);

  assert.equal(inspected.ok, true);

  const {container, codec, durationSeconds, sampleRate, channels} = inspected.metadata;
  const meta = {
    kind: "audio",
    mimeType: audioMimeTypeFor(container),
    byteSize: bytes.length,
    container,
    codec,
    durationSeconds,
    sampleRate,
    channels,
  };
  const ref = audioRefOf(digest);

  store.setRaw("assets", ref, row());
  store.setRaw("payloadMeta", digest, meta);
  store.setRaw("blobs", digest, {blob: new Blob([bytes])});

  return {digest, ref, meta, bytes};
};

const audioStoryOf = (...sources) => ({
  title: "Audio",
  scenes: sources.map((src) => ({pose: "formal", background: "office", duration: 3, caption: "x", voice: {src}})),
});

test("a local voice is resolved and verified like an image and keeps its role", async () => {
  const store = createMemoryAssetStore();
  const {digest, ref, meta} = await seedAudio(store, "tone-440-2.2s.wav");
  const state = await resolve(audioStoryOf(ref), store);

  assert.equal(state.kind, "resolved");
  assert.equal(state.allReady, true);
  assert.deepEqual(
    state.refs.map((entry) => [entry.usage.ref, entry.usage.category, entry.usage.roles, entry.status]),
    [[ref, "audio", ["voice"], "ready"]],
  );
  assert.deepEqual(state.verified.get(digest), {digest, ...meta});
  assert.equal(state.blobs.get(digest).type, "audio/wav");
});

test("audio refs never count against the image ref cap, with a ready or a disabled library", async () => {
  const many = Array.from({length: MAX_BROWSER_STORY_LOCAL_ASSET_REFS + 6}, (_, index) => audioRefOf(digestOf(1000 + index)));
  const story = audioStoryOf(...many);
  const ready = await resolve(story, createMemoryAssetStore());
  const disabled = await resolve(story, createMemoryAssetStore(), {library: {kind: "disabled", message: DISABLED_MESSAGE}});

  assert.equal(ready.kind, "resolved");
  assert.equal(ready.refs.every((entry) => entry.status === "missing"), true);
  assert.equal(disabled.kind, "resolved");
  assert.equal(disabled.refs.every((entry) => entry.status === "unavailable"), true);

  const images = storyOf(Array.from({length: MAX_BROWSER_STORY_LOCAL_ASSET_REFS}, (_, index) => [poseRef(index + 1), "office"]));
  const withAudio = {...images, scenes: images.scenes.map((scene, index) => (index === 0 ? {...scene, voice: {src: many[0]}} : scene))};
  const tooMany = storyOf(Array.from({length: MAX_BROWSER_STORY_LOCAL_ASSET_REFS + 1}, (_, index) => [poseRef(index + 1), "office"]));

  assert.equal((await resolve(withAudio, createMemoryAssetStore())).kind, "resolved");
  assert.equal((await resolve(tooMany, createMemoryAssetStore())).kind, "over-budget");
});

test("audio bytes never count against the image byte budget", async () => {
  const store = createMemoryAssetStore();
  const refs = [];

  for (let n = 1; n <= 6; n += 1) {
    store.setRaw("assets", audioRefOf(digestOf(n)), row());
    store.setRaw("payloadMeta", digestOf(n), {
      kind: "audio", mimeType: "audio/wav", byteSize: 50 * MIB, container: "wave", codec: "pcm",
      durationSeconds: 100, sampleRate: 48_000, channels: 2,
    });
    refs.push(audioRefOf(digestOf(n)));
  }

  // 300 MiB of audio is over the 256 MiB image limit, which is not an audio concern (AUDIO-005 adds the audio budget).
  assert.equal((await resolve(audioStoryOf(...refs), store)).kind, "resolved");
});

test("an audio row pointing at image metadata, and an image row pointing at audio metadata, are corrupt and their bytes are never read", async () => {
  const store = createMemoryAssetStore();
  const image = await seedImage(store, 4);
  const audio = await seedAudio(store, "tone-440-2.2s.wav");
  const audioRowForImage = audioRefOf(image.digest);
  const poseRowForAudio = buildLocalAssetRef("pose", audio.digest);

  store.setRaw("assets", audioRowForImage, row());
  store.setRaw("assets", poseRowForAudio, row());
  store.resetReads();

  const story = {...storyOf([[poseRowForAudio, "office"]]), music: {src: audioRowForImage}};
  const state = await resolve(story, store);

  assert.equal(state.kind, "resolved");
  assert.deepEqual(state.refs.map((entry) => [entry.usage.ref, entry.status]), [
    [poseRowForAudio, "corrupt"],
    [audioRowForImage, "corrupt"],
  ]);
  assert.match(state.refs[0].detail, /does not match the asset category/u);
  assert.equal(store.reads.blobs, 0);
});

test("a stored audio file whose bytes changed is corrupt", async () => {
  const store = createMemoryAssetStore();
  const {digest, ref, bytes} = await seedAudio(store, "tone-440-2.2s.wav");
  const altered = Uint8Array.from(bytes);

  altered[altered.length - 3] ^= 0xff;
  store.setRaw("blobs", digest, {blob: new Blob([altered])});

  const state = await resolve(audioStoryOf(ref), store);

  assert.deepEqual(state.refs.map((entry) => entry.status), ["corrupt"]);
  assert.match(state.refs[0].detail, /SHA-256/u);
});

const resolvedAudio = async (store) => {
  const audio = await seedAudio(store, "tone-440-2.2s.wav");
  const resolved = await resolve(audioStoryOf(audio.ref), store);

  assert.equal(resolved.kind, "resolved");
  assert.equal(resolved.allReady, true);

  return {...audio, resolved};
};

test("render preparation with an unchanged audio payload passes and leases its blob URL", async () => {
  const store = createMemoryAssetStore();
  const pool = createFakePool();
  const {resolved, ref, digest} = await resolvedAudio(store);
  const result = await prepare(resolved, store, createMemoryLockManager(), pool);

  assert.equal(result.ok, true);
  assert.equal(Object.isFrozen(result.localAssetSources), true);
  assert.deepEqual(result.localAssetSources[ref], {kind: "url", url: `blob:fake/${digest.slice(-8)}`});
  assert.deepEqual(pool.calls.acquire.map((call) => call.digest), [digest]);
  result.release();
  assert.deepEqual(pool.calls.release, [digest]);
});

for (const [title, patch] of [
  ["only durationSeconds", {durationSeconds: 99}],
  ["only sampleRate", {sampleRate: 44_100}],
  ["only channels", {channels: 1}],
  ["only codec (an invalid pair, so the record turns corrupt)", {codec: "mp3"}],
  ["only container (an invalid pair)", {container: "mp3"}],
]) {
  test(`render preparation returns the changed message before any URL when audio payloadMeta changes: ${title}`, async () => {
    const store = createMemoryAssetStore();
    const pool = createFakePool();
    const {resolved, digest, meta} = await resolvedAudio(store);

    // mimeType and byteSize stay as they were.
    store.setRaw("payloadMeta", digest, {...meta, ...patch});

    const result = await prepare(resolved, store, createMemoryLockManager(), pool);

    assert.equal(result.ok, false);
    assert.equal(result.message, "Local assets changed while preparing the render. Try again.");
    assert.equal(pool.calls.acquire.length, 0);
  });
}

test("a payloadMeta whose kind changed from audio to image, or from image to audio, fails closed", async () => {
  const audioStore = createMemoryAssetStore();
  const audioPool = createFakePool();
  const audio = await resolvedAudio(audioStore);

  audioStore.setRaw("payloadMeta", audio.digest, {mimeType: "image/png", byteSize: audio.meta.byteSize, width: 2, height: 2});

  const toImage = await prepare(audio.resolved, audioStore, createMemoryLockManager(), audioPool);

  assert.equal(toImage.ok, false);
  assert.equal(audioPool.calls.acquire.length, 0);

  const imageStore = createMemoryAssetStore();
  const imagePool = createFakePool();
  const {resolved, digest} = await resolvedTwoRefs(imageStore);
  const sample = await seedAudio(createMemoryAssetStore(), "tone-440-2.2s.wav");

  imageStore.setRaw("payloadMeta", digest, sample.meta);

  const toAudio = await prepare(resolved, imageStore, createMemoryLockManager(), imagePool);

  assert.equal(toAudio.ok, false);
  assert.equal(imagePool.calls.acquire.length, 0);
});

Replace `LocalAssetUsage` with the three types from Interfaces, then change `collectStoryLocalAssetUsages` as follows: its `usagesByRef` map holds `LocalImageUsage`, the existing loop is unchanged, and the last line becomes `return [...usagesByRef.values(), ...collectAudioUsages(story)];`. Add above it:

```ts
const collectAudioUsages = (story: Story): LocalAudioUsage[] => {
  const byRef = new Map<LocalAudioRef, LocalAudioUsage>();
  const add = (value: string, role: AudioRole, sceneIndex: number | null) => {
    if (!isLocalAudioRef(value)) {
      return;
    }

    let usage = byRef.get(value);

    if (usage === undefined) {
      usage = {
        ref: value,
        category: "audio",
        digest: value.slice(value.length - 64),
        sceneIndexes: [],
        roles: [],
      };
      byRef.set(value, usage);
    }

    if (!usage.roles.includes(role)) {
      usage.roles.push(role);
    }

    if (sceneIndex !== null && !usage.sceneIndexes.includes(sceneIndex)) {
      usage.sceneIndexes.push(sceneIndex);
    }
  };

  if (story.music !== undefined) {
    add(story.music.src, "music", null);
  }

  for (const [sceneIndex, scene] of story.scenes.entries()) {
    if (scene.sfx !== undefined) {
      add(scene.sfx.src, "sfx", sceneIndex);
    }

    if (scene.voice !== undefined) {
      add(scene.voice.src, "voice", sceneIndex);
    }
  }

  return [...byRef.values()];
};

export const splitUsagesByCategory = (
  usages: readonly LocalAssetUsage[],
): {image: LocalImageUsage[]; audio: LocalAudioUsage[]} => {
  const image: LocalImageUsage[] = [];
  const audio: LocalAudioUsage[] = [];

  for (const usage of usages) {
    if (usage.category === "audio") {
      audio.push(usage);
    } else {
      image.push(usage);
    }
  }

  return {image, audio};
};
```

3. `evaluateLocalAssetBudget(usages: readonly LocalImageUsage[], ...)`. Add a comment above `storyHasLocalAssetRefs`: `// Image refs only: audio refs are found with collectStoryLocalAssetUsages / storyHasAudio.` (the function body stays).

`src/web/localAssetPreviewStatus.ts`: move `describeLocalAssetPreviewStatus` out of `Preview.tsx` (delete it and its doc comment there, `import {describeLocalAssetPreviewStatus} from "../localAssetPreviewStatus.ts";`), then change it to:

```ts
import type {StoryLocalAssetState} from "./localAssetState.ts";

type NotReady = Extract<StoryLocalAssetState, {kind: "resolved"}>["refs"];

// Corrupt is shown like missing (D-9); only "unavailable" gets its own word.
const wordFor = (entries: NotReady): "missing" | "unavailable" =>
  entries.some((entry) => entry.status !== "unavailable")
    ? "missing"
    : "unavailable";

/**
 * Short status line for refs that are not ready, e.g.
 * "2 local assets are missing in this browser. Scenes 1, 3 show placeholders."
 * Audio is reported separately (it has no placeholder: its sound is not played).
 * Returns null when every ref is ready (or there is nothing to report yet).
 */
export const describeLocalAssetPreviewStatus = (
  state: StoryLocalAssetState | undefined,
): string | null => {
  if (state?.kind !== "resolved") {
    return null;
  }

  const notReady = state.refs.filter((entry) => entry.status !== "ready");

  if (notReady.length === 0) {
    return null;
  }

  const images = notReady.filter((entry) => entry.usage.category !== "audio");
  const audio = notReady.filter((entry) => entry.usage.category === "audio");
  const sentences: string[] = [];

  if (images.length > 0) {
    const scenes = [...new Set(images.flatMap((entry) => entry.usage.sceneIndexes))]
      .sort((a, b) => a - b)
      .map((index) => index + 1);

    sentences.push(
      `${images.length} local ${images.length === 1 ? "asset is" : "assets are"} ${wordFor(images)} in this browser.`,
      scenes.length === 1
        ? `Scene ${scenes[0]} shows a placeholder.`
        : `Scenes ${scenes.join(", ")} show placeholders.`,
    );
  }

  if (audio.length > 0) {
    sentences.push(
      `${audio.length} local audio ${audio.length === 1 ? "file is" : "files are"} ${wordFor(audio)} in this browser.`,
      audio.length === 1 ? "Its sound is not played." : "Their sounds are not played.",
    );
  }

  const details = [
    ...new Set(
      [
        state.failureMessage,
        ...notReady
          .filter((entry) => entry.status === "unavailable")
          .map((entry) => entry.detail),
      ].filter((detail): detail is string => detail !== null && detail !== ""),
    ),
  ];

  return [...sentences, ...details].join(" ");
};
```

### Task 8: The page's single audio duration map

`App` builds ONE `AudioDurationMap` from the Story plus `readAudioDurations` (local library durations) and passes the same object to the Player config and to `Preview`; `Preview` no longer builds its own. The map is recomputed when the Story's set of local audio refs changes or a library mutation commits (the refresh token also bumps on window focus and on other tabs' changes).

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `readAudioDurations` (Task 6), `storyAudioDurations`, `storyAudioRefs` (`src/audio/storyAudio.ts`), `AudioDurationMap`, `isLocalAudioRef`, `AssetLibraryStatus`.
- Produces (`src/web/useStoryAudioDurations.ts`): `localAudioRefsKey(story: Story): string` (the Story's distinct local audio refs, sorted, comma-joined; `""` when none), `parseLocalAudioRefsKey(key: string): LocalAudioRef[]`, `sameDurationMap(a: AudioDurationMap, b: AudioDurationMap): boolean`, `useStoryAudioDurations(story: Story, library: AssetLibraryStatus | null, refreshToken: number): AudioDurationMap`. `Preview` takes a required `audioDurations: AudioDurationMap` prop and uses exactly that object for `getWebPlayerConfig` and `inputProps`.

  localAudioRefsKey,
  parseLocalAudioRefsKey,
  sameDurationMap,
} from "../src/web/useStoryAudioDurations.ts";

const audioRef = (n) => `local:audio:sha256:${n.toString(16).padStart(64, "0")}`;
const scene = (extra = {}) => ({type: "intro", pose: "formal", background: "office", text: "Hi", duration: 3, ...extra});

describe("localAudioRefsKey", () => {
  it("lists the Story's distinct local audio refs, sorted, without bundled sounds", () => {
    const story = {
      title: "T",
      music: {src: audioRef(3)},
      scenes: [scene({sfx: {src: "boom"}, voice: {src: audioRef(2)}}), scene({sfx: {src: audioRef(3)}, voice: {src: audioRef(1)}})],
    };

    assert.equal(localAudioRefsKey(story), [audioRef(1), audioRef(2), audioRef(3)].join(","));
    assert.deepEqual(parseLocalAudioRefsKey(localAudioRefsKey(story)), [audioRef(1), audioRef(2), audioRef(3)]);
  });

  it("is empty for a Story without local audio and does not depend on scene order", () => {
    assert.equal(localAudioRefsKey({title: "T", scenes: [scene({sfx: {src: "ding"}})]}), "");
    assert.deepEqual(parseLocalAudioRefsKey(""), []);

    const a = {title: "T", scenes: [scene({voice: {src: audioRef(1)}}), scene({voice: {src: audioRef(2)}})]};
    const b = {title: "T", scenes: [...a.scenes].reverse()};

    assert.equal(localAudioRefsKey(a), localAudioRefsKey(b));
  });
});

describe("sameDurationMap", () => {
  it("is entry-wise equality", () => {
    assert.equal(sameDurationMap({}, {}), true);
    assert.equal(sameDurationMap({[audioRef(1)]: 2.2}, {[audioRef(1)]: 2.2}), true);
    assert.equal(sameDurationMap({[audioRef(1)]: 2.2}, {[audioRef(1)]: 2.3}), false);
    assert.equal(sameDurationMap({[audioRef(1)]: 2.2}, {}), false);
    assert.equal(sameDurationMap({[audioRef(1)]: 2.2}, {[audioRef(2)]: 2.2}), false);
  });
});

describe("one duration map per page (AUDIO-001 final-review follow-up)", () => {
  const walk = (directory) =>
    readdirSync(directory).flatMap((name) => {
      const path = join(directory, name);

      return statSync(path).isDirectory() ? walk(path) : [path];
    });

  it("App and Preview never build their own map: only the hook (and the interim render path) call storyAudioDurations", () => {
    const callers = walk("src/web")
      .filter((path) => /\.(ts|tsx)$/u.test(path))
      .filter((path) => /\bstoryAudioDurations\(/u.test(readFileSync(path, "utf8")))
      .map((path) => path.replaceAll("\\", "/"))
      .sort();

    assert.deepEqual(callers, ["src/web/browserRender.ts", "src/web/useStoryAudioDurations.ts"]);
  });

  it("App passes the hook's map to the Player config and to Preview", () => {
    const app = readFileSync("src/web/App.tsx", "utf8");

    assert.match(app, /const audioDurations = useStoryAudioDurations\(/u);
    assert.match(app, /getWebPlayerConfig\(activeStory, audioDurations\)/u);
    assert.match(app, /audioDurations=\{audioDurations\}/u);
  });
});
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement the hook**

`src/web/useStoryAudioDurations.ts`:

```ts
import {useEffect, useMemo, useState} from "react";
import type {AudioDurationMap} from "../audio/catalog.ts";
import {storyAudioDurations, storyAudioRefs} from "../audio/storyAudio.ts";
import {isLocalAudioRef, type LocalAudioRef} from "../localAssets/refs.ts";
import type {Story} from "../story/types.ts";
import type {AssetLibraryStatus} from "./assetLibrary/coordination.ts";
import {readAudioDurations} from "./assetLibrary/library.ts";

const NO_DURATIONS: AudioDurationMap = Object.freeze({});

/** The Story's distinct local audio refs, sorted, comma-joined: the effect key (`""` when none). */
export const localAudioRefsKey = (story: Story): string =>
  storyAudioRefs(story).filter(isLocalAudioRef).sort().join(",");

export const parseLocalAudioRefsKey = (key: string): LocalAudioRef[] =>
  key === "" ? [] : (key.split(",") as LocalAudioRef[]);

/** Entry-wise equality, so an unchanged library keeps the previous object (and every memo built on it). */
export const sameDurationMap = (
  a: AudioDurationMap,
  b: AudioDurationMap,
): boolean => {
  const first = Object.entries(a);

  return (
    first.length === Object.keys(b).length &&
    first.every(([ref, seconds]) => (b as Record<string, number | undefined>)[ref] === seconds)
  );
};

/**
 * The page's ONE audio duration map: bundled durations from the catalog plus the
 * durations of the Story's own local audio refs, read from the library (never
 * from the visible library page). Recomputed when the Story's set of local audio
 * refs changes or `refreshToken` bumps (a library mutation in this or another
 * tab, or window focus). Refs that are absent from the library stay out of the
 * map and keep resolving as missing.
 */
export const useStoryAudioDurations = (
  story: Story,
  library: AssetLibraryStatus | null,
  refreshToken: number,
): AudioDurationMap => {
  const refsKey = localAudioRefsKey(story);
  const store = library?.kind === "ready" ? library.store : null;
  const [local, setLocal] = useState<AudioDurationMap>(NO_DURATIONS);

  useEffect(() => {
    const refs = parseLocalAudioRefsKey(refsKey);
    let cancelled = false;
    const settle = (next: AudioDurationMap) => {
      if (!cancelled) {
        setLocal((current) => (sameDurationMap(current, next) ? current : next));
      }
    };

    if (store === null || refs.length === 0) {
      settle(NO_DURATIONS);

      return;
    }

    void readAudioDurations(store, refs).then(settle, () =>
      settle(NO_DURATIONS),
    );

    return () => {
      cancelled = true;
    };
  }, [store, refsKey, refreshToken]);

  return useMemo(() => storyAudioDurations(story, local), [story, local]);
};
```
