### Task 9: Audio import pipeline (inspect, decode, hash, commit) and flows

An `audio` branch of the import: size gate (before any read), read once, `inspectAudio`, `decodeAudioPeaks` + the truncation rule, hash, then the existing exclusive-lock commit (lock wait/cancel, `onLockAcquired`, channel announcement, persistent-storage request). The matching-file flow works for audio; a different audio file is refused with the mismatch text (the replacement dialog needs scene-field pickers, which AUDIO-004 owns).

**Files:**
- Create: `src/web/assetLibrary/importCommon.ts`, `src/web/assetLibrary/importAudio.ts`, `tests/audio-import.test.mjs`
- Modify: `src/web/assetLibrary/importAsset.ts`, `src/web/localAssetImportFlow.ts`, `src/web/useLocalAssetImport.ts`, `src/web/localAssetUi.ts` (`mismatchDialogText`), `src/web/components/MyAssetsSection.tsx` (boundary cast)

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `inspectAudio`, `AudioMetadata`, `audioMimeTypeFor`, `AUDIO_EMPTY_MESSAGE`, `AUDIO_TOO_LARGE_MESSAGE` (Task 2); `checkLocalAssetByteSize(size, "audio")` (Task 3); `isDecodeComplete`, `decodeAudioPeaks`, `DecodedAudioSummary` (Task 4); `AudioPayloadMetadata` (Task 5); `WaveformRecord`, `import-audio` mutation (Task 6); widened flow types (Task 6).
- Produces:
  - `src/web/assetLibrary/importCommon.ts`: `type ImportPhase = "reading" | "validating" | "decoding" | "hashing" | "storing"`, `type LocalAssetImportErrorCode` (the v0.3 codes plus `"audio-rejected" | "audio-decode-failed" | "audio-truncated"`), `class LocalAssetImportError`, `buildDefaultLabel(filename: string, category: LocalAssetCategory): string` (moved from `importAsset.ts`, same behaviour; `importAsset.ts` re-exports `LocalAssetImportError`, `ImportPhase`, `LocalAssetImportErrorCode`).
  - `src/web/assetLibrary/importAudio.ts`: `type PreparedLocalAudioImport = {ref: LocalAudioRef; defaultLabel: string; originalFilename: string; payloadMeta: AudioPayloadMetadata; blob: Blob; waveform: WaveformRecord}`, `type PrepareLocalAudioImportDeps = {subtle?: SubtleCrypto; inspectAudio?: (bytes: Uint8Array) => Promise<AudioInspection>; decodePeaks?: (blob: Blob, metadata: AudioMetadata) => Promise<DecodedAudioSummary>; onPhase?: (phase: ImportPhase) => void}`, `prepareLocalAudioImport(file: File, deps?: PrepareLocalAudioImportDeps): Promise<PreparedLocalAudioImport>`, `AUDIO_DECODE_FAILED_MESSAGE = "This audio file could not be decoded."`, `AUDIO_TRUNCATED_MESSAGE = "This audio file looks truncated."`.
  - `src/web/assetLibrary/importAsset.ts`: `PreparedLocalImageImport` (the v0.3 `PreparedLocalAssetImport` shape), `PreparedLocalAssetImport = PreparedLocalImageImport | PreparedLocalAudioImport`; `prepareLocalAssetImport` overloads `(file, category: ImageAssetCategory, deps?) => Promise<PreparedLocalImageImport>`, `(file, category: "audio", deps?) => Promise<PreparedLocalAudioImport>`, `(file, category: LocalAssetCategory, deps?) => Promise<PreparedLocalAssetImport>`; `commitPreparedLocalAssetImport` overloads `(prepared: PreparedLocalImageImport, library, deps?) => Promise<{ref: LocalImageRef; created: boolean}>`, `(prepared: PreparedLocalAudioImport, ...) => Promise<{ref: LocalAudioRef; created: boolean}>`, `(prepared: PreparedLocalAssetImport, ...) => Promise<{ref: LocalAssetRef; created: boolean}>`; `importLocalAsset(file, category: LocalAssetCategory, library, deps?)`. `PrepareLocalAssetImportDeps` gains the optional `inspectAudio` and `decodePeaks`.
  - `src/web/localAssetImportFlow.ts`: `importPhaseText("decoding") === "Decoding…"`, `importSuccessText(label: string, created: boolean, category?: LocalAssetCategory): string` (audio: `"<label>" was already in My audio; its stored copy was refreshed.`; the success text is unchanged), `prepareImportFile`, `commitPreparedImport`, `runLocalAssetImport`, `runMatchingFileImport` take `LocalAssetCategory`/`LocalAssetRef`.

`tests/audio-import.test.mjs`:

```js
import assert from "node:assert/strict";
import {describe, it} from "node:test";
import {sha256Hex} from "../src/localAssets/hash.ts";
import {
  AUDIO_CHANNELS_MESSAGE,
  AUDIO_EMPTY_MESSAGE,
  AUDIO_TOO_LARGE_MESSAGE,
  AUDIO_TOO_LONG_MESSAGE,
  AUDIO_UNSUPPORTED_MESSAGE,
  AUDIO_VIDEO_MESSAGE,
} from "../src/localAssets/audioInspection.ts";
import {buildLocalAssetRef} from "../src/localAssets/refs.ts";
import {
  commitPreparedLocalAssetImport,
  importLocalAsset,
  LocalAssetImportError,
  prepareLocalAssetImport,
} from "../src/web/assetLibrary/importAsset.ts";
import {
  AUDIO_DECODE_FAILED_MESSAGE,
  AUDIO_TRUNCATED_MESSAGE,
} from "../src/web/assetLibrary/importAudio.ts";
import {
  importPhaseText,
  importSuccessText,
  runLocalAssetImport,
  runMatchingFileImport,
} from "../src/web/localAssetImportFlow.ts";
import {buildWav, fixtureBytes} from "./helpers/audioBytes.mjs";
import {
  createMemoryAssetStore,
  createMemoryLockManager,
} from "./helpers/memoryAssetStore.mjs";

const MIB = 1024 * 1024;
const fileOf = (name, bytes) => new File([bytes], name);
const fixtureFile = (name) => fileOf(name, fixtureBytes(name));
const complete = (seconds) => async () => ({
  decodedSeconds: seconds,
  peaks: Uint8Array.from([1, 2, 3, 255]),
  peaksPerSecond: 50,
});
const makeLibrary = () => {
  const posted = [];

  return {
    store: createMemoryAssetStore(),
    locks: createMemoryLockManager(),
    posted,
    channel: {post: (message) => posted.push(message)},
  };
};
const spies = () => {
  const calls = {decode: 0, digest: 0};

  return {
    calls,
    deps: {
      decodePeaks: async (...args) => {
        calls.decode += 1;
        return complete(2.2)(...args);
      },
      subtle: {
        digest: (algorithm, data) => {
          calls.digest += 1;
          return globalThis.crypto.subtle.digest(algorithm, data);
        },
      },
    },
  };
};
const rejection = async (promise) => {
  try {
    await promise;
  } catch (error) {
    assert.ok(error instanceof LocalAssetImportError, String(error));

    return error;
  }

  assert.fail("expected a LocalAssetImportError");
};

describe("prepareLocalAssetImport for audio", () => {
  it("inspects, decodes and hashes the same bytes and builds the prepared import in phase order", async () => {
    const phases = [];
    const bytes = fixtureBytes("tone-440-2.2s.wav");
    const prepared = await prepareLocalAssetImport(fileOf("My voice.wav", bytes), "audio", {
      decodePeaks: complete(2.2),
      onPhase: (phase) => phases.push(phase),
    });
    const digest = await sha256Hex(bytes);

    assert.deepEqual(phases, ["reading", "validating", "decoding", "hashing"]);
    assert.equal(prepared.ref, buildLocalAssetRef("audio", digest));
    assert.equal(prepared.defaultLabel, "My voice");
    assert.equal(prepared.originalFilename, "My voice.wav");
    assert.deepEqual(prepared.payloadMeta, {
      kind: "audio",
      mimeType: "audio/wav",
      byteSize: bytes.length,
      container: "wave",
      codec: "pcm",
      durationSeconds: 2.2,
      sampleRate: 48_000,
      channels: 2,
    });
    assert.deepEqual(prepared.waveform, {peaksPerSecond: 50, peaks: Uint8Array.from([1, 2, 3, 255])});
    assert.equal(prepared.blob.type, "audio/wav");
    assert.deepEqual(new Uint8Array(await prepared.blob.arrayBuffer()), bytes);
  });

  it("chooses the payload MIME type from the inspected container, never from the file's own type", async () => {
    const file = new File([fixtureBytes("tone-440-2.2s.opus.ogg")], "voice.mp3", {type: "audio/mpeg"});
    const prepared = await prepareLocalAssetImport(file, "audio", {decodePeaks: complete(2.2135)});

    assert.equal(prepared.payloadMeta.mimeType, "audio/ogg");
    assert.equal(prepared.payloadMeta.container, "ogg");
    assert.equal(prepared.blob.type, "audio/ogg");
    assert.equal(prepared.defaultLabel, "voice");
  });

  it("really decodes a PCM WAV in Node and returns peaks near full scale", async () => {
    const prepared = await prepareLocalAssetImport(fixtureFile("tone-440-2.2s.wav"), "audio");

    assert.equal(prepared.waveform.peaks.length, 111);
    assert.equal(Math.min(...prepared.waveform.peaks) >= 250, true);
    assert.equal(prepared.waveform.peaksPerSecond, 111 / 2.2);
  });

  it("falls back to 'Untitled audio' and caps long names at 80 code points", async () => {
    const dotted = await prepareLocalAssetImport(fileOf(".wav", fixtureBytes("tone-880-0.4s.wav")), "audio", {decodePeaks: complete(0.4)});
    const long = await prepareLocalAssetImport(fileOf(`${"x".repeat(100)}.wav`, fixtureBytes("tone-880-0.4s.wav")), "audio", {decodePeaks: complete(0.4)});

    assert.equal(dotted.defaultLabel, "Untitled audio");
    assert.equal(long.defaultLabel, "x".repeat(80));
  });

  it("accepts a 30 MiB WAV (the audio limit) and rejects 51 MiB without reading or hashing a byte", async () => {
    const big = buildWav({dataBytes: 30 * MIB - 44});
    const accepted = await prepareLocalAssetImport(fileOf("big.wav", big), "audio", {decodePeaks: complete(163.84)});

    assert.equal(accepted.payloadMeta.byteSize, 30 * MIB);

    const huge = fileOf("huge.wav", buildWav({dataBytes: 51 * MIB - 44}));
    const state = {reads: 0};
    const { calls, deps } = spies();

    huge.arrayBuffer = async () => {
      state.reads += 1;
      return new ArrayBuffer(0);
    };

    const error = await rejection(prepareLocalAssetImport(huge, "audio", deps));

    assert.equal(error.code, "too-large");
    assert.equal(error.message, AUDIO_TOO_LARGE_MESSAGE);
    assert.equal(state.reads, 0);
    assert.deepEqual(calls, {decode: 0, digest: 0});
  });

  it("an empty file is empty and is never read", async () => {
    const error = await rejection(prepareLocalAssetImport(fileOf("empty.wav", new Uint8Array(0)), "audio", spies().deps));

    assert.equal(error.code, "empty");
    assert.equal(error.message, AUDIO_EMPTY_MESSAGE);
  });

  for (const [name, message] of [
    ["not-audio.txt", AUDIO_UNSUPPORTED_MESSAGE],
    ["with-video.mp4", AUDIO_VIDEO_MESSAGE],
    ["long-301s.mp3", AUDIO_TOO_LONG_MESSAGE],
    ["three-channel.wav", "Audio must be mono or stereo."],
    ["zero-channel.wav", "Audio must be mono or stereo."],
  ]) {
    it(`rejects ${name} with its message, before any decode or hash`, async () => {
      const { calls, deps } = spies();
      const error = await rejection(prepareLocalAssetImport(fixtureFile(name), "audio", deps));

      assert.equal(error.code, "audio-rejected");
      assert.equal(error.message, message);
      assert.deepEqual(calls, {decode: 0, digest: 0});
    });
  }

  it("a decoder failure is 'could not be decoded' and nothing is hashed", async () => {
    const { calls, deps } = spies();
    const error = await rejection(
      prepareLocalAssetImport(fixtureFile("tone-440-2.2s.mp3"), "audio", {
        ...deps,
        decodePeaks: async () => {
          throw new Error("codec unsupported");
        },
      }),
    );

    assert.equal(error.code, "audio-decode-failed");
    assert.equal(error.message, AUDIO_DECODE_FAILED_MESSAGE);
    assert.equal(error.message, "This audio file could not be decoded.");
    assert.equal(calls.digest, 0);
  });

  it("the truncation rule rejects a short decode and a zero-sample decode", async () => {
    const truncated = await rejection(
      prepareLocalAssetImport(fixtureFile("truncated.mp3"), "audio", {decodePeaks: complete(5.98)}),
    );
    const silent = await rejection(
      prepareLocalAssetImport(fixtureFile("tone-880-0.4s.wav"), "audio", {decodePeaks: complete(0)}),
    );

    assert.equal(truncated.code, "audio-truncated");
    assert.equal(truncated.message, AUDIO_TRUNCATED_MESSAGE);
    assert.equal(truncated.message, "This audio file looks truncated.");
    assert.equal(silent.code, "audio-truncated");
    assert.equal((await prepareLocalAssetImport(fixtureFile("truncated.mp3"), "audio", {decodePeaks: complete(9.9)})).payloadMeta.durationSeconds > 10, true);
  });

  it("an unreadable file and a failing digest are import errors with audio wording", async () => {
    const unreadable = fixtureFile("tone-440-2.2s.wav");

    unreadable.arrayBuffer = async () => {
      throw new Error("disk");
    };

    assert.equal((await rejection(prepareLocalAssetImport(unreadable, "audio"))).message, "The file could not be read.");

    const broken = await rejection(
      prepareLocalAssetImport(fixtureFile("tone-440-2.2s.wav"), "audio", {
        decodePeaks: complete(2.2),
        subtle: {digest: async () => { throw new Error("no crypto"); }},
      }),
    );

    assert.equal(broken.code, "storage-error");
    assert.equal(broken.message, "The audio file could not be fingerprinted in this browser.");
  });
});

describe("committing an audio import", () => {
  const prepare = (name = "tone-440-2.2s.wav", duration = 2.2) =>
    prepareLocalAssetImport(fixtureFile(name), "audio", {decodePeaks: complete(duration)});

  it("writes row, metadata, bytes and waveform in one apply and announces the change", async () => {
    const library = makeLibrary();
    const prepared = await prepare();
    const digest = prepared.ref.slice(-64);
    const result = await commitPreparedLocalAssetImport(prepared, library);

    assert.deepEqual(result, {ref: prepared.ref, created: true});
    assert.equal(library.store.applyCalls.length, 1);
    assert.equal(library.store.applyCalls[0].kind, "import-audio");
    assert.equal(library.store.getRaw("assets", prepared.ref).label, "tone-440-2.2s");
    assert.equal(library.store.getRaw("payloadMeta", digest).kind, "audio");
    assert.equal(library.store.hasRaw("blobs", digest), true);
    assert.deepEqual(Object.keys(library.store.getRaw("waveforms", digest)).sort(), ["peaks", "peaksPerSecond"]);
    assert.equal(library.store.stores.thumbnails.size, 0);
    assert.deepEqual(library.posted, [{type: "asset-library-changed", refs: [prepared.ref], digests: [digest]}]);
    assert.deepEqual(await commitPreparedLocalAssetImport(prepared, library), {ref: prepared.ref, created: false});
  });

  it("an aborted signal cancels the wait for the lock and writes nothing", async () => {
    const library = makeLibrary();
    const prepared = await prepare();
    const controller = new AbortController();

    controller.abort();

    const error = await rejection(commitPreparedLocalAssetImport(prepared, library, {signal: controller.signal}));

    assert.equal(error.code, "cancelled");
    assert.equal(library.store.applyCalls.length, 0);
  });

  it("maps quota and other storage failures to audio-worded errors", async () => {
    const library = makeLibrary();
    const prepared = await prepare();

    library.store.failNextApply();

    const full = await rejection(commitPreparedLocalAssetImport(prepared, library));

    assert.equal(full.code, "storage-full");
    assert.equal(full.message, "There is not enough browser storage left for this audio file.");

    library.store.failNextApply(new Error("disk"));

    const failed = await rejection(commitPreparedLocalAssetImport(prepared, library));

    assert.equal(failed.code, "storage-error");
    assert.equal(failed.message, "The audio file could not be saved to browser storage.");
  });

  it("importLocalAsset runs prepare, 'storing' and commit", async () => {
    const library = makeLibrary();
    const phases = [];
    const result = await importLocalAsset(fixtureFile("tone-440-2.2s.wav"), "audio", library, {
      decodePeaks: complete(2.2),
      onPhase: (phase) => phases.push(phase),
    });

    assert.equal(result.created, true);
    assert.deepEqual(phases, ["reading", "validating", "decoding", "hashing", "storing"]);
  });
});

describe("audio in the import flows", () => {
  it("has the copy of the spec", () => {
    assert.equal(importPhaseText("decoding"), "Decoding…");
    assert.equal(importSuccessText("Voice", true, "audio"), 'Imported "Voice".');
    assert.equal(importSuccessText("Voice", false, "audio"), '"Voice" was already in My audio; its stored copy was refreshed.');
    assert.equal(importSuccessText("Hero", false), '"Hero" was already in My assets; its stored copy was refreshed.');
    assert.equal(importSuccessText("Hero", false, "pose"), '"Hero" was already in My assets; its stored copy was refreshed.');
  });

  it("runLocalAssetImport imports, deduplicates and applies the ref after the commit", async () => {
    const library = makeLibrary();
    const inFlight = [];
    const applied = [];
    const run = () =>
      runLocalAssetImport({
        file: fixtureFile("tone-440-2.2s.wav"),
        category: "audio",
        library,
        setInFlight: (value) => inFlight.push(value),
        apply: (ref) => applied.push(ref),
        deps: {decodePeaks: complete(2.2)},
      });
    const first = await run();
    const second = await run();

    assert.equal(first.ok, true);
    assert.equal(first.message, 'Imported "tone-440-2.2s".');
    assert.equal(second.ok, true);
    assert.equal(second.created, false);
    assert.equal(second.message, '"tone-440-2.2s" was already in My audio; its stored copy was refreshed.');
    assert.deepEqual(inFlight, [true, false, true, false]);
    assert.equal(applied.length, 2);
  });

  it("a rejected file leaves the store untouched and releases the lock", async () => {
    const library = makeLibrary();
    const inFlight = [];
    const result = await runLocalAssetImport({
      file: fixtureFile("three-channel.wav"),
      category: "audio",
      library,
      setInFlight: (value) => inFlight.push(value),
      apply: () => assert.fail("must not apply"),
    });

    assert.deepEqual([result.ok, result.message], [false, AUDIO_CHANNELS_MESSAGE]);
    assert.deepEqual(inFlight, [true, false]);
    assert.equal(library.store.applyCalls.length, 0);
  });

  it("Import matching file: the exact file restores the ref; a different audio file is refused with the mismatch text and writes nothing", async () => {
    const wav = fixtureBytes("tone-440-2.2s.wav");
    const missingRef = buildLocalAssetRef("audio", await sha256Hex(wav));
    const library = makeLibrary();
    const dialogs = [];
    const run = (file) =>
      runMatchingFileImport({
        file,
        category: "audio",
        missingRef,
        library,
        setInFlight: () => {},
        openMismatchDialog: (dialog) => {
          dialogs.push(dialog);
          return true;
        },
        apply: () => assert.fail("must not apply"),
        deps: {decodePeaks: complete(2.2)},
      });
    const different = await run(fixtureFile("tone-880-0.4s.wav"));

    assert.equal(different.kind, "failed");
    assert.match(different.message, /^This file is a different audio file \([0-9a-f]{4}…[0-9a-f]{4}\), so it can't restore the missing one\.$/u);
    assert.equal(dialogs.length, 0);
    assert.equal(library.store.applyCalls.length, 0);

    const restored = await run(fixtureFile("tone-440-2.2s.wav"));

    assert.equal(restored.kind, "restored");
    assert.equal(restored.ref, missingRef);
    assert.equal(restored.message, 'Imported "tone-440-2.2s".');
    assert.equal(library.store.applyCalls.length, 1);
  });
});
```

(`rejection` expects a thrown `LocalAssetImportError`; `fixtureFile` creates a `File` from the committed bytes. Phases for a non-audio category are unchanged: the image tests in `tests/asset-library.test.mjs` and `tests/local-asset-flows.test.mjs` stay as they are.)

`src/web/assetLibrary/importCommon.ts`:

```ts
import type {ImageRejection} from "../../localAssets/imageInspection.ts";
import type {LocalAssetCategory} from "../../localAssets/refs.ts";
import {MAX_LOCAL_ASSET_LABEL_LENGTH} from "./constants.ts";

export type ImportPhase =
  | "reading"
  | "validating"
  | "decoding"
  | "hashing"
  | "storing";

export type LocalAssetImportErrorCode =
  | ImageRejection
  | "decode-failed"
  | "thumbnail-failed"
  | "audio-rejected"
  | "audio-decode-failed"
  | "audio-truncated"
  | "storage-full"
  | "storage-error"
  /** The user cancelled while the commit was still waiting for the library lock; nothing was written. */
  | "cancelled";

export class LocalAssetImportError extends Error {
  code: LocalAssetImportErrorCode;
  cause?: unknown;

  constructor(
    code: LocalAssetImportErrorCode,
    message: string,
    options?: {cause?: unknown},
  ) {
    super(message);
    this.name = "LocalAssetImportError";
    this.code = code;

    if (options?.cause !== undefined) {
      this.cause = options.cause;
    }
  }
}

/**
 * D-12: filename without its last extension, trimmed, cut to the label limit in
 * code points; falls back to "Untitled <category>" when nothing is left.
 */
export const buildDefaultLabel = (
  filename: string,
  category: LocalAssetCategory,
): string => {
  const dot = filename.lastIndexOf(".");
  const stem = dot === -1 ? filename : filename.slice(0, dot);
  const label = Array.from(stem.trim())
    .slice(0, MAX_LOCAL_ASSET_LABEL_LENGTH)
    .join("")
    .trim();

  return label === "" ? `Untitled ${category}` : label;
};
```

`src/web/assetLibrary/importAudio.ts`:

```ts
import {isDecodeComplete} from "../../audio/decodeCheck.ts";
import {
  AUDIO_EMPTY_MESSAGE,
  AUDIO_TOO_LARGE_MESSAGE,
  audioMimeTypeFor,
  inspectAudio,
  type AudioInspection,
  type AudioMetadata,
} from "../../localAssets/audioInspection.ts";
import {sha256Hex} from "../../localAssets/hash.ts";
import {checkLocalAssetByteSize} from "../../localAssets/imageInspection.ts";
import type {AudioPayloadMetadata} from "../../localAssets/readiness.ts";
import {buildLocalAssetRef, type LocalAudioRef} from "../../localAssets/refs.ts";
import {decodeAudioPeaks, type DecodedAudioSummary} from "./audioPeaks.ts";
import {
  buildDefaultLabel,
  LocalAssetImportError,
  type ImportPhase,
} from "./importCommon.ts";
import type {WaveformRecord} from "./records.ts";

export const AUDIO_DECODE_FAILED_MESSAGE =
  "This audio file could not be decoded.";
export const AUDIO_TRUNCATED_MESSAGE = "This audio file looks truncated.";

export type PreparedLocalAudioImport = {
  ref: LocalAudioRef;
  defaultLabel: string;
  originalFilename: string;
  payloadMeta: AudioPayloadMetadata;
  blob: Blob;
  waveform: WaveformRecord;
};

export type PrepareLocalAudioImportDeps = {
  subtle?: SubtleCrypto;
  inspectAudio?: (bytes: Uint8Array) => Promise<AudioInspection>;
  decodePeaks?: (
    blob: Blob,
    metadata: AudioMetadata,
  ) => Promise<DecodedAudioSummary>;
  onPhase?: (phase: ImportPhase) => void;
};

/**
 * Everything that can fail before a write: size gate (before any byte is
 * read), one read, container inspection, one full decode (peaks and the
 * truncation rule) and hashing. The bytes inspected, decoded, hashed and stored
 * are the same bytes. Takes no lock and writes nothing. Throws LocalAssetImportError.
 */
export const prepareLocalAudioImport = async (
  file: File,
  deps: PrepareLocalAudioImportDeps = {},
): Promise<PreparedLocalAudioImport> => {
  // 1. Bound the size before any bytes are read.
  const size = checkLocalAssetByteSize(file.size, "audio");

  if (!size.ok) {
    throw new LocalAssetImportError(
      size.reason,
      size.reason === "empty" ? AUDIO_EMPTY_MESSAGE : AUDIO_TOO_LARGE_MESSAGE,
    );
  }

  // 2. Read once.
  deps.onPhase?.("reading");

  let bytes: Uint8Array;

  try {
    bytes = new Uint8Array(await file.arrayBuffer());
  } catch (error) {
    throw new LocalAssetImportError(
      "malformed",
      "The file could not be read.",
      {cause: error},
    );
  }

  // 3. Container metadata (AINV-7), by content and never by extension or MIME.
  deps.onPhase?.("validating");

  const inspection = await (deps.inspectAudio ?? inspectAudio)(bytes);

  if (!inspection.ok) {
    throw new LocalAssetImportError("audio-rejected", inspection.message);
  }

  const {metadata} = inspection;
  const mimeType = audioMimeTypeFor(metadata.container);
  const blob = new Blob([bytes as BlobPart], {type: mimeType});

  // 4. One full decode: waveform peaks and the D-9 truncation rule.
  deps.onPhase?.("decoding");

  let summary: DecodedAudioSummary;

  try {
    summary = await (deps.decodePeaks ?? decodeAudioPeaks)(blob, metadata);
  } catch (error) {
    throw new LocalAssetImportError(
      "audio-decode-failed",
      AUDIO_DECODE_FAILED_MESSAGE,
      {cause: error},
    );
  }

  if (!isDecodeComplete(summary.decodedSeconds, metadata.durationSeconds)) {
    throw new LocalAssetImportError("audio-truncated", AUDIO_TRUNCATED_MESSAGE);
  }

  // 5. Identity (INV-3): hash of the original bytes.
  deps.onPhase?.("hashing");

  let digest: string;

  try {
    digest = await sha256Hex(bytes, deps.subtle);
  } catch (error) {
    // An environment failure (for example no SubtleCrypto), not a file problem.
    throw new LocalAssetImportError(
      "storage-error",
      "The audio file could not be fingerprinted in this browser.",
      {cause: error},
    );
  }

  return {
    ref: buildLocalAssetRef("audio", digest),
    defaultLabel: buildDefaultLabel(file.name, "audio"),
    originalFilename: file.name,
    payloadMeta: {
      kind: "audio",
      mimeType,
      byteSize: bytes.length,
      container: metadata.container,
      codec: metadata.codec,
      durationSeconds: metadata.durationSeconds,
      sampleRate: metadata.sampleRate,
      channels: metadata.channels,
    },
    blob,
    waveform: {
      peaksPerSecond: summary.peaksPerSecond,
      peaks: summary.peaks,
    },
  };
};
```

4. Replace `toStorageError` with the noun-aware version:

```ts
const toStorageError = (
  error: unknown,
  noun: "image" | "audio file",
): LocalAssetImportError =>
  isStorageFull(error)
    ? new LocalAssetImportError(
        "storage-full",
        `There is not enough browser storage left for this ${noun}.`,
        {cause: error},
      )
    : new LocalAssetImportError(
        "storage-error",
        `The ${noun} could not be saved to browser storage.`,
        {cause: error},
      );
```

5. Replace `commitPreparedLocalAssetImport` and `importLocalAsset` with:

```ts
/**
 * Commits an already-prepared import under the exclusive lock and posts the
 * change message. The Story is never touched here.
 */
export function commitPreparedLocalAssetImport(
  prepared: PreparedLocalImageImport,
  library: ImportLibrary,
  deps?: CommitPreparedLocalAssetImportDeps,
): Promise<{ref: LocalImageRef; created: boolean}>;
export function commitPreparedLocalAssetImport(
  prepared: PreparedLocalAudioImport,
  library: ImportLibrary,
  deps?: CommitPreparedLocalAssetImportDeps,
): Promise<{ref: LocalAudioRef; created: boolean}>;
export function commitPreparedLocalAssetImport(
  prepared: PreparedLocalAssetImport,
  library: ImportLibrary,
  deps?: CommitPreparedLocalAssetImportDeps,
): Promise<{ref: LocalAssetRef; created: boolean}>;
export async function commitPreparedLocalAssetImport(
  prepared: PreparedLocalAssetImport,
  library: ImportLibrary,
  deps?: CommitPreparedLocalAssetImportDeps,
): Promise<{ref: LocalAssetRef; created: boolean}> {
  const noun = "waveform" in prepared ? "audio file" : "image";
  let result;
  let lockAcquired = false;

  try {
    result = await withAssetLibraryLock(
      library.locks,
      "exclusive",
      () => {
        lockAcquired = true;
        deps?.onLockAcquired?.();

        const createdAt = new Date().toISOString();

        return library.store.apply(
          "waveform" in prepared
            ? {
                kind: "import-audio",
                ref: prepared.ref,
                defaultLabel: prepared.defaultLabel,
                originalFilename: prepared.originalFilename,
                createdAt,
                payloadMeta: prepared.payloadMeta,
                blob: prepared.blob,
                waveform: prepared.waveform,
              }
            : {
                kind: "import",
                ref: prepared.ref,
                defaultLabel: prepared.defaultLabel,
                originalFilename: prepared.originalFilename,
                createdAt,
                payloadMeta: prepared.payloadMeta,
                blob: prepared.blob,
                thumbnail: prepared.thumbnail,
              },
        );
      },
      deps?.signal === undefined ? undefined : {signal: deps.signal},
    );
  } catch (error) {
    // A rejection while the lock was never granted and the signal is aborted is
    // our own cancel. After the grant an AbortError can only come from the
    // store (an aborted transaction): that stays a storage error.
    if (!lockAcquired && deps?.signal?.aborted === true) {
      throw new LocalAssetImportError("cancelled", "Import cancelled.", {
        cause: error,
      });
    }

    throw toStorageError(error, noun);
  }

  if (result.kind !== "imported") {
    throw new LocalAssetImportError(
      "storage-error",
      `The ${noun} could not be saved to browser storage.`,
    );
  }

  try {
    library.channel.post({
      type: "asset-library-changed",
      refs: [prepared.ref],
      digests: [prepared.ref.slice(prepared.ref.lastIndexOf(":") + 1)],
    });
  } catch {
    // The channel is a best-effort refresh signal; the import succeeded.
  }

  // Fire and forget: persist() can sit on a permission prompt (Firefox) and
  // must never delay a successful import. It never rejects.
  void requestPersistentStorageOnce(deps);

  return {ref: prepared.ref, created: result.created};
}

/** prepareLocalAssetImport + onPhase("storing") + commitPreparedLocalAssetImport. */
export const importLocalAsset = async (
  file: File,
  category: LocalAssetCategory,
  library: ImportLibrary,
  deps: PrepareLocalAssetImportDeps & {storage?: StorageManager} = {},
): Promise<{ref: LocalAssetRef; created: boolean}> => {
  const prepared = await prepareLocalAssetImport(file, category, deps);

  deps.onPhase?.("storing");

  return commitPreparedLocalAssetImport(prepared, library, deps);
};
```

Add `LocalAssetCategory`, `LocalAssetRef`, `LocalAudioRef` to the refs import. The existing image tests keep passing: image messages are the same strings (`noun` "image").

`tests/audio-ui.test.mjs`:

```js
import assert from "node:assert/strict";
import {describe, it} from "node:test";
import {formatAudioDuration} from "../src/audio/formatDuration.ts";
import {
  audioRefUsesMusic,
  deleteConfirmationText,
  deleteConfirmButtonLabel,
  describeMissingLocalAsset,
  fileAcceptFor,
  mismatchDialogText,
  sceneNumbersUsingAudioRef,
  usedByScenesText,
} from "../src/web/localAssetUi.ts";

const ref = (n) => `local:audio:sha256:${n.toString(16).padStart(64, "0")}`;
const poseRef = `local:pose:sha256:${"1".padStart(64, "0")}`;

describe("formatAudioDuration", () => {
  it("rounds to whole seconds, never shows 0:00 for a positive duration, and handles bad input", () => {
    const cases = [
      [0.12, "0:01"], [2.2, "0:02"], [2.6, "0:03"], [59.6, "1:00"], [125, "2:05"],
      [163.84, "2:44"], [300, "5:00"], [0, "0:00"], [-1, "0:00"], [Number.NaN, "0:00"], [Number.POSITIVE_INFINITY, "0:00"],
    ];

    for (const [seconds, text] of cases) {
      assert.equal(formatAudioDuration(seconds), text, String(seconds));
    }
  });
});

describe("audio usage in the Story", () => {
  const story = {
    music: {src: ref(1)},
    scenes: [{sfx: {src: ref(2)}}, {voice: {src: ref(2)}}, {sfx: {src: ref(3)}, voice: {src: ref(2)}}, {}],
  };

  it("counts scenes by sfx or voice (any audio role except the Story's music)", () => {
    assert.deepEqual(sceneNumbersUsingAudioRef(story, ref(2)), [1, 2, 3]);
    assert.deepEqual(sceneNumbersUsingAudioRef(story, ref(3)), [3]);
    assert.deepEqual(sceneNumbersUsingAudioRef(story, ref(1)), []);
    assert.equal(audioRefUsesMusic(story, ref(1)), true);
    assert.equal(audioRefUsesMusic(story, ref(2)), false);
    assert.equal(audioRefUsesMusic({scenes: []}, ref(1)), false);
  });
});

describe("copy for audio", () => {
  it("usedByScenesText mentions the music and leaves the image text alone", () => {
    assert.equal(usedByScenesText([]), "Not used by any scene in this Story.");
    assert.equal(usedByScenesText([1, 3]), "Used by scenes 1, 3.");
    assert.equal(usedByScenesText([2]), "Used by scene 2.");
    assert.equal(usedByScenesText([], true), "Used by the music.");
    assert.equal(usedByScenesText([2], true), "Used by scene 2 and the music.");
    assert.equal(usedByScenesText([1, 3], true), "Used by scenes 1, 3 and the music.");
  });

  it("deleteConfirmationText: images unchanged, audio counts scenes and the music", () => {
    assert.equal(deleteConfirmationText("Hero", "pose", 0), 'Delete "Hero" (pose) from My assets?');
    assert.match(deleteConfirmationText("Hero", "pose", 2), /^"Hero" \(pose\) is used by 2 scenes in this Story\. Deleting it leaves those scenes with a missing local asset/u);
    assert.equal(deleteConfirmationText("Voice", "audio", 0), 'Delete "Voice" (audio) from My audio?');
    assert.equal(deleteConfirmationText("Voice", "audio", 0, false), 'Delete "Voice" (audio) from My audio?');

    const tail = "Deleting it removes that sound from the preview, and MP4 rendering stays blocked until you re-import the same file or choose a replacement. Other exported YAML files may also use it.";

    assert.equal(deleteConfirmationText("Voice", "audio", 2), `"Voice" (audio) is used by 2 scenes in this Story. ${tail}`);
    assert.equal(deleteConfirmationText("Voice", "audio", 1), `"Voice" (audio) is used by 1 scene in this Story. ${tail}`);
    assert.equal(deleteConfirmationText("Voice", "audio", 0, true), `"Voice" (audio) is used by the music in this Story. ${tail}`);
    assert.equal(deleteConfirmationText("Voice", "audio", 1, true), `"Voice" (audio) is used by 1 scene and the music in this Story. ${tail}`);
    assert.equal(deleteConfirmButtonLabel(0), "Delete asset");
    assert.equal(deleteConfirmButtonLabel(1), "Delete asset anyway");
  });

  it("describeMissingLocalAsset: audio has no replacement picker yet", () => {
    const audio = describeMissingLocalAsset({ref: ref(5), sceneNumbers: [1, 2], damaged: false, usesMusic: true});

    assert.equal(audio.title, "Missing local audio");
    assert.equal(audio.usedBy, "Used by scenes 1, 2 and the music.");
    assert.equal(audio.hint, "Import the original file to restore it.");
    assert.equal(audio.damagedNote, null);
    assert.equal(describeMissingLocalAsset({ref: ref(5), sceneNumbers: [], damaged: true}).damagedNote, "The stored copy is damaged.");

    const image = describeMissingLocalAsset({ref: poseRef, sceneNumbers: [1], damaged: false});

    assert.equal(image.title, "Missing local pose");
    assert.equal(image.hint, "Import the original file to restore it, or pick any other pose to replace it.");
  });

  it("the mismatch text names the kind of file, and the file pickers accept the right types", () => {
    assert.match(mismatchDialogText(poseRef), /^This file is a different image \(/u);
    assert.match(mismatchDialogText(ref(7)), /^This file is a different audio file \(/u);
    assert.equal(fileAcceptFor("pose"), "image/png,image/jpeg,image/webp");
    assert.match(fileAcceptFor("audio"), /\.mp3.*\.wav.*\.m4a.*\.ogg/u);
  });
});
```

```js
describe("AUDIO-003 audio page", () => {
  const audioMeta = (n) => ({
    kind: "audio", mimeType: "audio/wav", byteSize: 10, container: "wave", codec: "pcm",
    durationSeconds: n + 0.5, sampleRate: 48_000, channels: 2,
  });

  it("lists audio, loads no thumbnail and carries the stored duration of audio entries only", async () => {
    const base = createFakeStore("audio", numbers(3));
    const store = {
      ...base,
      async getPayloadMeta(digest) {
        const n = Number.parseInt(digest, 16);

        if (n === 1) return {status: "present", value: audioMeta(1)};
        if (n === 2) return {status: "present", value: {mimeType: "image/png", byteSize: 1, width: 1, height: 1}};

        return {status: "absent"};
      },
    };
    const created = [];
    let thumbnailLoads = 0;
    const controller = createLocalAssetPageController({
      store,
      category: "audio",
      loadThumbnail: async () => {
        thumbnailLoads += 1;
        return {ok: true, blob: new Blob([])};
      },
      createUrl: (blob) => {
        created.push(blob);
        return "blob:test";
      },
      revokeUrl: () => {},
    });

    await controller.sync(null);

    const {entries, total} = controller.getSnapshot();

    assert.equal(total, 3);
    assert.equal(entries.length, 3);
    assert.equal(thumbnailLoads, 0);
    assert.equal(created.length, 0);
    assert.equal(entries.every((entry) => entry.thumbnailUrl === null), true);
    assert.equal(entries[0].audio.durationSeconds, 1.5);
    assert.equal(entries[1].audio, undefined);
    assert.equal(entries[2].audio, undefined);
    controller.dispose();
  });
});
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement the helpers**

`src/audio/formatDuration.ts`:

```ts
/** "m:ss" for a card: whole seconds, at least 0:01 for any positive duration, 0:00 for bad input. */
export const formatAudioDuration = (seconds: number): string => {
  if (!Number.isFinite(seconds) || seconds <= 0) {
    return "0:00";
  }

  const total = Math.max(1, Math.round(seconds));

  return `${Math.floor(total / 60)}:${String(total % 60).padStart(2, "0")}`;
};
```

```ts
/** What an audio usage count needs from a scene: a Story scene or a visual draft scene both fit. */
export type SceneAudioValues = {
  readonly sfx?: {readonly src: string};
  readonly voice?: {readonly src: string};
};

/** 1-based numbers of the scenes whose sfx or voice equals ref (the Story's music has no scene). */
export const sceneNumbersUsingAudioRef = (
  story: {readonly scenes: readonly SceneAudioValues[]},
  ref: LocalAudioRef,
): number[] =>
  story.scenes.flatMap((scene, index) =>
    scene.sfx?.src === ref || scene.voice?.src === ref ? [index + 1] : [],
  );

export const audioRefUsesMusic = (
  story: {readonly music?: {readonly src: string}},
  ref: LocalAudioRef,
): boolean => story.music?.src === ref;

const IMAGE_FILE_ACCEPT = "image/png,image/jpeg,image/webp";
const AUDIO_FILE_ACCEPT =
  "audio/mpeg,audio/wav,audio/x-wav,audio/mp4,audio/x-m4a,audio/ogg,.mp3,.wav,.m4a,.ogg,.opus";

/** The `accept` attribute of the import inputs. */
export const fileAcceptFor = (category: LocalAssetCategory): string =>
  category === "audio" ? AUDIO_FILE_ACCEPT : IMAGE_FILE_ACCEPT;
```

```ts
export const deleteConfirmationText = (
  label: string,
  category: LocalAssetCategory,
  sceneCount: number,
  usesMusic = false,
): string => {
  if (category === "audio") {
    const usage = [
      ...(sceneCount > 0 ? [`${sceneCount} ${scenesNoun(sceneCount)}`] : []),
      ...(usesMusic ? ["the music"] : []),
    ].join(" and ");

    return usage === ""
      ? `Delete "${label}" (audio) from My audio?`
      : `"${label}" (audio) is used by ${usage} in this Story. Deleting it removes that sound from the preview, and MP4 rendering stays blocked until you re-import the same file or choose a replacement. Other exported YAML files may also use it.`;
  }

  return sceneCount === 0
    ? `Delete "${label}" (${category}) from My assets?`
    : `"${label}" (${category}) is used by ${sceneCount} ${scenesNoun(sceneCount)} in this Story. Deleting it leaves those scenes with a missing local asset, and MP4 rendering stays blocked until you re-import the same file or choose a replacement. Other exported YAML files may also use it.`;
```

```ts
  const withUrl = (entry: LocalAssetEntry): LocalAssetPageEntry => {
    const audio = audioByDigest.get(entry.digest);

    return {
      ...entry,
      thumbnailUrl: urls.get(entry.digest) ?? null,
      ...(audio === undefined ? {} : {audio}),
    };
  };

  // Audio has no thumbnail; its card shows the stored duration instead.
  const wantsThumbnail = (entry: LocalAssetEntry) =>
    category !== "audio" && entry.row.status === "present";

  const loadAudioDetails = async (entries: readonly LocalAssetEntry[]) => {
    if (category !== "audio") {
      return;
    }

    await Promise.all(
      entries.map(async (entry) => {
        if (entry.row.status !== "present" || audioByDigest.has(entry.digest)) {
          return;
        }

        try {
          const meta = await store.getPayloadMeta(entry.digest);

          if (meta.status === "present" && isAudioPayload(meta.value)) {
            audioByDigest.set(entry.digest, meta.value);
          }
        } catch {
          // The card simply shows no duration.
        }
      }),
    );
  };
```

`src/web/components/MyAudioSection.tsx`:

```tsx
import {useContext} from "react";
import type {LocalAudioRef} from "../../localAssets/refs.ts";
import {shortAssetId} from "../../localAssets/sources.ts";
import {AssetLibraryContext} from "../assetLibraryContext.ts";
import type {MutationCancelable} from "../localAssetManageFlow.ts";
import {
  LOCAL_ASSET_LOAD_ERROR,
  type LocalAssetPageEntry,
} from "../localAssetPageController.ts";
import {
  audioRefUsesMusic,
  fileAcceptFor,
  localOnlyDisclosure,
  sceneNumbersUsingAudioRef,
  type SceneAudioValues,
} from "../localAssetUi.ts";
import {useLocalAssetImport} from "../useLocalAssetImport.ts";
import {useLocalAssetManage} from "../useLocalAssetManage.ts";
import {useLocalAssetPage} from "../useLocalAssetPage.ts";
import {LocalAssetCard} from "./LocalAssetCard.tsx";
import {MissingLocalAssetCard} from "./MissingLocalAssetCard.tsx";

type MyAudioSectionProps = {
  /** What the user is looking at: the visual draft's music and scenes (a pending draft is never under-counted). */
  music?: {readonly src: string};
  scenes: readonly SceneAudioValues[];
};

// Nothing is selected from this list yet (AUDIO-004 adds the scene-field pickers).
const ignoreImportedRef = () => {};

/**
 * The minimal My audio list: heading with the total, the local-only
 * disclosure, Import audio, a card per file (label, duration, rename, delete),
 * a missing card for every audio ref of the Story that is not usable here
 * (with Import matching file), and paging.
 */
export const MyAudioSection = ({music, scenes}: MyAudioSectionProps) => {
  const context = useContext(AssetLibraryContext);
  const page = useLocalAssetPage("audio");
  const imports = useLocalAssetImport("audio", ignoreImportedRef);
  const manage = useLocalAssetManage();

  if (context === null) {
    return null;
  }

  const {status, localAssetState, transitionPending} = context;
  const ready = status.kind === "ready";
  const story = {music, scenes};
  const usageOf = (ref: LocalAudioRef) => ({
    sceneNumbers: sceneNumbersUsingAudioRef(story, ref),
    usesMusic: audioRefUsesMusic(story, ref),
  });
  const missing =
    localAssetState.kind === "resolved"
      ? localAssetState.refs.filter(
          (item) => item.usage.category === "audio" && item.status !== "ready",
        )
      : [];

  const cardActions = (entry: LocalAssetPageEntry) => ({
    deleteDisabled: transitionPending,
    onRename: (label: string, onCancelable: MutationCancelable) => {
      imports.clearOutcome();

      return manage.rename(entry.ref, label, onCancelable);
    },
    onDelete: () => {
      const usage = usageOf(entry.ref as LocalAudioRef);

      imports.clearOutcome();
      manage.requestDelete({
        ref: entry.ref,
        label:
          entry.row.status === "present"
            ? entry.row.value.label
            : `Damaged entry ${shortAssetId(entry.ref)}`,
        category: "audio",
        sceneCount: usage.sceneNumbers.length,
        usesMusic: usage.usesMusic,
      });
    },
  });

  return (
    <div className="my-assets my-audio" data-my-audio="">
      <h5>{ready ? `My audio · ${page.total}` : "My audio"}</h5>
      {ready ? (
        <>
          <p className="my-assets-disclosure">
            {localOnlyDisclosure(window.location.origin)}
          </p>
          <div className="my-assets-toolbar">
            <label className="file-button import-asset-button">
              Import audio
              <input
                type="file"
                accept={fileAcceptFor("audio")}
                // Never start an import under a pending App transition panel
                // (an open asset dialog already disables the whole fieldset).
                disabled={transitionPending}
                onChange={(event) => {
                  const file = event.target.files?.[0];

                  if (file !== undefined) {
                    manage.clearNotice();
                    void imports.importFile(file);
                  }

                  // Lets the same file be chosen again.
                  event.target.value = "";
                }}
              />
            </label>
          </div>
          <p className="my-assets-status" role="status">
            {imports.statusText !== ""
              ? imports.statusText
              : (manage.cancelledNotice ?? "")}
          </p>
          {imports.errorText === null ? null : (
            <p className="my-assets-status my-assets-error" role="alert">
              {imports.errorText}
            </p>
          )}
          {manage.notice === null ? null : (
            <p className="my-assets-status my-assets-error" role="alert">
              {manage.notice}
            </p>
          )}
        </>
      ) : (
        <p className="my-assets-status" data-my-assets-status>
          {status.message}
        </p>
      )}

      {missing.length > 0 || (ready && page.entries.length > 0) ? (
        <div className="asset-grid my-assets-grid">
          {missing.map((item) => (
            <MissingLocalAssetCard
              key={item.usage.ref}
              assetRef={item.usage.ref}
              category="audio"
              kind={item.status === "ready" ? "missing" : item.status}
              {...usageOf(item.usage.ref as LocalAudioRef)}
              unavailableMessage={item.detail ?? ""}
              // Only a disabled or unavailable LIBRARY hides the action (R11).
              onImportMatching={
                ready
                  ? (file) => {
                      manage.clearNotice();
                      void imports.importMatchingFile(file, item.usage.ref);
                    }
                  : undefined
              }
              importDisabled={transitionPending}
            />
          ))}
          {ready
            ? page.entries.map((entry) => (
                <LocalAssetCard
                  key={entry.ref}
                  entry={entry}
                  selected={false}
                  canManage
                  {...cardActions(entry)}
                />
              ))
            : null}
        </div>
      ) : null}

      {ready && page.error !== null ? (
        <p className="my-assets-status" role="alert">
          {LOCAL_ASSET_LOAD_ERROR}
        </p>
      ) : null}

      {ready &&
      page.error === null &&
      page.total === 0 &&
      page.loaded &&
      !page.loading ? (
        <p className="my-assets-empty">No imported audio yet.</p>
      ) : null}

      {ready && (page.hasPrevious || page.hasNext) ? (
        <div className="my-assets-paging">
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={page.previous}
            disabled={page.loading || !page.hasPrevious}
          >
            <span aria-hidden="true">‹</span> Previous
          </button>
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={page.next}
            disabled={page.loading || !page.hasNext}
          >
            Next <span aria-hidden="true">›</span>
          </button>
        </div>
      ) : null}
    </div>
  );
};
```

- [ ] **Step 6: Seed helper for audio**

Append to `tests/browser/helpers/seedAssetLibrary.mjs`:

```js
const AUDIO_FIXTURE_DIR = new URL("../../fixtures/local-audio/", import.meta.url);

// PCM WAV fixtures only: their metadata is exact (frames / 48000), so the
// integrity check (SHA-256 plus inspectAudio of the bytes) accepts the record.
const WAV_FIXTURE_SECONDS = {
  "tone-220-10s.wav": 10,
  "tone-440-2.2s.wav": 2.2,
  "tone-880-0.4s.wav": 0.4,
  "tone-220-12s.wav": 12,
};

/** Reads a committed local-audio fixture. */
export const readAudioFixture = async (name) => {
  const bytes = new Uint8Array(await readFile(new URL(name, AUDIO_FIXTURE_DIR)));

  return {
    name,
    bytes,
    base64: Buffer.from(bytes).toString("base64"),
    digest: digestOfBytes(bytes),
  };
};

/** A seed record (row, audio payloadMeta, bytes) for a PCM WAV fixture; the default label is the file name without extension. */
export const audioWavRecord = async (name, options = {}) => {
  const seconds = WAV_FIXTURE_SECONDS[name];

  if (seconds === undefined) {
    throw new Error(`Not a seedable WAV fixture: ${name}`);
  }

  const fixture = await readAudioFixture(name);

  return {
    ref: localAssetRef("audio", fixture.digest),
    digest: fixture.digest,
    meta: {
      kind: "audio",
      mimeType: "audio/wav",
      byteSize: fixture.bytes.length,
      container: "wave",
      codec: "pcm",
      durationSeconds: seconds,
      sampleRate: 48_000,
      channels: 2,
    },
    bytesBase64: fixture.base64,
    mimeType: "audio/wav",
    row: options.row !== false,
    label: options.label ?? name.replace(/\.[^.]+$/u, ""),
    thumbnail: null,
  };
};
```

```js
import {createHash} from "node:crypto";
import {mkdtemp, rm, writeFile} from "node:fs/promises";
import {tmpdir} from "node:os";
import {join} from "node:path";
import {expect, test} from "@playwright/test";
import {buildWav, fixtureBytes} from "../helpers/audioBytes.mjs";
import {
  audioWavRecord,
  deleteAssetRows,
  localAssetRef,
  readAudioFixture,
} from "./helpers/seedAssetLibrary.mjs";
import {applyYaml, openSeeded, waitForOwner} from "./helpers/localAssetPage.mjs";

const MIB = 1024 * 1024;
const section = (page) => page.locator("[data-my-audio]");
const importInput = (page) => section(page).locator('.my-assets-toolbar input[type="file"]');
const statusLine = (page) => section(page).getByRole("status");
const alertLine = (page) => section(page).getByRole("alert");
const cards = (page) => section(page).locator("[data-local-asset-card]");
const cardFor = (page, ref) => page.locator(`[data-local-asset-card="${ref}"]`);
const missingCard = (page, ref) => page.locator(`[data-missing-local-asset-card="${ref}"]`);
const heading = (page, total) => section(page).getByRole("heading", {name: `My audio · ${total}`});
const refOf = (bytes) => localAssetRef("audio", createHash("sha256").update(bytes).digest("hex"));
const upload = (name, bytes, mimeType = "application/octet-stream") => ({name, mimeType, buffer: Buffer.from(bytes)});
const imported = (label) => `Imported "${label}".`;

// Playwright refuses in-memory upload buffers over 50 MiB, so large files are uploaded by path.
const tempDirectories = [];
const tempFile = async (name, bytes) => {
  const directory = await mkdtemp(join(tmpdir(), "tora-my-audio-"));

  tempDirectories.push(directory);

  const path = join(directory, name);

  await writeFile(path, bytes);

  return path;
};

test.afterAll(async () => {
  for (const directory of tempDirectories) {
    await rm(directory, {recursive: true, force: true});
  }
});

const yamlSource = async (page) => {
  await page.getByRole("button", {name: "Open YAML"}).click();

  const source = await page.getByLabel("YAML source").inputValue();

  await page.getByRole("button", {name: "Open visual editor"}).click();

  return source;
};

const audioStoryYaml = ({music, scenes}) =>

    "title: Audio story",

      `    duration: ${duration}`,

test("importing WAV, MP3 and Ogg Opus shows a card per file with its m:ss duration and leaves the Story alone", async ({page}) => {
  await page.goto("/", {waitUntil: "domcontentloaded"});
  await waitForOwner(page);

  const before = await yamlSource(page);
  const files = [
    ["tone-440-2.2s.wav", "tone-440-2.2s", "0:02"],
    ["tone-440-2.2s.mp3", "tone-440-2.2s", "0:02"],
    ["tone-440-2.2s.opus.ogg", "tone-440-2.2s.opus", "0:02"],
    ["tone-220-10s.wav", "tone-220-10s", "0:10"],
  ];

  for (const [index, [name, label, duration]] of files.entries()) {
    const bytes = fixtureBytes(name);
    const ref = refOf(bytes);

    await importInput(page).setInputFiles(upload(name, bytes));
    await expect(statusLine(page)).toHaveText(imported(label));
    await expect(heading(page, index + 1)).toBeVisible();
    await expect(cardFor(page, ref)).toContainText(label);
    await expect(cardFor(page, ref).locator("[data-audio-duration]")).toHaveText(duration);
    await expect(cardFor(page, ref).getByRole("button", {name: `Rename ${label}`})).toBeVisible();
  }

  await expect(cards(page)).toHaveCount(4);
  expect(await yamlSource(page)).toBe(before);
  await expect(page.locator(".app-shell")).toHaveAttribute("data-authoring-locked", "false");
});

test("importing the same file again says it was already in My audio and keeps one card", async ({page}) => {
  await page.goto("/", {waitUntil: "domcontentloaded"});
  await waitForOwner(page);

  const bytes = fixtureBytes("tone-440-2.2s.wav");

  await importInput(page).setInputFiles(upload("tone-440-2.2s.wav", bytes));
  await expect(statusLine(page)).toHaveText(imported("tone-440-2.2s"));
  await importInput(page).setInputFiles(upload("tone-440-2.2s.wav", bytes));
  await expect(statusLine(page)).toHaveText('"tone-440-2.2s" was already in My audio; its stored copy was refreshed.');
  await expect(cards(page)).toHaveCount(1);
  await expect(heading(page, 1)).toBeVisible();
});

test("each rejected file shows its message, stores nothing and changes nothing", async ({page}) => {
  test.setTimeout(60_000);
  await page.goto("/", {waitUntil: "domcontentloaded"});
  await waitForOwner(page);

  const before = await yamlSource(page);
  // Equal messages are never adjacent, so a stale alert cannot satisfy the next assertion.
  const cases = [
    ["not-audio.txt", upload("not-audio.txt", fixtureBytes("not-audio.txt")), "This is not a supported audio file. Use MP3, WAV, M4A or Ogg Opus."],
    ["three-channel.wav", upload("three-channel.wav", fixtureBytes("three-channel.wav")), "Audio must be mono or stereo."],
    ["with-video.mp4", upload("with-video.mp4", fixtureBytes("with-video.mp4")), "Video files are not supported. Use an audio file."],
    ["zero-channel.wav", upload("zero-channel.wav", fixtureBytes("zero-channel.wav")), "Audio must be mono or stereo."],
    ["long-301s.mp3", upload("long-301s.mp3", fixtureBytes("long-301s.mp3")), "Audio can be at most 300 seconds (5 minutes) long."],
    ["truncated.mp3", upload("truncated.mp3", fixtureBytes("truncated.mp3")), "This audio file looks truncated."],
    ["empty.wav", upload("empty.wav", new Uint8Array(0)), "This audio file is empty."],
    ["huge.wav", await tempFile("huge.wav", buildWav({dataBytes: 51 * MIB - 44})), "This audio file is larger than 50 MiB."],
  ];

  for (const [, file, message] of cases) {
    await importInput(page).setInputFiles(file);
    await expect(alertLine(page)).toHaveText(message);
    await expect(cards(page)).toHaveCount(0);
    await expect(heading(page, 0)).toBeVisible();
    await expect(page.locator(".app-shell")).toHaveAttribute("data-authoring-locked", "false");
  }

  expect(await yamlSource(page)).toBe(before);
});

test("a 30 MiB WAV imports: the audio limit is 50 MiB, not the 25 MiB image limit", async ({page}) => {
  test.setTimeout(90_000);
  await page.goto("/", {waitUntil: "domcontentloaded"});
  await waitForOwner(page);

  const bytes = buildWav({dataBytes: 30 * MIB - 44});

  await importInput(page).setInputFiles(await tempFile("big.wav", bytes));
  await expect(statusLine(page)).toHaveText(imported("big"), {timeout: 60_000});
  await expect(cardFor(page, refOf(bytes)).locator("[data-audio-duration]")).toHaveText("2:44");
});

test("an M4A (AAC) is imported where the browser decodes AAC and refused as undecodable where it does not", async ({page}) => {
  await page.goto("/", {waitUntil: "domcontentloaded"});
  await waitForOwner(page);

  const aac = await page.evaluate(async () =>
    typeof AudioDecoder === "undefined"
      ? false
      : (await AudioDecoder.isConfigSupported({codec: "mp4a.40.2", sampleRate: 44_100, numberOfChannels: 2})).supported === true,
  );

  await importInput(page).setInputFiles(upload("tone-440-2.2s.m4a", fixtureBytes("tone-440-2.2s.m4a")));

  if (aac) {
    await expect(statusLine(page)).toHaveText(imported("tone-440-2.2s"));
    await expect(cards(page)).toHaveCount(1);
  } else {
    await expect(alertLine(page)).toHaveText("This audio file could not be decoded.");
    await expect(cards(page)).toHaveCount(0);
  }
});

test("rename changes the label only (Enter saves, Escape cancels) and never the Story", async ({page}) => {
  const record = await audioWavRecord("tone-440-2.2s.wav");

  await openSeeded(page, [record]);

  const before = await yamlSource(page);
  const card = cardFor(page, record.ref);

  await card.getByRole("button", {name: "Rename tone-440-2.2s"}).click();
  await card.getByLabel("New name for tone-440-2.2s").fill("  Narration  ");
  await card.getByLabel("New name for tone-440-2.2s").press("Enter");
  await expect(card).toContainText("Narration");
  await expect(card.locator("[data-audio-duration]")).toHaveText("0:02");

  await card.getByRole("button", {name: "Rename Narration"}).click();
  await card.getByLabel("New name for Narration").fill("Discarded");
  await card.getByLabel("New name for Narration").press("Escape");
  await expect(card).toContainText("Narration");
  await expect(card).not.toContainText("Discarded");
  expect(await yamlSource(page)).toBe(before);
});

test("delete counts the scenes using the ref in any audio role, music included; the missing card and Import matching file restore it", async ({page}) => {
  const music = await audioWavRecord("tone-220-10s.wav");
  const voice = await audioWavRecord("tone-440-2.2s.wav");
  const spare = await audioWavRecord("tone-880-0.4s.wav");

  await openSeeded(page, [music, voice, spare]);
  await applyYaml(
    page,
    audioStoryYaml({
      music: music.ref,
      scenes: [{sfx: voice.ref}, {voice: voice.ref, duration: "auto"}],
    }),
  );
  await page.getByRole("button", {name: "Open visual editor"}).click();
  await expect(heading(page, 3)).toBeVisible();
  await expect(missingCard(page, music.ref)).toHaveCount(0);

  // An unused file asks the plain question.
  await cardFor(page, spare.ref).getByRole("button", {name: "Delete tone-880-0.4s"}).click();
  await expect(page.getByRole("dialog")).toContainText('Delete "tone-880-0.4s" (audio) from My audio?');
  await page.getByRole("dialog").getByRole("button", {name: "Cancel"}).click();
  await expect(page.getByRole("dialog")).toHaveCount(0);

  // The music-only file.
  await cardFor(page, music.ref).getByRole("button", {name: "Delete tone-220-10s"}).click();
  await expect(page.getByRole("dialog")).toContainText("is used by the music in this Story.");
  await page.getByRole("dialog").getByRole("button", {name: "Cancel"}).click();

  // The file used by sfx in scene 1 and voice in scene 2.
  await cardFor(page, voice.ref).getByRole("button", {name: "Delete tone-440-2.2s"}).click();
  await expect(page.getByRole("dialog")).toContainText("is used by 2 scenes in this Story.");
  await page.getByRole("dialog").getByRole("button", {name: "Delete asset anyway"}).click();
  await expect(page.getByRole("dialog")).toHaveCount(0);
  await expect(heading(page, 2)).toBeVisible();
  await expect(cardFor(page, voice.ref)).toHaveCount(0);

  const missing = missingCard(page, voice.ref);

  await expect(missing).toBeVisible();
  await expect(missing).toContainText("Missing local audio");
  await expect(missing).toContainText("Used by scenes 1, 2.");
  await expect(missing).toContainText("Import the original file to restore it.");

  // A different audio file cannot restore it and writes nothing.
  await missing.locator('input[type="file"]').setInputFiles(upload("tone-880-0.4s.wav", fixtureBytes("tone-880-0.4s.wav")));
  await expect(alertLine(page)).toContainText("This file is a different audio file");
  await expect(missing).toBeVisible();
  await expect(heading(page, 2)).toBeVisible();

  // The exact file restores it and the Story is untouched.
  const yamlBefore = await yamlSource(page);

  await missing.locator('input[type="file"]').setInputFiles(upload("tone-440-2.2s.wav", fixtureBytes("tone-440-2.2s.wav")));
  await expect(statusLine(page)).toHaveText(imported("tone-440-2.2s"));
  await expect(missing).toHaveCount(0);
  await expect(cardFor(page, voice.ref)).toBeVisible();
  await expect(heading(page, 3)).toBeVisible();
  expect(await yamlSource(page)).toBe(yamlBefore);
});

test("a Story ref that is not in the library shows a missing card with its scenes, and a deleted row in another tab appears as missing", async ({page}) => {
  const voice = await audioWavRecord("tone-440-2.2s.wav");

  await openSeeded(page, [voice]);
  await applyYaml(page, audioStoryYaml({scenes: [{voice: voice.ref}]}));

  await expect(cardFor(page, voice.ref)).toBeVisible();
  await expect(missingCard(page, voice.ref)).toHaveCount(0);

  await deleteAssetRows(page, [voice.ref]);
  await page.reload({waitUntil: "domcontentloaded"});
  await waitForOwner(page);
  await expect(missingCard(page, voice.ref)).toBeVisible();
  await expect(missingCard(page, voice.ref)).toContainText("Used by scene 1.");
  await expect(heading(page, 0)).toBeVisible();
});

test("the Player length comes from the imported voice's duration: one auto scene of a 2.2 s voice is 81 frames, not the 90-frame fallback", async ({page}) => {
  test.setTimeout(60_000);

  const voice = await audioWavRecord("tone-440-2.2s.wav");

  await openSeeded(page, [voice]);
  await applyYaml(page, audioStoryYaml({scenes: [{voice: voice.ref, duration: "auto"}]}));
  await page.getByRole("button", {name: "Open visual editor"}).click();
  await expect(cardFor(page, voice.ref)).toBeVisible();
  // The control bar shows the Player length: 2.7 s ("0:02") from the imported voice, not the 3 s fallback ("0:03").
  await expect(page.locator(".preview-frame")).toContainText("0:00 / 0:02");

  // The Player returns to frame 0 when it ends, so record the highest frame it reaches.
  await page.evaluate(() => {
    const probe = document.querySelector("[data-tora-frame]");

    window.__maxFrame = 0;
    new MutationObserver(() => {
      window.__maxFrame = Math.max(window.__maxFrame, Number(probe.dataset.toraFrame));
    }).observe(probe, {attributes: true, attributeFilter: ["data-tora-frame"]});
  });

  const playButton = page.getByRole("button", {name: "Play video"});

  await expect(playButton).toBeVisible();
  await playButton.click();
  await expect
    .poll(() => page.evaluate(() => window.__maxFrame), {timeout: 15_000})
    .toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(80);
  await page.waitForTimeout(1500);
  // 81 frames: the last is frame 80. The 3 s fallback would have reached frame 89.
  expect(await page.evaluate(() => window.__maxFrame)).toBe(80);
});
```

`tests/browser/my-audio-m4a.spec.mjs` (system Chrome only, see the config edits below):

```js
import {createHash} from "node:crypto";
import {expect, test} from "@playwright/test";
import {fixtureBytes} from "../helpers/audioBytes.mjs";
import {localAssetRef} from "./helpers/seedAssetLibrary.mjs";
import {waitForOwner} from "./helpers/localAssetPage.mjs";

const section = (page) => page.locator("[data-my-audio]");
const refOf = (bytes) => localAssetRef("audio", createHash("sha256").update(bytes).digest("hex"));

// Bundled Chromium cannot decode AAC; system Chrome can, so M4A is verified here.
test("an M4A (AAC-LC) file imports with its duration and survives a reload", async ({page}) => {
  await page.goto("/", {waitUntil: "domcontentloaded"});
  await waitForOwner(page);

  for (const [name, label, duration] of [
    ["tone-440-2.2s.m4a", "tone-440-2.2s", "0:02"],
    ["tone-220-10s.m4a", "tone-220-10s", "0:10"],
  ]) {
    const bytes = fixtureBytes(name);

    await section(page)
      .locator('.my-assets-toolbar input[type="file"]')
      .setInputFiles({name, mimeType: "audio/mp4", buffer: Buffer.from(bytes)});
    await expect(section(page).getByRole("status")).toHaveText(`Imported "${label}".`);
    await expect(
      page.locator(`[data-local-asset-card="${refOf(bytes)}"] [data-audio-duration]`),
    ).toHaveText(duration);
  }

  await page.reload({waitUntil: "domcontentloaded"});
  await waitForOwner(page);
  await expect(section(page).getByRole("heading", {name: "My audio · 2"})).toBeVisible();
});
```

Config edits: in `playwright.config.mjs` add `"my-audio-m4a.spec.mjs"` to `testIgnore` (system Chrome needed); in `playwright.browser-render.config.mjs` add `"my-audio-m4a.spec.mjs"` to `testMatch`.

- [ ] **Step 8: Run to verify**

Run: `node --experimental-strip-types --test tests/audio-ui.test.mjs tests/local-asset-page.test.mjs` - Expected: PASS.
Run: `npm test`, `npm run lint`, `npm run web:build`, then `npx playwright test --project=chromium tests/browser/my-audio.spec.mjs` - Expected: PASS (the M4A test takes the "not decodable" branch in bundled Chromium), then `npx playwright test --project=chromium` (every v0.3 My assets spec unchanged) and `npx playwright test --config=playwright.browser-render.config.mjs my-audio-m4a.spec.mjs` - Expected: PASS in system Chrome.
