---

### Task 11: CLI audio assets (`local-assets/audio/`, staging, `npm run assets`)

The CLI finds the same files: `categoryFolder.audio = "audio"`, candidates are classified by `inspectAudio` (metadata only: Node has no WebCodecs), matched by digest, symlinks skipped, bytes snapshotted on match, staged to `__local-assets/audio/<digest>.<ext>`. The size check uses `stats.size` before anything is read. `npm run assets` lists audio with its duration, only when the `audio/` folder exists, so every v0.3 output line stays identical. `renderStory` keeps refusing Stories with audio (AUDIO-005 removes the block and calls `stageLocalAssetsForStory`).

**Files:**
- Modify: `scripts/localAssets.ts`, `scripts/assets.ts`, `local-assets/README.md`
- Modify tests: `tests/cli-local-assets.test.mjs` (append)

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `inspectAudio`, `AudioMetadata`, `audioFileExtensionFor`, `AUDIO_EMPTY_MESSAGE`, `AUDIO_TOO_LARGE_MESSAGE` (Task 2), `checkLocalAssetByteSize(size, category)` (Task 3), `formatAudioDuration` (Task 10), `LocalAssetUsage`/`LocalAudioUsage` (Task 7), `buildWav`, `fixtureBytes` (Task 1).
- Produces (`scripts/localAssets.ts`):
  - `categoryFolder = {pose: "poses", background: "backgrounds", audio: "audio"}`.
  - `type ImageCandidate = {relativePath: string; ok: true; ref: LocalImageRef; digest: string; image: InspectedImage; bytes: Uint8Array}`, `type AudioCandidate = {relativePath: string; ok: true; ref: LocalAudioRef; digest: string; audio: AudioMetadata; bytes: Uint8Array}`, `type RejectedCandidate = {relativePath: string; ok: false; reason: ImageRejection | "changed-during-scan" | "unreadable"} | {relativePath: string; ok: false; reason: "audio-rejected"; message: string}`, `type CandidateResult = ImageCandidate | AudioCandidate | RejectedCandidate` (the image shapes are exactly the v0.3 ones).
  - `scanCategory(root, category: LocalAssetCategory, deps?)`, `categoryFolderExists(root, category: LocalAssetCategory, deps?)`.
  - `type AudioInventoryLine = {relativePath: string; ref: LocalAudioRef; durationSeconds: number} | {relativePath: string; rejected: string}`, `inventoryAudioAssets(root: string, deps?: ScanDeps): Promise<{folderExists: boolean; lines: AudioInventoryLine[]}>`. `inventoryLocalAssets` is unchanged (poses and backgrounds only).
  - `stageLocalAssetsForStory` stages `audio` usages the same way (staged path `__local-assets/audio/<digest>.<mp3|wav|m4a|ogg>`; `sources[ref] = {kind: "static", path}`).

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing tests**

Append to `tests/cli-local-assets.test.mjs` (add to its imports: `inventoryAudioAssets` from `../scripts/localAssets.ts`; `AUDIO_EMPTY_MESSAGE, AUDIO_TOO_LARGE_MESSAGE, AUDIO_UNSUPPORTED_MESSAGE` from `../src/localAssets/audioInspection.ts`; `AUDIO_RENDER_BLOCK_MESSAGE` from `../src/audio/storyAudio.ts`; `buildWav, fixtureBytes` from `./helpers/audioBytes.mjs`; `MissingLocalAssetsError` and `renderStory` are already imported):

```js
const MIB = 1024 * 1024;
const audioRefOf = (bytes) => `local:audio:sha256:${nodeSha(bytes)}`;
const audioStoryOf = ({music, voices = [], sfx = []}) => ({
  title: "Audio",
  ...(music === undefined ? {} : {music: {src: music}}),
  scenes: [
    ...voices.map((src) => ({...sceneOf("formal", "office"), voice: {src}})),
    ...sfx.map((src) => ({...sceneOf("formal", "office"), sfx: {src}})),
  ],
});

describe("local-assets/audio: scanning", () => {
  it("classifies by content: matches by digest whatever the name, rejects wrong content, skips nothing silently", async () => {
    const root = await makeRoot();
    const voice = fixtureBytes("tone-440-2.2s.mp3");
    const music = fixtureBytes("tone-220-10s.wav");
    const ding = fixtureBytes("tone-880-0.4s.opus.ogg");

    await put(root, "audio/voice.mp3", voice);
    await put(root, "audio/renamed.bin", voice);
    await put(root, "audio/sub/music.wav", music);
    await put(root, "audio/sub/ding.ogg", ding);
    await put(root, "audio/fake.mp3", buildTextBytes());
    await put(root, "audio/picture.png", await fixture("pose-magenta.png"));

    const results = await collect(scanCategory(root, "audio"));
    const byPath = Object.fromEntries(results.map((result) => [result.relativePath, result]));

    assert.deepEqual(results.map((result) => result.relativePath), ["fake.mp3", "picture.png", "renamed.bin", "sub/ding.ogg", "sub/music.wav", "voice.mp3"]);
    assert.deepEqual(
      [byPath["voice.mp3"].ref, byPath["renamed.bin"].ref, byPath["sub/music.wav"].ref, byPath["sub/ding.ogg"].ref],
      [audioRefOf(voice), audioRefOf(voice), audioRefOf(music), audioRefOf(ding)],
    );
    assert.deepEqual(
      [byPath["voice.mp3"].audio.container, byPath["sub/music.wav"].audio.container, byPath["sub/ding.ogg"].audio.container],
      ["mp3", "wave", "ogg"],
    );
    assert.equal(Math.abs(byPath["sub/music.wav"].audio.durationSeconds - 10) < 0.001, true);
    assert.deepEqual(
      [byPath["fake.mp3"], byPath["picture.png"]].map(({ok, reason, message}) => ({ok, reason, message})),
      [
        {ok: false, reason: "audio-rejected", message: AUDIO_UNSUPPORTED_MESSAGE},
        {ok: false, reason: "audio-rejected", message: AUDIO_UNSUPPORTED_MESSAGE},
      ],
    );
  });

  it("skips symlinked files and directories but resolves regular siblings", async (context) => {
    const root = await makeRoot();
    const outside = await makeRoot();

    await put(root, "audio/real.wav", fixtureBytes("tone-880-0.4s.wav"));
    await put(outside, "target.wav", fixtureBytes("tone-440-2.2s.wav"));
    await put(outside, "dir/inner.wav", fixtureBytes("tone-440-2.2s.wav"));

    try {
      await symlink(join(outside, "target.wav"), join(root, "audio", "link.wav"), "file");
      await symlink(join(outside, "dir"), join(root, "audio", "linkdir"), "dir");
    } catch (error) {
      if (error.code === "EPERM" || error.code === "EACCES") {
        context.skip("symlinks are not permitted here");

        return;
      }

      throw error;
    }

    const {opened, deps} = spyOpen();
    const results = await collect(scanCategory(root, "audio", deps));

    assert.deepEqual(results.map((result) => result.relativePath), ["real.wav"]);
    assert.equal(opened.length, 1);
  });

  it("uses the audio limit before reading: a 30 MiB WAV is read, a 51 MiB file is never opened, and the image limit is unchanged", async () => {
    const root = await makeRoot();
    const big = buildWav({dataBytes: 30 * MIB - 44});

    await put(root, "audio/big.wav", big);
    await put(root, "audio/huge.wav", buildWav({dataBytes: 51 * MIB - 44}));
    await put(root, "audio/empty.wav", new Uint8Array(0));
    await put(root, "poses/big.png", new Uint8Array(26 * MIB));

    const {opened, deps} = spyOpen();
    const audio = await collect(scanCategory(root, "audio", deps));
    const byPath = Object.fromEntries(audio.map((result) => [result.relativePath, result]));

    assert.equal(byPath["big.wav"].ok, true);
    assert.equal(byPath["big.wav"].ref, audioRefOf(big));
    assert.equal(Math.abs(byPath["big.wav"].audio.durationSeconds - 163.84) < 0.01, true);
    assert.equal(byPath["huge.wav"].message, AUDIO_TOO_LARGE_MESSAGE);
    assert.equal(byPath["empty.wav"].message, AUDIO_EMPTY_MESSAGE);
    assert.deepEqual(opened.map((path) => path.replaceAll("\\", "/").split("/").at(-1)), ["big.wav"]);

    const poseSpy = spyOpen();
    const poses = await collect(scanCategory(root, "pose", poseSpy.deps));

    assert.deepEqual(poses.map(({ok, reason}) => ({ok, reason})), [{ok: false, reason: "too-large"}]);
    assert.deepEqual(poseSpy.opened, []);
  });
});

describe("local-assets/audio: staging", () => {
  it("stages originals under __local-assets/audio/<digest>.<ext> and leaves the repository public/ alone", async () => {
    const root = await makeRoot();
    const voice = fixtureBytes("tone-440-2.2s.mp3");
    const music = fixtureBytes("tone-220-10s.wav");
    const ding = fixtureBytes("tone-880-0.4s.opus.ogg");
    const m4a = fixtureBytes("tone-880-0.4s.m4a");

    await put(root, "audio/a-voice.mp3", voice);
    await put(root, "audio/b-music.wav", music);
    await put(root, "audio/c-ding.ogg", ding);
    await put(root, "audio/d-voice.m4a", m4a);

    const story = audioStoryOf({music: audioRefOf(music), voices: [audioRefOf(voice), audioRefOf(m4a)], sfx: [audioRefOf(ding)]});
    const {publicDir, staged} = await stageInto(story, root);
    const expected = [
      [audioRefOf(music), music, `__local-assets/audio/${nodeSha(music)}.wav`],
      [audioRefOf(voice), voice, `__local-assets/audio/${nodeSha(voice)}.mp3`],
      [audioRefOf(m4a), m4a, `__local-assets/audio/${nodeSha(m4a)}.m4a`],
      [audioRefOf(ding), ding, `__local-assets/audio/${nodeSha(ding)}.ogg`],
    ];

    assert.deepEqual(staged.sources, Object.fromEntries(expected.map(([ref, , path]) => [ref, {kind: "static", path}])));

    for (const [, original, path] of expected) {
      assert.deepEqual(Buffer.from(await readFile(join(publicDir, ...path.split("/")))), Buffer.from(original));
    }

    await assert.rejects(stat(join(repoRoot, "public", "__local-assets")), {code: "ENOENT"});
  });

  it("stages images and audio of one Story independently, each category from its own folder", async () => {
    const root = await makeRoot();
    const pose = await fixture("pose-magenta.png");
    const voice = fixtureBytes("tone-440-2.2s.wav");

    await put(root, "poses/pose.png", pose);
    await put(root, "audio/voice.wav", voice);

    const story = {...audioStoryOf({voices: [audioRefOf(voice)]})};

    story.scenes[0].pose = poseRefOf(pose);

    const {publicDir, staged} = await stageInto(story, root);

    assert.deepEqual(Object.keys(staged.sources).sort(), [audioRefOf(voice), poseRefOf(pose)].sort());
    assert.equal((await readFile(join(publicDir, "__local-assets", "audio", `${nodeSha(voice)}.wav`))).length, voice.length);
  });

  it("snapshots bytes: editing the source after a match cannot change the staged file", async () => {
    const root = await makeRoot();
    const voice = fixtureBytes("tone-440-2.2s.wav");
    const source = join(root, "audio", "voice.wav");

    await put(root, "audio/voice.wav", voice);

    const publicDir = await makeRoot();

    await stageLocalAssetsForStory(audioStoryOf({voices: [audioRefOf(voice)]}), {
      root,
      publicDir,
      onStaged: async () => {
        await writeFile(source, new Uint8Array([1, 2, 3]));
      },
    });

    assert.deepEqual(Buffer.from(await readFile(join(publicDir, "__local-assets", "audio", `${nodeSha(voice)}.wav`))), Buffer.from(voice));
  });

  it("stages a 30 MiB WAV; a 51 MiB file is refused and its bytes are never opened, hashed or staged", async () => {
    const root = await makeRoot();
    const big = buildWav({dataBytes: 30 * MIB - 44});
    const huge = buildWav({dataBytes: 51 * MIB - 44});

    await put(root, "audio/a-big.wav", big);
    await put(root, "audio/b-huge.wav", huge);

    const {opened, deps} = spyOpen();
    const {publicDir, staged} = await stageInto(audioStoryOf({voices: [audioRefOf(big)]}), root, {}, deps);

    assert.equal((await stat(join(publicDir, "__local-assets", "audio", `${nodeSha(big)}.wav`))).size, 30 * MIB);
    assert.deepEqual(Object.keys(staged.sources), [audioRefOf(big)]);

    const hugeSpy = spyOpen();

    await assert.rejects(
      stageInto(audioStoryOf({voices: [audioRefOf(huge)]}), root, {}, hugeSpy.deps),
      (error) => error instanceof MissingLocalAssetsError,
    );
    assert.deepEqual(hugeSpy.opened.map((path) => path.replaceAll("\\", "/").split("/").at(-1)), ["a-big.wav"]);
  });

  it("an audio file in poses/ or an image in audio/ never satisfies an audio ref", async () => {
    const root = await makeRoot();
    const voice = fixtureBytes("tone-440-2.2s.wav");

    await put(root, "poses/voice.wav", voice);
    await put(root, "audio/pose.png", await fixture("pose-magenta.png"));

    await assert.rejects(stageInto(audioStoryOf({voices: [audioRefOf(voice)]}), root), (error) => error instanceof MissingLocalAssetsError);
  });

  it("a missing audio ref is reported with its roles, the audio folder and the audio hint; the image text is unchanged", async () => {
    const root = join(await makeRoot(), "assets");
    const musicRef = `local:audio:sha256:${"b".repeat(64)}`;
    const voiceRef = `local:audio:sha256:${"c".repeat(64)}`;
    const story = {...audioStoryOf({music: musicRef, voices: [voiceRef]})};

    await assert.rejects(stageInto(story, root), (error) => {
      assert.ok(error instanceof MissingLocalAssetsError);
      assert.equal(
        error.message,
        [
          "Cannot render Story: 2 local assets are missing.",
          "",
          "Audio:",
          `  ${musicRef}`,
          "  used by: the music",
          `  searched: ${displayRoot(root)}/audio/`,
          "",
          `  ${voiceRef}`,
          "  used by scenes: 1",
          `  searched: ${displayRoot(root)}/audio/`,
          "",
          "Copy the original audio file (MP3, WAV, M4A or Ogg Opus) into that folder (any filename),",
          "or replace the reference in the Story. Run `npm run assets` to list the refs",
          "of the files that are there.",
        ].join("\n"),
      );

      return true;
    });
  });
});

describe("npm run assets with audio", () => {
  const runAssets = (root) => {
    const env = {...process.env, TORA_LOCAL_ASSETS_ROOT: root};

    return spawnSync(process.execPath, ["--experimental-strip-types", join(repoRoot, "scripts", "assets.ts")], {
      cwd: repoRoot,
      env,
      encoding: "utf8",
    });
  };

  it("lists audio refs with duration after the image sections, and skipped files with their reason", async () => {
    const root = await makeRoot();
    const voice = fixtureBytes("tone-440-2.2s.wav");
    const long = buildWav({dataBytes: 30 * MIB - 44});

    await put(root, "audio/voice.wav", voice);
    await put(root, "audio/fake.mp3", buildTextBytes());
    await put(root, "audio/z-long.wav", long);

    const result = runAssets(root);
    const base = root.replaceAll("\\", "/");

    assert.equal(result.status, 0, result.stderr);
    assert.equal(
      result.stdout,
      [
        `${base}/poses/ (folder not found — create it and copy images into it)`,
        "",
        `${base}/backgrounds/ (folder not found — create it and copy images into it)`,
        "",
        `${base}/audio/fake.mp3`,
        `  skipped: ${AUDIO_UNSUPPORTED_MESSAGE}`,
        "",
        `${base}/audio/voice.wav`,
        `  ${audioRefOf(voice)}`,
        "  duration: 0:02 (2.20 s)",
        "",
        `${base}/audio/z-long.wav`,
        `  ${audioRefOf(long)}`,
        "  duration: 2:44 (163.84 s)",
        "",
      ].join("\n"),
    );
  });

  it("says when an existing audio folder has no files, and prints nothing about audio when there is no folder", async () => {
    const withFolder = await makeRoot();
    const without = await makeRoot();

    await mkdir(join(withFolder, "audio"));
    await mkdir(join(without, "poses"));

    const base = withFolder.replaceAll("\\", "/");

    assert.equal(
      runAssets(withFolder).stdout,
      [
        `${base}/poses/ (folder not found — create it and copy images into it)`,
        "",
        `${base}/backgrounds/ (folder not found — create it and copy images into it)`,
        "",
        `${base}/audio/ (no files)`,
        "",
      ].join("\n"),
    );
    assert.equal(runAssets(without).stdout.includes("audio"), false);
  });

  it("inventoryAudioAssets reports whether the folder exists", async () => {
    const root = await makeRoot();

    assert.deepEqual(await inventoryAudioAssets(root), {folderExists: false, lines: []});
  });
});
```

2. Constants and types:

```ts
export const categoryFolder = {
  pose: "poses",
  background: "backgrounds",
  audio: "audio",
} as const;

export type ImageCandidate = {
  relativePath: string;
  ok: true;
  ref: LocalImageRef;
  digest: string;
  image: InspectedImage;
  bytes: Uint8Array;
};

export type AudioCandidate = {
  relativePath: string;
  ok: true;
  ref: LocalAudioRef;
  digest: string;
  audio: AudioMetadata;
  bytes: Uint8Array;
};

export type RejectedCandidate =
  | {
      relativePath: string;
      ok: false;
      reason: ImageRejection | "changed-during-scan" | "unreadable";
    }
  | {relativePath: string; ok: false; reason: "audio-rejected"; message: string};

export type CandidateResult =
  | ImageCandidate
  | AudioCandidate
  | RejectedCandidate;
```

(the old `CandidateResult` type is deleted).
3. `categoryDirectory(root, category: LocalAssetCategory)`, `categoryFolderExists(root, category: LocalAssetCategory, deps)`, `scanCategory(root, category: LocalAssetCategory, deps)`.
4. Replace `inspectEntry` with:

```ts
const inspectEntry = async (
  directory: string,
  entry: Entry,
  category: LocalAssetCategory,
  deps: ScanDeps,
): Promise<CandidateResult> => {
  const {relativePath, stats} = entry;
  // The size is bounded by the category's own limit before anything is read or hashed.
  const size = checkLocalAssetByteSize(stats.size, category);

  if (!size.ok) {
    return category === "audio"
      ? {
          relativePath,
          ok: false,
          reason: "audio-rejected",
          message:
            size.reason === "empty" ? AUDIO_EMPTY_MESSAGE : AUDIO_TOO_LARGE_MESSAGE,
        }
      : {relativePath, ok: false, reason: size.reason};
  }

  const openImpl = deps.open ?? open;
  const absolute = join(directory, ...relativePath.split("/"));
  let bytes: Uint8Array;

  try {
    const handle = await openImpl(absolute, "r");

    try {
      const opened = await handle.stat();

      if (!opened.isFile() || !sameIdentity(stats, opened)) {
        return {relativePath, ok: false, reason: "changed-during-scan"};
      }

      bytes = await handle.readFile();
    } finally {
      await handle.close();
    }
  } catch {
    return {relativePath, ok: false, reason: "unreadable"};
  }

  if (category === "audio") {
    // Metadata only: Node has no WebCodecs, the render preflight decodes (AUDIO-005).
    const inspection = await inspectAudio(bytes);

    if (!inspection.ok) {
      return {
        relativePath,
        ok: false,
        reason: "audio-rejected",
        message: inspection.message,
      };
    }

    const digest = await sha256Hex(bytes);

    return {
      relativePath,
      ok: true,
      ref: buildLocalAssetRef("audio", digest),
      digest,
      audio: inspection.metadata,
      bytes,
    };
  }

  const inspection = inspectImageBytes(bytes);

  if (!inspection.ok) {
    return {relativePath, ok: false, reason: inspection.reason};
  }

  const digest = await sha256Hex(bytes);

  return {
    relativePath,
    ok: true,
    ref: buildLocalAssetRef(category, digest),
    digest,
    image: inspection.image,
    bytes,
  };
};
```

5. `describeScanFailure` takes the whole rejected result:

```ts
const describeScanFailure = (result: RejectedCandidate): string => {
  if (result.reason === "audio-rejected") {
    return result.message;
  }

  switch (result.reason) {
    case "changed-during-scan":
      return "The file changed while it was being scanned.";
    case "unreadable":
      return "The file could not be read.";
    default:
      return describeImageRejection(result.reason);
  }
};
```

and `inventoryCategory` (image categories only):

```ts

      result.ok && "image" in result
        ? {relativePath: result.relativePath, ref: result.ref}
        : {
            relativePath: result.relativePath,
            rejected: result.ok ? "" : describeScanFailure(result),
          },
    );
  }
```

Add below `inventoryLocalAssets`:

```ts
export type AudioInventoryLine =
  | {relativePath: string; ref: LocalAudioRef; durationSeconds: number}
  | {relativePath: string; rejected: string};

/** The audio folder of `npm run assets`: separate from inventoryLocalAssets so its result shape stays as in v0.3. */
export const inventoryAudioAssets = async (
  root: string,
  deps: ScanDeps = {},
): Promise<{folderExists: boolean; lines: AudioInventoryLine[]}> => {
  if (!(await categoryFolderExists(root, "audio", deps))) {
    return {folderExists: false, lines: []};
  }

  const lines: AudioInventoryLine[] = [];

  for await (const result of scanCategory(root, "audio", deps)) {
    lines.push(
      result.ok && "audio" in result
        ? {
            relativePath: result.relativePath,
            ref: result.ref,
            durationSeconds: result.audio.durationSeconds,
          }
        : {
            relativePath: result.relativePath,
            rejected: result.ok ? "" : describeScanFailure(result),
          },
    );
  }

  return {folderExists: true, lines};
};
```

  const scenes = usage.sceneIndexes.map((scene) => scene + 1).join(", ");

    return `  used by scenes: ${scenes}`;

  const music = usage.roles.includes("music");

};

and the formatter:

```ts
const formatAudioLines = (root: string, lines: AudioInventoryLine[]): string[] => {
  const output: string[] = [];

  for (const line of lines) {
    output.push(
      `${displayRoot(root)}/${categoryFolder.audio}/${line.relativePath}`,
      ...("ref" in line
        ? [
            `  ${line.ref}`,
            `  duration: ${formatAudioDuration(line.durationSeconds)} (${line.durationSeconds.toFixed(2)} s)`,
          ]
        : [`  skipped: ${line.rejected}`]),
      "",
    );
  }

  return output;
};
```

In the first "Decisions specific to this spec" bullet, after "...and the ruling is recorded here.", append: ` **Ruling: Vorbis is rejected.** The repository ffmpeg has no Vorbis encoder (the native one does not exist in this build: \`-c:a vorbis\` fails with "Unknown encoder"), so no Ogg Vorbis fixture can be produced and the Chrome decode test cannot run; \`inspectAudio\` answers an Ogg Vorbis file with the message above.`

Replace the sentence "Today's handler loops over all store names and creates each one; with a version 1 database present that would throw on the existing stores, so it must create a store only when `!database.objectStoreNames.contains(name)`." with "The handler creates a store only when `!database.objectStoreNames.contains(name)` (as since v0.3), so a version 1 database gets exactly `waveforms`."
5. Append a section at the end:

```markdown
## Implementation notes

- Duration authority is mediabunny's `getDurationFromMetadata()`, falling back to `computeDuration()`; a truncated MP3 with a Xing header therefore keeps its declared length (10.031 s for the 10 s fixture) while a scan would report 5.98 s, which is what lets the truncation rule catch it.
- Image `payloadMeta` records keep the v0.3 shape (no `kind` field); `kind: "audio"` marks audio, a stored `kind: "image"` is tolerated and dropped on read.
- The library read API takes the store first: `readAudioMetadata(store, ref)`, `readWaveform(store, digest)`, `readAudioDurations(store, refs)`. Peaks are laid over the container duration by timestamp.
- Import: one read of the file, then inspect, decode (peaks and truncation rule), hash, commit. M4A needs a browser that decodes AAC (system Chrome); elsewhere it is refused with `This audio file could not be decoded.`
- "Import matching file" with a different audio file is refused with the mismatch text and writes nothing; the replacement dialog arrives with AUDIO-004's scene-field pickers.
- `npm run assets` prints the audio section only when `local-assets/audio/` exists. "Staged by `renderStory`" is tested through `stageLocalAssetsForStory`, because the AUDIO-001 interim render block refuses Stories with audio until AUDIO-005.
- Fixtures: the repository ffmpeg has no `anoisesrc`, filters or Vorbis encoder; the noise fixture is a seeded sum of sine partials below 2 kHz (`scripts/generateAudioFixtures.mjs`).
```
